      • Regongar
      • Tristian
    • 情景触发
      • 剧情
      • 状态
Amiri：好想打架啊，真想狠狠揍人一顿，我的双拳都感到饥渴难耐了
Ugh, I miss a good fight. I want to hit somebody so hard, my fists are itching.
Amiri：要是有吃的就好了……再来点酒！等我到了酒馆，我要给自己关在里面，把酒喝光了再出来！
Some food would be nice... And some booze! When I get to a tavern, I'll lock myself inside and won't come out until all the barrels are empty!
Amiri：嘿，我的披风都破破烂烂的了……得去抓头熊，只有一张大熊皮才配得上我！
Heh, my cape is all worn out... Got to catch a bear. A big one's hide would fit me just right!
Amiri：人们都说女孩不用太聪明，说得没错！
Everyone says brains aren't important for a lass. And they're right!
Amiri：女人需要的是二头肌！给这帮人脸上狠狠来一拳，所有人都会放尊重点了，没错。
A woman needs a bicep, that's what! A whack in the face will make anyone respect you – that's right.
Amiri：看看咱们这伙人……我就纳闷，我们是怎么活到现在的？
I'm looking at our gang... And I'm wondering. How the hell are we still alive yet?
Amiri：大家都那么机灵，这让我很难受。都在写厉害的书、念着花里胡哨的祷词……而我只会靠拳头来解决问题，看来这也是为什么我们能活到现在，哈哈。
Everyone's so smart, it makes me sick. Writing smart books, fancy prayers to the gods... I only punch first and then sort it out. I guess that's why we're still alive, haha.
Amiri：母狼的獠牙和公狼一样锐利，打猎也同样出色。母熊战斗起来像恶魔一样凶狠。那为什么我的部落不让女人从烧水煮饭这种事里解脱出来呢？！
Female wolves are as sharp-toothed as the males, and they hunt just as good. Female bears fight like daemons. So why my tribe won't let women lift their heads from the cooking pot?!
Ekun：人并不总是理智，他们会为了私利行同野兽、立愚蠢的规矩，而不是去学习动物都懂的道理。
People are not always wise. They act like beasts when it suits them. Or invent foolish things rather than learn what animals know.
Amiri：你是怎么做到不在森林里迷路的？这些树看起来都一个样！
How do you find your way in the forest, huh? All the trees look the same to me!
Ekun：人也都长着两只手两条腿，但你可以分辨出不同人。
People are the same as well. We have two hands, two legs. But you can tell them apart when you meet them.
Amiri：我恨巨魔！想到他们对艾昆的村子做了什么，我就恨不得把他们都嚼碎！
I hate trolls! After what they did to Ekun's village, I could tear them apart with my teeth!
Ekun：托拉格保佑你，安米芮，你要明白复仇不是凭空来的。
Torag bless you, Amiri. You know how revenge is forged.
Amiri：啊，饿死我了。我现在能吃下一整头猛犸象，连象鼻子都吃掉。
Oh, I am so hungry. I could eat a whole mammoth, trunk and all.
Ekun：保证不了猛犸象，可以给你打头鹿，够吃吗？
Can't promise a mammoth. Might be able to shoot a deer, though. Would a deer do?
Amiri：艾昆，你的狼为啥盯着我看？我又不是一块肉！
Ekun, why is your wolf staring at me? I'm not a chunk of meat!
Ekun：他喜欢你，他觉得你们是同类。
He likes you. You have lot in common.
Amiri：你打架也挺厉害的，虽然弓箭可比不上刀剑，不过……嗯，你用得还不错！
You fight pretty good! A bow is no match for a sword, but... Well, you make do all right!
Ekun：就连你的夸奖听起来都像要约架，不过我懂，谢谢。
Even your praise sounds like a challenge to fight. But I understand you. Thank you.
Amiri：你的老家那边有山吗？你看着像高地人，强壮又坚韧……跟住在平原的那帮软蛋完全不一样。
Are there mountains where you're from? You seem like a highlander – strong, tough... Not like wusses from the plains!
Ekun：确实有山，但山里不住人，都是牛头人、鹰身女妖……很危险，我住城里。
There are mountains, yes, but people don't live there. Minotaurs, harpies – dangerous places. I'm from the city.
Amiri：我在营地附近发现了一些脚印，肯定有埋伏！
I saw tracks near the camp! I'm telling you it's an ambush!
Ekun：没有埋伏，我能感觉到，只是一只黄鼠狼，而且很肥。
No ambush. I'd feel it. Just a badger. A fat one.
Amiri：你还挺不赖！我们来单挑吧！看看谁是这支队伍里最强的战士，怎么样？
You're not bad at all! Let's fight one on one! Find out who is the best fighter on this team, huh?
Ekun：不用单挑，你最强。
No need to fight. You are the best.
Amiri：我喜欢你的狼！我们山里人都很喜欢这种动物，以前邻近的部落养了只特别大的狼……呃！大到可以活活吞下一个人！连同马一起！
I like your wolf! In the mountains we like them too. A tribe near us used to breed such giant ones – ugh! They'd swallow a man alive! Horse and all!
Ekun：是不是还能吞下一整只猛犸象？
Maybe a whole mammoth too?
Amiri：嘿，矮人。你有没有想念过你的家乡和族人？听你之前讲的事，感觉你的族人比我的族人还糟糕……
Hey, dwarf. You ever miss your home and your kin? Sounds from what you've said, your kin is even more crap than mine…
Harrim：你为什么要明知故问？
Why ask, if you know the answer?
Amiri：我说，哈瑞姆，我给你的脑袋来上一拳怎么样？这样你就不会再说什么最后的终结之类的胡话了！
Listen, Harrim, how about I give you a nice whack on the dome? All that bull about the end of the end will come flying right out of you!
Harrim：如果你想杀了我，那就动手吧。反正人终有一死，死在现在，还是一个小时以后，或是一年以后，又有什么关系呢？别误会，只要我还活着，就会一直宣讲教义。
If you want to kill me – do it. What does it matter when we die – now, or an hour from now, or a year from now? Don't take it wrong, but as long as I live, I will preach.
Amiri：嘿，有没有人试过把哈瑞姆灌醉？他喝多了后还会这么无聊吗？
Hey, has anyone tried to get Harrim dead drunk? Is he still so boring when he's loaded?
Harrim：哦，安米芮啊安米芮……我曾终日酩酊大醉，不过那是在我信仰格罗图斯之前的事了……我不愿再回到那段灰黯的岁月。
Oh, Amiri, Amiri... There were times when I was never sober. This was long ago, before I came to Groetus... And I do not wish to return to those dark days.
Amiri：我问你啊，哈瑞姆，你的这位破锣图斯，他长什么样？是不是一头黏糊糊的恶心怪物，蹲在山洞里像蛇一样扭来扭去，等着我们都来送死？
Listen, Harrim, this Broetus of yours, what's he like? Some disgusting slimy monster, sitting in a cave waiting for us all to die, writhing around like a snake?
Harrim：正好相反，安米芮。格罗图斯是一轮皎洁的明月，照耀着法拉丝玛的坟场……并非世俗意义上的美，他的模样确实像一个骷髅头。
It's the opposite, Amiri. Groetus is the beautiful moon shining over Pharasma's Boneyard... Well, not what is normally called beautiful... He does look like a skull.
Amiri：哈瑞姆，你这人挺奇怪的，明明其他的矮人都很有趣。他们都喜欢喝酒、玩闹和大声嚷嚷，只有你一直在自言自语。
You're strange, Harrim. Dwarves are usually fun. They like the bottle, they like to play and shout. But you just mumble all the time.
Harrim：一个动辄好斗、还不相信死亡无可避免的野蛮人，居然说我奇怪？这可真好笑。
I am called strange by a barbarian who gets into every fight she can, and doesn't believe in the inevitability of death? This is truly amazing.
Amiri：哈瑞姆，你为什么加入我们的队伍？反正你都已经在等待终结了，还在乎自己在哪吗？
Harrim, why on earth do you tag along with us, huh? Why do you care where you sit as you wait for your big ending?
Harrim：有时我也疑惑，格罗图斯的低语将我带到此地，而这里也不比其它地方差。
Sometimes I wonder. But I am where Groetus' whisper has brought me, and this place is no worse than any other.
Amiri：喂，你这僵尸，能不能死一边去？看见你就烦。
Listen, stiff, how about you go rot someplace else. I'm sick of even looking at you.
Jaethal：你是不是看到我就想起自己活不长了？还是说你嫉妒我能永生？反正我也不在乎你怎么想。
Do I remind you of your own imminent death? Or perhaps you are jealous that I have eternity ahead of me? In any case, I couldn't care less about your opinion.
Amiri：你为什么不留在老家？你曾经是一名审判官，受人尊敬！
Why couldn't you just stay at home? You were a judge, you had respect!
Jaethal：尊敬本身并没有什么价值，关键在于它是否能帮你得到想要的东西，而我想要的远非尊敬所能换取的。
Respect has very little intrinsic value. The question is whether it gets you what you want. And I wanted far more than respect could fetch me.
Amiri：你故意把自己变成了不死生物……天啊，真恶心！
You made yourself undead on purpose… Blech, that's disgusting!
Jaethal：你大可以评价些你懂的事，比如啤酒口味，对不懂的事就闭嘴。
Judge what you know about – the taste of beer, for example. But keep quiet about things you have no idea about.
Amiri：离我远点，亡魂！我一下子就能掰断你的胳膊！
Stay away from me, you spook! I'll tear off your hands before you can blink!
Jaethal：你太高看自己了，我对没开化的蠢货没兴趣。
You flatter yourself. I've no interest in primitive boneheads.
Amiri：你要是多举举比叶子重的东西，可能就不会这么弱了。
Maybe you wouldn't be so weak if you ever tried to lift something heavier than a leaf.
Jubilost：所以我们才需要你，负责干重活，杀掉那些大怪物。而我负责发挥我无比的智慧和完美的才华。
That's why you are here, to do the heavy lifting, and to kill the big monsters. I'm here because of my supreme intellect and impeccable talents.
Amiri：朱比罗斯特，昨天你埋头翻书的时候，我听到你在嘀咕我。
Listen, Jubilost, I heard you mumble something about me yesterday, while you were digging in your books.
Jubilost：是的，我只是在学习一些关于巨魔、巨人以及其他食人魔的知识，我想你对这些非常了解。
Yes, yes, I was just updating my knowledge on trolls, giants and other ogres. I think you've much the same knowledge.
Amiri：朱比罗斯特，你有没有去过猛犸象大王之国？
Jubilost, have you ever been to the Realm of the Mammoth Lords?
Jubilost：当然去过！我都写过两本关于那个地方的书了。一本生存指南，一本你们的蛮荒歌谣集，还有一篇讲如何烹饪猛犸象鼻的文章！
Of course I have! I've written two books about it. A survival guide, and a book of your savage songs. – And an article about cooking a mammoth's trunk!
Amiri：你在开玩笑吗，小家伙？我可比你聪明十倍！
Are you kidding me, pipsqueak? Just so you know, I'm ten times smarter than you!
Jubilost：十倍？你居然能数到十？真是个天大的发现了！你还真比看上去要聪明些。
Ten times? Oh, so you can count to ten? That's an amazing discovery! You really are smarter than you seem.
Amiri：你给我起的啥名字？丸？没？你是不是以为用一些奇怪的词，我就不知道你是在取笑我，嗯？
What did you call me? Perv... Ferv... You think if you use funny words, I won't understand you are making fun of me, huh?!
Jubilost：首先，‘完美’这个词并不是贬义，其次，这个词肯定不是用来形容你的。如果我想骂你，那我肯定会用更简单的词，这样你才能听懂。
First, the word 'perfectionism' is not derogatory, and second, the word certainly doesn't apply to you. If I wanted to insult you, I'll choose nice short words so you could understand.
Amiri：你就会窝里横，一开战却躲在我背后！
You keep making jokes, but it's my back that you hide behind in battle!
Jubilost：我不是躲，我那是提供战术辅助。要是你不懂，下次战斗我干脆袖手旁观。我敢保证，你马上就能体会到缺了我的后果！
I'm not hiding, I'm providing tactical support. If you don't understand the difference, maybe I should just sit still during our next battle. I assure you, you'll feel my absence very quickly!
Kalikke/Kanerah：再告诉你件事吧！我一般就瞄着你大喊大叫的地方打，靠这个判断敌人方位可好用了！
I'll tell you another one! – I usually aim wherever you're yelling. It's the best way to guess where the enemies are!
Amiri：你给我的感觉像是有个舒适的家、有张温暖的床、还有人给你煮饭。你跑出来图什么？城里人的好日子让你变得弱不禁风。
You smell like a warm bed, a cozy home, and food cooked by someone else. What the hell are you doing out here? Civilization has made you too weak.
Jubilost：但是我很聪明，是队伍里最聪明的。虽然我力气比熊小，但比它们都聪明，而你虽然力气跟熊一样大，智商却也和它们差不多。
But smart – the smartest. I'm weaker than a bear but I'm smarter. Meanwhile you're about equal to a bear on both counts.
Amiri：嘿，书呆子！想不想听捕猎剑齿枭熊的故事？那次狩猎可刺激了！
Hey, book kid! Want to hear a story about hunting a saber-toothed owlbear? That was a good hunt!
Linzi：剑齿枭熊？哈哈哈！安米芮啊，枭熊没有牙齿，它们长的是尖喙！
A saber-toothed owlbear? Hahaha! But Amiri, owlbears don't have teeth – they have beaks!
Amiri：我曾杀死过一整群猛犸象，但当时离营地太远，所以我就把它们的象鼻子扎在一起，拖了两天才拖回家。
Once I killed a whole herd of mammoths. But our camp was too far away! So I tied their trunks together and dragged them home for two days.
Linzi：一定都是微型猛犸象吧！和松鼠一样大小！
Those must have been tiny mammoths! The size of squirrels!
Amiri：书呆子，你不相信我讲的故事？这些故事都是真的，特别有意思！
Book kid, you don't believe my stories?! They're good, interesting stories!
Linzi：你知道吗，我们吟游诗人有时候也会在书里编一些故事……但是我们不会写得那么假。
You know, we bards sometimes lie a lot in our books too... But we try to not to be so obvious about it.
Amiri：我说，琳翠……能不能编一段关于我的传奇？至少来个一小段也好！
Listen, Linzi... Can you make up a legend about me? A short one, at least!
Linzi：你？一小段？你的事迹足以写成两大篇英雄史诗，剩下的还够写出一篇冒险故事！
About you? A short one?! But your feats would be enough for two heroic epics – with plenty left over for a saga!
Amiri：诺克诺克，你看着又小又弱，但打起架来像熊一样凶猛！
Nok-Nok, you are so small and feeble, but ferocious like a bear!
NokNok：我是拉玛什图最青睐的战士！母亲只喜欢用尽全力的刺杀和劈砍！
I am Lamashtu's favored. Mother likes when stabs and jabs are heartfelt!
Amiri：嘿，地精！我看到你在我的背包附近鬼鬼祟祟的，想偷东西吗？
Hey, goblin! I saw you near my things. Tried to steal something?
NokNok：安米芮有奇特的锐器。想要仔细看看。没碰你的！不想被那锐器割手！
Amiri has strange stabber. Wanted to look closer. Touched nothing! Don't want to get hands slashed with that stabber!
Amiri：我好无聊啊！真想找人痛快打一架！
I feel so dreary! I want to punch someone so hard!
Octavia：冷静点，安米芮。这里没人陪你打架，也没必要动手。你要是想听，我可以给你讲个故事。
Easy, Amiri. There's no one to fight here, nor any reason to. I could read you something for fun if you like.
Kalikke：真是怪事，安米芮，我不认为你是个恶人，可有时候觉得你活着只是为了破坏。
It's so strange, Amiri. I see no evil in you. But sometimes I think you live only for destruction.
Amiri：我想问一下，奥克塔维雅，在你老家，女人真的像垃圾一样被对待吗？
Listen, Octavia. Where you're from, do they also treat women like garbage?
Octavia：我不太记得故乡的事了。但我是在纽玫瑞亚长大的，在那里，无论男女都不被当人看，我甚至说不清谁的处境更糟糕。
I don't remember my home too well. But in Numeria where I grew up, they treated everyone like dirt, men and women alike. I don't even know which one's worse.
Amiri：瑞贡尕和你本该凑成一双靴子，只可惜两只都是左脚，哈！
Regongar and you are like two boots that make a pair. Only both are left boots, ha!
Octavia：是啊！如果我们不需要彼此，那还有谁会需要我们呢？
Yeah, that's right! Who else would need us, if we didn't need each other?
Amiri：你用一个法术就轰倒了那家伙，哈！除了一双冒烟的鞋，什么都不剩！
The way you smashed that creep with a spell, ha! Left nothing but smoking boots!
Octavia：你也不错啊，拎起那两个家伙，然后把头盔砸在一起！发出了敲钟的声音！
And the way you picked up those two and crashed their helmets together! It sounded like the bells were tolling!
Kanerah：[叹气] 如果我没那么狠地轰他，或许就能找到一些比鞋更值钱的东西了……
[sighs] And if I didn't 'smash' him so hard, maybe we'd have found something more valuable than boots...
Amiri：奥克塔维雅，你为什么那么在意你的发型？有必要吗？在这里你还能吸引谁？是巨魔还是什么其它怪物？
Octavia, why do you always primp your hair, huh? Why the hell you need to? Who are you going to charm here? Trolls or something?
Octavia：人人都有软肋，我喜欢让自己漂漂亮亮的，之前在奴隶营里根本没条件打扮。
Everyone has their little weaknesses. I like to look pretty. I didn't get a chance to do it much in the slave camp.
Amiri：成天摆弄这些法术……全是小把戏！要是你的法术书不见了还怎么战斗？让我来教教你怎么使剑吧！
This magic all the time... It's all just tricks and fooling around! What if you lost all your books? How'd you fight then? Let me show you a few moves with a sword!
Octavia：噢，我可不想抢了你这位伟大战士的风头！你自己好好磨练剑术就行，我就在你身后继续耍我的小把戏。
Oh, I wouldn't want to steal the great warrior's glory! Let's leave the swordplay to you, and I'll stay behind you with my tricks.
Amiri：嘿，大牙，来摔个跤，活动活动怎么样？我好无聊。
Hey, fangs, how about some wrestling? I'm bored.
Regongar：跟你活动筋骨？要不要找张床？我早就想跟你大战三百回合了，哈哈！
Hand to hand with you? How about we do it inside a sack? I'm always ready for that, ha ha!
Amiri：瑞贡尕，你会不会觉得你的獠牙很碍事？上嘴皮撅成那样一定很难受。
Regongar, don't your fangs bother you? It must be uncomfortable to have such a snout.
Regongar：怎么？吃饭和亲吻都不碍事，还要求什么呢？
Why? I can eat just fine – and kiss. What else is there?
Amiri：我希望我也可以，就是……闪电！砰！然后眼前只剩下灰烬和青烟，你能不能教教我？
I wish I could just, you know – lightning – and BAM! And there's just smoke and embers left. Will you teach me?
Regongar：是啊，那感觉太棒了！但这是天生的，教不了。要是想学魔法，你就得像奥克塔维雅一样，会认字！
Yeah, it's great! But it's inborn – you either have it or you don't. To learn magic you'd have to read books like Octavia!
Amiri：瑞贡尕，这个科技团伙还是什么的东西……你有没有想过干脆把他们全杀掉？
Regongar, about this Technic Gang or whatever... Did you try just like killing all of them?
Regongar：哈，好主意！问题是我们只有两个人，他们有好几千个。就算他们一个个排成队让我们杀，我们割喉也得割到老！
Ha, a great plan! Only that there was two of us and thousands of them. Even if they lined up and didn't fight back, we'd be slicing their throats until we were old!
Amiri：好想喝啤酒，来一桶就更好了，或者来两桶！然后找个枕头呼呼大睡……
A pint of beer would be nice. Or a barrel. Or better two! And hit the pillow...
Regongar：啤酒只能等我们回去再说了，不过枕头就不用等了……哈哈……嗯……
The beer will have to wait till we get back. But the pillow doesn't have to, ha ha hmm…
Amiri：闪电确实厉害……不过呢，所有魔法都只是耍花招，战斗中最好使的还是剑！
Lightning is good, of course... But still, all this magic is just fooling around. What matters in battle is a sword!
Regongar：哈哈，你只是在嫉妒我的本事你学不会！
Ha ha, you're just jealous that I can do it and you can't!
Amiri：等打起来的时候你最好给我小心点！如果我说了这个怪物是我的，你就去找别的目标，绝不能抢了我的功劳！
Watch where you're going in battle! If I say a monster is mine, then you find another one, and don't steal my glory!
Regongar：我才不管什么功劳不功劳的！我只想尽快杀光敌人！之后你可以跟大家说所有怪物都是你一个人干掉的！
I don't give a damn about glory! I just want the enemies dead – and quick! After that, you can just tell everyone you slaughtered them all by yourself!
Kalikke：我宁愿惹你生气，也不愿看着怪物杀了你或其他人。
I'd rather make you mad than let a monster kill you — or any of the others.
Amiri：你能不能再演示一下刚才那个动作？就是把那家伙劈成两半的那招？嘿，你为什么又在坏笑？你再敢嘴巴不干净，我就弄死你！
Will you show me that move? The one you used to slice that creep in half? Hey, what you grinning at? You just dare to make another joke about your tent!
Regongar：哈哈哈！你就知道我在想什么！好吧，我来教你，没问题！
Ha ha ha! You're reading my mind! All right, I'll show you, no problem!
Amiri：崔斯提安，你为什么一直这么沉闷，嗯？我见过你战斗的样子！你的剑上沾满鲜血，脚下堆满尸体……但你的表情始终那么悲伤，就像死的是你家人一样。
Tristian, why you so boring, huh? I've seen you fight! Sword covered in blood, corpses underneath your feet – but your face is so sad, it's like your favorite uncle's funeral.
Tristian：莎恩芮教导我们要对所有人抱有仁慈之心，即便是敌人。我打断了他们生命的旅程，也就剥夺了他们为自己赎罪、改过自新的机会。
Sarenrae teaches us to show mercy to all, even one's enemies. By interrupting their life's journey, I've deprived them of a chance to better themselves and atone for their sins.
Amiri：要是有人打你，你只需要格挡然后反击，狠狠揍那个混蛋，把他的耳朵都打飞！但是你听听你一直都在说什么：仁慈、宽恕……
If someone hits you on your cheek, you just need to block it, counter-punch, and hit the bastard so hard his ears fall off! Just listen to yourself: mercy, forgiveness...
Tristian：安米芮，你混淆了仁慈和愚蠢。成为一个仁慈的人并不代表要一味忍耐、处处退让
Amiri, you confuse mercy and stupidity. One can be merciful and also able to stand up for oneself.
Amiri：嘿，崔斯提安！我们来掰腕子吧！我赌我们掰五次，我就会赢五次。
Hey, Tristian! Want to arm-wrestle? I bet I'll win five out of five!
Tristian：如果你想要向大家证明你的力量，那去和巨魔掰手腕吧，就算你把它的手腕掰断了，它也会很快复原的。
If you want to show everyone your strength, go wrestle a troll. At least his broken bones will heal quickly.
Amiri：我问你啊，崔斯提安，你怎么事事都想原谅别人？揍他们一顿，他们立刻就会放尊重点了！
Listen, Tristian, why you always forgive everyone for everything? Punch them once, then they respect you right away!
Tristian：安米芮，我不需要这种尊重，它只能带来压抑的愤怒。他们迟早会明白自己的错误，并为自己的草率和鲁莽之语道歉。
I don't need that kind of respect, Amiri. It brings nothing but suppressed anger. Sooner or later, they'll understand they were wrong, and apologize for their carelessness and rash words.
Amiri：那可好，既然你的莎恩芮可以原谅所有人、所有事，那如果我不还你钱，她也会原谅的，是吧？
Listen, if your Sarenrae forgives everyone and everything, maybe she'll forgive me about the couple of coins I owe you, huh?
Tristian：安米芮啊安米芮……莎恩芮可以宽恕人的罪过，而不是债务。她会给每个人弥补自己过错的机会，所以你也会得到机会还钱！
Amiri, Amiri... Sarenrae forgives sins, not debts. She gives everyone a chance to atone for their mistakes. So you have a chance to repay me!
Amiri：你一直这么礼貌、轻声细语的，不累吗？你难道不想偶尔发泄一下吗，嗯？拿起剑，我们来过两招！我会点到为止的！
Aren't you tired of being so polite and soft? Don't you want to let off some steam, huh? Go on, pick up a sword, let's practice! I won't hit you too hard!
Tristian：谢谢你的好意，但我不会举起武器。我的双手是用来治疗伤口的，而不是造成伤口。
Thank you for caring, but I don't want to take up arms. These hands like to heal wounds, not inflict them.
Amiri：我搞不懂你，你在战斗中并不懦弱，也不会临阵脱逃，但你为什么总像个毛头小鬼一样唉声叹气的？
I don't get you. You're not a coward in battle. You don't flee from our enemies. So why you always keep sighing and whining like a pup?
Tristian：安米芮，勇气正是由悲悯磨砺而出的锋芒。不过你没有把我视作懦夫，我很感动。
Courage is the hardened end of compassion, Amiri. But I am heartened you do not take me for a coward.
Amiri：今早我抓起一件衣服，发现上面绣着图案……松树、云朵、还有一只枭熊在和巨魔作战……老实交代，是谁干的？
So I pick up my clothes this morning — and see some pictures embroided on it — pine trees, clouds, an owlbear fighting a troll... Spill it out, who did that?!
Valerie：我承认，是我。本来只是想修补一下……结果不小心走神了。
I confess. It was I. — I only meant to patch them up a bit… and got a little carried away.
Amiri：已经五比五了吗？还是四比五？
Five to five already? Or still four to five?
Valerie：我赢了四次，你赢了五次，今天的对练很尽兴！
I won four times, you won five. A good day for sparring!
Amiri：你的功夫还不错，但有些单调，格挡、突刺，全靠手里的盾牌……这有什么意思？你想想，一拳打在太阳穴，一拳打在下巴上，然后再狠狠打第三拳，让你的对手眼冒金星！
You fight good but you're boring. A block, a lunge, all with a shield in your hand – where's the fun in that? Just imagine: hit one in the ear, the other in the jaw, and punch the third one so hard he sees above the clouds!
Valerie：要不是我的盾牌，全队伍所有钱都要用来买治疗药水了。你喜欢冲锋陷阵，我完全没意见，但最好还是让我掩护你，安全起见！
If it weren't for my shield, the whole party would spend all we have on healing potions. You like cracking skulls, and I'm perfectly content with that, but you'd better let me watch your back. It'll be a lot safer for you!
Amiri：你总是在练习，但总是一个人练，像你这样一直重复同一个突刺动作，不觉得无聊吗？要不要跟我一起练？
You practice so much – and always by yourself. Aren't you tired of doing the same lunges over and over again? How about we work together?
Valerie：当然可以，不过我还是会花时间独自练习。对我来说，战斗中最关键的就是纪律、自制力和钢铁般的意志。
Fine, why not. But it won't replace my practice. For me, the most important asset in battle is discipline, self-control and iron will.
Amiri：嘿，我也想出生在贵族家庭，有仆人伺候，还会把早饭送到床边。我的部落就没那么好了，他们只会一脚踹醒你，然后催你去给他们做饭……那羊肉味太膻了！
Heh, I wish I was born in a noble family. Servants to lick my boots and bring me breakfast in bed. Not like in my tribe. They wake you up with a kick, and hustle you to cook for them – the stinking goats!
Valerie：我敢说你会比我更受不了贵族生活，如果让你多试穿几套礼服、或是和长辈一起喝下午茶，你一定会砸毁那个地方，然后逃进森林！
I bet your time among nobility would be even shorter than mine. You'd jump up the moment you were trying on your third dress, or drinking tea with your auntie. You'd trash the place and run into the forest!
Amiri：瓦尔瑞，你最喜欢什么武器？
Valerie, what weapon do you like best?
Valerie：手上握着的就是最好的，我接受过各种兵器训练：长矛、斧头、大锤、长柄斧，徒手战斗也没问题。
Whichever one I'm holding. I've been trained to fight using any blade. Spears, axes, bludgeons and poleaxes. As well as with no weapon at all.
Amiri：在我老家有句俗话，‘穿得越厚，整理好遗体送去火葬花的时间就越久’。这话说的就是你啊，瓦尔瑞！
Back home we'd say, 'the thicker the armor, the longer it takes to ready the corpse for the pyre.' That's definitely true about you, Valerie!
Valerie：但处理你的遗体就很快，我们只需要把你的胳膊和腿缝回去，让你看上去像个人样就行。
But you'd be quick. We'd just sew your arms and legs back, so you're more-or-less human shaped.
生日祝福
Amiri：哈！为什么这么安静？我们不应该为首领举杯庆祝吗？
Ha! Why is it so quiet? Aren't we going to toast and drink in the name of our chief?
Amiri：生日是个好日子，{主角名}！是时候数数你今年打过的硬仗，还有猎杀的野兽和怪物了！我只有一个祝愿：希望在你回首往事时，不至于没什么令人兴奋的事可以回忆！
A birthday is a good day, {name}! Time to count all the great fights you had this year, all the beasts and monsters you've killed! I wish you one thing: may you never see a day when you turn around and there's nothing exciting to remember!

Looks like Tartuccio wasn't here for the Stag Lord. I don't know what he is up to, but I know what I'll do when I catch that snotty little saucebox...

That gnome is a slippery snake. But spineless sneaks rarely work for themselves. They always need someone to take orders from...

There's no point in being bitter, Amiri. The baroness/baron will decide who will wear the Stag Lord's helm. She is our leader. It's up to her.

C21_AfterDelicateSituation

That frog thing in the lake... watcha call it... it's weird — something green and slimy messing with so many people's heads!

The fey have their own ways of dealing with mortals. Take care not to get caught by such a frog yourself!

Nixies are famous tricksters. And I've heard they drown any victims who resist their tricks.

The nixie's ridiculous appearance has always filled me with disgust and curiosity at the same time. I wonder what kind of zombies they'd make...

I've never met a fey before – but now I see they're more dangerous than genies. That tiny water girl subdued those wood-cutters so easily!
C21_HodagDefeat

Actually, that hodag wasn't so tough. — Though it was fat like a beached whale. But compared to giants it was just a pile of crap.

I don't think we'll find many giants in these parts. But you know what — trolls are their distant relatives. You could certainly practice killing them.

I'd love to see a real giant! But fight one? I think I'll pass!

C21_RiverFreedoms
Amiri：我听本地人提到什么水上规矩之类的。
I've heard the locals talking 'bout some Water Rules.
Octavia：你说的是《河流自由法》吧，安米芮。那是失窃之地民众遵从的一套不成文的法则，我也听本地人提起过。
You're talking about River Freedoms, Amiri. It's the unified set of unwritten laws that the people of the Stolen Lands live by. I've heard the locals talking about them too.
Jubilost：失窃之地的法规：六条河流自由法，真是自立法规的一个有趣案例。比方说在这里，鬼鬼祟祟入室盗窃就比老老实实当街抢劫要严重得多，穷山恶水出刁民吧。
The Six River Freedoms, the laws of the Stolen Lands. It's a curious example of self-imposed legislation. For example, sneaky burglary here is considered a much more serious felony than honest robbery. A tough place makes for a tough people.
Linzi：你说的大概是《河流自由法》吧，那是河域诸国流传的法规。对了，头一条河流自由法就保证了言论自由——虽然大多统治者对此完全没当回事。
You probably mean the River Freedoms. They're the traditional laws of the River Kingdoms. By the way, the first of them guarantees freedom of speech — though some rulers don't give a damn about it.
C22_AfterGoblinVillage

Did you see that goblin king's wolf? That was one impressive beast!
Jubilost：唔……那家伙的皮毛肯定能做一张奢华的地毯！真可惜我这里放不下了，不然我一定会好好考虑一下的……
Hm... The beast's hide would make a luxurious carpet! Too bad I have no place to put it, or I'd think very carefully on the matter...
Octavia：我在想它最爱吃什么，也许是不听话的亲戚吧？
I wonder about its favorite food. Disobedient relatives, perhaps?

I haven't the slightest idea how the goblins tame them! Where did they get the sense to do it? One can only imagine it was some sort of divine intervention...

Great beast all right! A Hero should ride it, not some shatshank like goblin king!
C22_Start

Everyone keeps talking about monsters! Talk, talk, talk. Me — I can't wait for another fight!

C31_AfterBarbarianCamp

Ughhh, I didn't like that masked witch. Who are these Defaced Sisters, anyway? Why would fierce warriors crawl through dungeons on their say, instead of fighting and hunting?
Jubilost：我也从来没听说过她们，也许是个什么新兴的教派？那群野蛮人居然肯由她们差遣，也真是奇怪。
I've never heard of them before either. Perhaps they're some new religious order? It is indeed strange that the barbarians would obey them.

She gave the feeling of something... familiar to me. Grief, and darkness.

One thing is sure. They did not tell us all they know.

I feel the Defaced Sisters share some terrible grief. What could make these women reject their names and their faces?
C31_SeasonOfBloomFeedback

Ha, Ekun, Ekun... How can I answer this question? I'm more surprised that none of you think about it much at all!

I am a dead man, just like us all. We are alive for a moment, and dead for eternity. Which condition do you think more important?

Stay away from me, Jaethal. You smell like the dead.

My perfume smells of lavender, sandalwood and a hint of musk. While you stink of grief and gloom. Which of us is more dead?

Unlife is worse than death. Death is honest, Jaethal. Why do you fear it so?
Jaethal：你真的认为你自己就不惧怕死亡吗？那么你还等什么呢？手边不是就有把剑嘛。跟随你的家人而去吧……反正没人会为你哭丧。
Do you truly think you've no fear of death? What are you waiting for then? A blade is always at hand. Follow your family – no one's going to cry for you.
Ekun：要和你一同旅行真是太糟糕了，耶萨尔。
It's too bad our paths lie side by side, Jaethal.
Jaethal：要忍受你作为我的队友，我也不好受啊。也许有一天我们可以彻底地解决我们之间的争端。目前来说，你只能闭嘴，好好地完成自己的任务。
I find no pleasure in bearing you as an ally, either. Perhaps the day will come when you and I settle our arguments once and for all. For now, just stay quiet and do what you must.
Ekun：{主角名}信任你。但是我不。我会提防你。
{name} trusts you. I don't. I watch you.
Jaethal：这和信任有什么关系？{主角名}很清楚我们的合作关系有利无害，而且他知道我也明白这一点。你尽管提防我。不过如果我想要加害于你，你就会发现你根本来不及反应。
What does trust have to do with it? {Name} sees the benefits of our alliance, and knows I see them too. You can watch all you want, but if I decide to turn on you, you'll see it too late to react.
Ekun：你的所作所为和怪物没什么两样。相比之下你更像是一只巨魔，而非精灵！你没有自尊吗？
You have the manners of a monster. You are more like a troll than an elf! Where is your pride?
Jaethal：队伍里的人都觉得你是个一字千金的人。但是一见到我，你就突然口若悬河起来。所以你能不能大发慈悲，用你那传说中的沉默寡言来和我相处。
Everyone in the party thinks you're the quiet one. But with me you are surprisingly eloquent. But perhaps you'd be so kind as to show me your legendary reticence.
Ekun：不要站在我的身后。否则我就拿箭射你。我并不信任你。
Don't stand behind me. I'll shoot you. I don't trust you.
Jaethal：那你最好也别站在我的上风处。你身上的气味简直让我想用尽全部法术来把你净化掉，连骨头都不剩下。
And you'd better not stand upwind of me. Your smell makes me want to waste a couple spells to cleanse you. – To the bone.
Ekun：朱比罗斯特，我们是盟友，不是敌人。你为什么要对与你一同浴血奋战的人这么刻薄呢？
Jubilost. We are allies, not enemies. Why are you unkind to those who shed their blood with you?
Jubilost：我刻薄？艾昆，我亲爱的朋友啊，我明明对整个队伍的人都非常友好、宽容而且和善，尽管你们有些人并不配我的好态度！而且呢，你觉得我‘刻薄’，我更想称之为‘坦诚’。
I'm unkind? Ekun, my dear friend, I'm incredibly kind, tolerant, and placid towards our entire troupe, even to those who may not deserve it! Besides, what you call 'unkindness' I call 'honesty'.
Ekun：朱比罗斯特不管吃多少都吃不饱，他那么小的个子，怎么会有这么大的食量？
Jubilost is never full, no matter how much he eats. How can a tiny body hold such a huge stomach?
Jubilost：我想吃多少就吃多少！又不是吃不起！还有你最好把你的狗拴好，昨天晚上它偷吃了我留着当早餐的肉干！
I eat as much as I like! I can afford it! You'd better watch your dog, though. – Last night it stole the jerked meat I'd saved for morning!
Ekun：朱比罗斯特，你去过那么多国家，见识过那么多风土人情。在一些地方，你还这么牙尖嘴利的话，可能会惹祸上身……
Jubilost, you've been to many countries. Seen many different customs. There are places where a sharp word nets you a knife in a back...
Jubilost：的确如此！不过话说回来，我也不是好惹的。我的话有时候很伤人，但我的炼金火焰药瓶更让人痛不欲生！
Very true! But on the other hand, I'm not helpless either. My words might sometimes be painful, but a vial of alchemical fire hurts a good deal more!
Ekun：我好像读过你的书，关于恐龙的，对吗？
I think I've read your book. About dinosaurs. Am I right?
Jubilost：啊，是的！‘巨型动物与其烹饪方法’这本？是的，那本书很不错！嘿，也不知道我是否还有机会再次尝尝三角龙的舌头呢？
Ah yes! 'Megafauna And How To Cook It'? Yes, that one wasn't bad! Heh, but will I ever try the tongue of a triceratops again?
Ekun：坐下，别咬人。我也不喜欢他，不过他是我们的朋友。
Sit. Don't attack. I don't like him either, but he's a friend.
Jubilost：哈，告诉你的宠物，我也不喜欢它，我本想亲口告诉它，但自从它咬坏了我的野外炼金参考书后，我就再也不想理它了！
Ha. Tell your beast I don't like it either. I'd tell it myself, but we're not on speaking terms after it gnawed on my field alchemy reference book!
Ekun：你的话语如同一条毒蛇，伤害着每一个人，为什么要这样？。
You hurt everyone. Why? Your tongue is like a snake.
Jubilost：我绝不会无缘无故伤害任何人！我只是指出别人的错误，只可惜没人愿意承认自己有错！所以他们只会迁怒于我，而不是感激我并做出改变！
I never hurt anyone without a good reason! I point out people's mistakes for them. It's just that hardly anybody likes to admit they're wrong! – So they get angry at me instead of thanking me and improving themselves!
Ekun：提高警惕，要是有人跟踪我们怎么办。
Look around. What if someone is following us.
Jubilost：你总是在提防袭击！想想啊，你可是所向无敌的艾昆，再加上你那几个荒唐的朋友，有哪个神智清醒的人会去跟踪一支这样的队伍？
You're always on the lookout for an attack! But just think about it: who in their right mind would follow a party including the invincible Ekun and a few of his insane friends?
Kalikke：我一直在观察周围，这里所有东西都很特别，和我的故乡完全不同，就连天空看起来都不一样！
All I do is look around. Everything is so different here, so unlike my homeland. Even the sky seems different!
Kanerah：我怀疑跟来的可能是我的仰慕者，就算在这种穷乡僻壤，也很难甩掉他们！
Admirers, I suspect. They're hard to lose, even in this backwater!
Ekun：你说沃达凯翘辫子了？他留着辫子吗？为什么是翘辫子，而不是翘腿？
You said Vordakai is screwed? Screwed to what? Why use screws and not nails?
Linzi：因为……辫子比较多样啊。你可以翘辫子、剪辫子还可以扯辫子。
Because screws are multipurpose tools. You can screw a table, screw up or screw around.
Ekun：别盯着我，小作家。我可没有故事可供你写进书里。
Don't look at me, storyteller. I have no stories for your book.
Linzi：是吗？森林里的每一片树叶和每一只小虫你都能讲得头头是道！怎么偏偏就不能讲讲你自己呢？
Oh, how come? You can talk about every leaf and bug in the forest! You can't say anything about yourself?
Ekun：你要是想听故事，就去问别人吧，我这人可没什么英雄事迹。
If you need stories, ask the others. I've performed no feats.
Linzi：你觉得我只对英雄事迹感兴趣？哈！傻子都能写出英雄传说来，但要说到真实的人生嘛……
You think I'm only interested in feats? Ha! Any fool can write about feats. But about true life...
Ekun：琳翠，我不想提我自己的事情，我的家庭，我的人生，都不想聊。
Linzi. I don't want to talk about myself. Or my family. Or my life. I don't.
Linzi：我都还没开口呢，你就已经要我住口了！我只是想问问你还有没有盐！
I even didn't open my mouth yet, and already you're shutting me up! I just wanted to ask if you had any salt left!
Ekun：你一定会喜欢安布萨朗的，那是一座熙熙攘攘的大城市。
You would like it in Absalom. It's a noisy city. Big.
Linzi：嘿，我也希望可以将我的故乡换成安布萨朗！而你没准也会在我那个宁静的小村庄过得安乐。但是现在……好吧！现在我们的家都在这里，在失窃之地了！
Heh, I wish I could trade my homeland for Absalom! And you'd probably be happy in my quiet village. But now – oh well! Now we both have our home here, in the Stolen Lands!
Kalikke：我喜欢时不时造访各座城市，但我更喜欢睡在繁星下的帐篷里，而不是只能盯着石头墙发呆。
I like visiting cities from time to time, but I'd rather sleep in a tent under the stars, than stare at stone walls.
Kanerah：噢对，我想去那儿！或许我还会接受星石试炼！试想一下，我会成为什么样的女神！
Oh yes, I'd like to go there! And maybe take the Test of the Starstone! Just imagine what a goddess I would make!
Ekun：为什么人们要把所有事情都写下来？记忆可比纸张可靠多了。
Why do people write everything down? Memory is firmer than paper.
Linzi：你没法在图书馆里借阅别人的记忆！再说了，记忆会随人的死亡一同消逝，而书籍则可以永久保存……好吧，或许没办法永久保存，但肯定比写书的作者寿命更长！
You can't borrow someone else's memory and read it in a library! Besides, memories die with their owners, while books keep living... Well, maybe not forever, but definitely longer than their authors!
Kalikke：它让我心境平和。铭记一时的美好是一回事，但要在多年后将它活灵活现地描述出来就是另一回事了。
I find peace in it. Remembering the beauty of a moment is one thing, but describing it in a way that will bring it to life years later — that's something different.
Kanerah：尊敬的艾昆达尤，你过去也当过商队守卫，你觉得要是你的雇主只在脑子里记账，而不在纸上写下来，他的买卖还能做大吗？
Most honorable Ekundayo, you used to guard caravans. Do you think your employer's business would have thrived if he only did his accounting in memory, not on paper?
Ekun：别碰那些花，很危险，有毒的。
Don't touch those flowers. They're dangerous. Poison.
Linzi：噢！真的吗？我本来还打算摘一束呢！为什么这么漂亮的花会有毒呢？不过谢谢你的警告。
Oh! Seriously?! And I was about to pick a bouquet! How can such beautiful flowers be poisonous? Thanks for the warning.
Ekun：离我远点，诺克诺克。我们道不同不相为谋。
Keep your distance, Nok-Nok. We follow different paths.
NokNok：为什么？你要走另一条路吗？一个人去冒险？如果是真的，那么就再见了！
How so? Will you go separately? Seek your own adventure? If so, farewell then!
Ekun：你的内心很善良，不过奴隶的镣铐却在你心中留下了深深的伤疤。
Your heart is kind. But the shackles of slavery leave deep scars.
Octavia：我不太明白你的意思，艾昆。不过我是被自己亲生母亲卖掉的……没错，这种事一辈子也忘不了。
I'm not sure I take your meaning, Ekun. But being sold into slavery by your own mother — it's true, you can't just forget something like that.
Ekun：你很善良，不过你的善意起不了作用，我再也回不到从前了。
You are kind, but your kindness misses its target. I will never be the same.
Octavia：我并没抱有期望！你可以成为任何你喜欢的样子，这才是真正的自由！
I don't have any targets! You can be whatever you like. That's true freedom!
Ekun：奥克塔维雅，请告诉我，你的双亲是不是……
Octavia, tell me, did your parents...
Octavia：别再说了好吗？我理解，那些发生在你家人身上的事……但是看在诸神的份上，别再提及我的家人了，不然我可要说很不好听的话了！
Not another word, all right? I know, I know, your family and everything... But, for the love of the gods, don't mention my parents again, unless you want to hear something you won't like!
Ekun：你有一处瘀伤，这会让你很不舒服的，可以用水蛭驱散淤血。
You have a bruise. That will bother you. A leech would help.
Octavia：什么？水蛭？不了谢谢。它总会自己慢慢痊愈的！
What? A leech?! No thank you. It'll somehow have to figure out a way to heal by itself!
Ekun：瑞贡尕，你不够珍视生命，你的鲁莽终究会害了你。
Regongar, you don't value life. Your recklessness will be your undoing.
Regongar：我不够珍视生命？哈！我可太懂过日子了！痛快的战斗、丰盛的饭菜，开怀畅饮还有温暖的被窝……这就是生活啊！
I don't value my life? Ha! I have a fantastic knack for life! A good fight, a hearty meal, a strong drink and a warm bed – now that's life!
Ekun：我的匕首在哪？瑞贡尕，你看到我的匕首了吗？
Where's my dagger? Regongar, have you seen it?
Regongar：你的匕首？我好像是找到一把小刀，用来切面包了。是在你的背包旁边找到的……你到那边的草丛里找找看。
Your dagger? I think I grabbed a knife to cut myself some bread. It was lying by your knapsack – look over there in the grass.
Ekun：你把你现在变成这样的原因归罪于科技联盟，但奴隶主的鞭子只会伤害肉体，摧残你灵魂的人是你自己。
You blame the Technic League for what you have become. But a slaver's lash hurts only the body. It was you alone who mangled your soul.
Regongar：是是是，带着你这套大道理见鬼去吧，自作聪明的家伙。你自己都活成这样，还好意思来说教别人！
Sure, well you can go to hell with your philosophy. Some man of wisdom! You pissed away everything you had in life, and now you want to go around teaching others!
Ekun：瑞贡尕，你该庆幸有奥克塔维雅，请珍惜她。
You are lucky, Regongar. You have Octavia. Treasure her.
Regongar：那可不是嘛！多亏你提醒我，不然我还真不知道应该去珍惜我生命中最重要的人。谢谢你了啊，艾昆……你简直就是我的人生导师！
No way! Without you, I'd never have figured out that I should value the most important person in my life. Thanks, Ekun – you really opened my eyes here!
Ekun：你太残忍了，如果不去压制你心中兽性，最终也会沦为一头野兽。
You are too cruel. If you do not control the beast in you, you'll become an animal yourself.
Regongar：我本来就是一头野兽！他们本来想把我驯成看门犬，到头来却养成了一头野狼，哈哈！
I've always been an animal! They wanted to raise me like a watchdog, but they got themselves a wild wolf, ha ha.
Ekun：崔斯提安，你看我的眼神很奇怪。有什么问题吗？
Tristian, you're looking at me strangely. What's wrong?
Tristian：我在你身上看到了如此强烈的痛苦和折磨……请原谅我无法为你减轻负担。
I see so much pain and suffering in you... Forgive me for not being able to lighten your burden.
Ekun：我亲眼见识过你的战斗能力，战斗时也毫不畏惧，但你为什么要回避战斗呢？
You can fight. You aren't afraid. I have seen this. So why do you avoid fighting?
Tristian：因为无论输赢，每场战斗都是一次失败。每夺去一条生命，就有一个灵魂再也无法忏悔和得到宽恕。
Because every battle, win or lose, is another defeat. Every death is another soul that will never come to repent and be forgiven.
Ekun：牧师。你经常打量我。但是从不说话。
Cleric. You often look at me. But never speak.
Tristian：你不常聊自己的事。我……能感觉到你的痛苦。要是我能帮到你就好了。但是我的朋友，我很伤心，因为以我的能力无法宽慰你。
You rarely speak yourself. I… feel your pain. Would that I could ease it. And I am saddened, my friend, that it's beyond my power to console you.
Ekun：你从不说粗鄙的话，但你的所作所为却时常冷酷无情，为什么会这样呢？
You seldom utter a rude word, but your words and actions are often cruel. Why is this?
Valerie：这二者并不冲突，我的剑永远锋利闪亮，也无法容许我的盔甲沾染尘土……不过一旦身处战场，我也绝不会手下留情。
One doesn't exclude the other. My sword always shines, I can't stand dirt on my armor... but when it comes to battle, I slash without mercy.
Ekun：没有信仰的人生十分艰难，我离不开信仰。
It's difficult to live without faith. I couldn't do it.
Valerie：恕我冒昧，不过……你需要信仰，只是因为你从苦难中幸存。信仰于你就像是跛足者需要拐杖，而对我来说并不是必需。
Forgive me if my words seem rude, but... You only need faith because you've survived what you have. You need faith like a lame man needs his crutches. And I need it like a healthy man does.
Ekun：我没有欣赏过多少画作，但是我见过的那几幅都非常美丽。
I haven't seen many pictures. But those I have seen were beautiful.
Valerie：如果你像我一样见过很多幅画以后，就不会这么想了。那些画都……冰冷，浮夸，毫无价值。
You would think otherwise if you'd seen as many as I have. They are... Cold. Pretentious. And useless.
Ekun：你会认为自己高人一等吗？仅仅因为你的双亲是贵族？
Do you think you are better than others? Is it because your parents are nobles?
Valerie：这不是傲慢，而是自尊。可惜大多数人，特别是平民，从小就被教育要时刻自我怀疑，因此他们无法理解这二者之间的差别。
It's not arrogance, just dignity. Unfortunately, many people – especially among the commoners – are taught from childhood always to doubt themselves. That's why they don't understand the difference.
生日祝福
Ekun：今天您是主角，{主角名}。是时候请您的朋友们说说他们的祝福了。
It's your day, {name}. Time for your friends to say their well-wishes.
Ekun：我希望您总有一个目标，并且永远不会忽视它。指路人永不迷失。祝一切安好，生活幸福。这是我要说的。
Here's my wish: always have a purpose, and never lose sight of it. Those who possess a guiding light are never lost. Be well, live happily. That is what I have to say.
C21_AfterTartukAndTrollsEvent
Ekun：我一直都听说巨魔怕火。又是一件被改变的事实。
Knew all my life that trolls fear fire. One more thing that's changed.
Valerie：别担心那件事——我们还有酸液可以用，只要确保补给中的酸液充足就行了。
Don't worry on that account — we always can resort to acid. We just have to make sure we keep enough among our supplies.
Jubilost：我想知道，这将如何影响他们的文化……他们会开始用明火做饭吗？或者晚上围着篝火，坐在一起聊天，就像我们一样？我有一个科学家朋友，他肯定会对这些问题很感兴趣！
I wonder how will this influence their culture... Will they begin cooking on open fires? Or gather around the bonfire to sit and chat in the evening, like we do? I've a scientist friend who'd be thrilled at such questions!
Jaethal：几乎无敌……他们不懂怜悯，也不吃东西——直到周围堆积起足够多敌人的尸体……这样的勇士是无价之宝。
Almost invincible... They know no pity, need no food — until there are enough enemy corpses around... Such warriors are priceless.
Kalikke/Kanerah：只有随身带着火的人才能真正学会不畏惧它。至于巨魔……我们只是需要多用点火焰，仅此而已。
Only one who carries fire inside them can truly learn not to fear it. And as for trolls... We just need more fire, that's all.
C21_QuestLostChild
Ekun：纳玛彻斯可不是小孩子该来的地方，太危险了。
The Narlmarches is no place for a little boy. Too many dangers.
Octavia：我想知道，这些沼泽的中央隐藏着什么……我们会有机会知道真相吗？
I wonder what's hidden in the heart of these swamps... Will we ever know?
Valerie：打起精神来，艾昆，很快，我们就能去救那个可怜的孩子了。
Keep your spirits up, Ekun. Soon we'll come to the unfortunate child's rescue.
Linzi：他会没事的，你会看到的！孩子们并不像某些成年人那样无法自理——而且他们的小脑筋往往转得更快。
He'll be all right, you'll see! Children aren't nearly as helpless as some adults — and they often think much faster.
C21_Start
Ekun：我听说在别的土地上，在某个地方住着长金色鳞片的狗头人。
I heard that somewhere, in other lands, there live kobolds with golden scales.
Amiri：金色、蓝色还是红色……谁在乎呢？他们都是一样的：一群懦弱的小兽！
Gold, blue, red... who cares? They are all the same: little gutless beasts!
Octavia：金色的狗头人？他们会不会是金龙的远亲啊？你觉得呢？
Golden kobolds? Could they be distant relatives of golden dragons? What do you think?
Linzi：你知道的，不能只是因为你在一首歌里听到……你应该知道……不是所有的吟游诗人的话都能信的，艾昆。我们中有些人昏了头，只拣好听的话说。但是当然了，我总是很注意自己的作品的！
You know, just because you heard it in a song... you know... You can't trust all the bards, Ekun. Some of us get carried away and just say whatever sounds good. But of course I myself always mind what I write!
Kalikke/Kanerah：人们叙述的遥远土地上的奇闻异事……地方越远，奇迹就越惊人！
The miracles they recount in distant lands... The farther the land is, the more amazing its wonders!
C22_MonstersOutOfPeople
Ekun：我一直相信，每个人内心都有一只野兽。现在我看到，这些野兽挣扎着想要出来。
I've always believed that everyone carries an animal inside. Now I see these animals struggling to come out.
Jubilost：我希望你不要把它看作一个哲学问题。很显然，我们正在和某种邪恶的魔法打交道。面对它的时候，我们必须记住这一点。
I hope you don't see it as a philosophical problem. Obviously, we're dealing with some evil magic here, and we'll have to remember that when we confront it.
Tristian：心中的野兽？也许你说得对，不过我们现在面对的可不是什么灵魂深处的怪物。
The animal inside? You may be right about that, but what we're fighting now is not some inner monster of the soul.
Jaethal：哈，就像蝴蝶破茧而出。
Hah. Like the butterfly from its cocoon.
NokNok：要是诺克诺克体内也有大野兽就好了。也许现在还没跑出来。诺克诺克还有计划……
Would be awesome if greatbeast was inside Nok-Nok. But maybe not come out yet. Nok-Nok still have plans...
Kalikke/Kanerah：想要让一只怪物挣扎出来，首先得放一只进去。
In order for a monster to break out, it needs to have been put inside in the first place.
C22_Start
Ekun：和平的村落附近的怪兽。很糟糕。非常糟糕。
Monsters near peaceful villages. That's bad. Very bad.
Octavia：我真希望能知道他们来自何处。可能是什么魔法现象，也可能是自然原因导致的大迁徙。
I wish I knew where they came from. It might be some magical phenomenon, or a great migration brought on by natural causes.
Valerie：生活是残酷的，而我们都各有各的悲剧。但我们会竭尽所能打败这种威胁！
Life can be cruel and we all face our share of tragedy. But we'll do everything we can to defeat this menace!
Regongar：农民肯定满足不了这种胃口！而且他们也不好煮熟，哈哈！他们甚至还自带餐具——斧头，用来切块，还有干草叉，用来叉起食物。
Peasants certainly sate the appetite! And they're easy to cook, ha ha! They even bring their own cutlery — axes to cut them up with, and pitchforks to eat from.
Kalikke/Kanerah：怪兽在和平的农户之间公然徘徊。这不可能是自然发生的事情，应该是邪恶的意志使然。
Monsters prowling openly among the houses of the peaceful peasants. This cannot be a natural event. There's an evil will at work.
C31_AfterRavenFirstDialog
Ekun：真奇怪。我很了解鸟类，也见过很多种鸟，却从没见过有意识的鸟。
How odd. I know much about birds. Seen a lot of them. Never seen one that was sentient.
Jubilost：如果有人类长着鸽子的头脑，那么也一定有鸟儿长着人类的头脑！
If there be men with the minds of pigeons, there must be birds with the minds of men!
Octavia：魔法可以做很多事情。但就算这只渡鸦是只魔法生物，也实在太不寻常了。
Magic can do many things. But this raven seems unusual even for a magical creature.
Jaethal：侮辱当地的统治者，是一件‘有意识’的事情吗？不如说是‘鲁莽’吧。
Is it 'sentient' to insult the local ruler? One might instead say 'foolhardy'.
Kalikke/Kanerah：这只渡鸦真古怪！他是谁？这片土地的精魂？还是某人的信使？要么大概又是一个改变了外形，想来开一个残酷的玩笑的妖精？
What a strange bird this raven is! Who is he? A spirit of these lands? Or someone's messenger? Or perhaps another fey who changed their appearance to play a cruel joke?
C31_VoiceOgAdventures
Ekun：遥远的国度，危险的所在，古老的奥秘，它们都在发出自己的声音。一些人听到了它们的召唤，追寻着它们，而有些人则充耳不闻。踏上旅途的人，许多都殒命途中。
Far-away lands, dangerous places, ancient mysteries. Each has its own voice. Some hear that call and seek after it, while others are deaf to it. Of those who go, many perish.
Octavia：但其他人还是会跟随他们的步伐。毕竟，我们也来到了这里。而我们应该大概估计是死不了的！
But others will follow. After all, we came here too. And we most certainly will not perish!
Valerie：愚蠢。一项正义的事业是值得为之献身的，但为了对冒险的渴求送死却不值得。
Foolishness. A righteous cause is worth dying for. A thirst for adventures is not.
Regongar：……也有些人，被玻璃杯碰撞的脆响和路边旅店里妓女的呻吟所诱惑。说到这个，我们什么时候回都城？
...and then there's those who are most tempted by the sounds of clinking glasses and moaning whores at the roadside inn. Speaking of which, when are we heading back to the capital?
C41

There is much cruelty in the world. But still people start wars with each other. Haven't they suffered enough?

Don't look for sense in people's behavior — there is none.

A barbarian sees we have a big warm house — and comes here to take it. What he doesn't see is that we're carrying a big heavy stick behind our back, ha ha ha hmm...

What do you want from Kellids? I'm telling you — if you crack open skulls of the whole tribe, and scrape out what's inside, it won't be enough to fill even one normal head.
Kanerah：自然既不残酷也不仁慈。只是人会和魔鬼交易。
Nature knows neither cruelty nor mercy. Only people make deals with devils.

What are you talking about, Ekun? Are you thinking he might have something to do with our other trouble?

This is precisely why plebeians shouldn't know how to read. Propaganda leaflets wouldn't confuse people, if there weren't anyone to read them.

Valerie：我们该怎么应对这个不寻常的威胁呢？我们又不能任命一个将军，再派遣几支部队——他们连个作战对象都没有！
How do we respond to such an unusual threat? We can't appoint a general and send troops — they'd have no one to fight!
NokNok：对！更多敌人！诺克诺克捅，诺克诺克杀！
Yes! More enemies! Nok-Nok stabs, Nok-Nok kills!
C51_MonsterCampVisited
Ekun：怪物才不会思考。它四处杀戮，是因为它别的什么都不懂，恨它也没有意义。我知道的……没有用。
A monster does not think. It kills because it doesn't know any better. There is no sense in hating it. I know this... It doesn't help.
Regongar：思考，理由……有什么区别吗？你看着别人的脸，血液就开始沸腾；砸碎他的头，然后继续前行。你得享受生活，你懂的。
Thinking, knowing... What's the damn difference? You look at someone's mug and your blood starts to boil. Crack his skull and keep on walking. You've got to enjoy life, you know.
Linzi：呃，我也不知道了。难道它们真的完全不思考吗？甚至连巨魔都有某种宗教呢。我认为每一个生灵都会选择怎样生活——怎样举止。
Oh, I don't know. Do they really not think at all? Trolls even have some sort of religion. I think every living creature chooses how to live — and how to behave.
Harrim：你不能不去恨怪物——就像你不能不爱自己的家人一样。这两种感情都会折磨你，束缚你的精神，但你还是没有做好抛弃它们的准备。
You cannot stop hating the monsters — the same way you cannot stop loving your family. Both feelings torment you, both bind your spirit, but still you are not ready to give them up.
DeadInParty
Ekun：每当看到死亡，我就想起一些不愿想起的事情。
Death reminds me of things I'd rather forget.
Ekun：又一个生命逝去……我见到的死亡已经太多了。
One more death… I've seen far too much of it.

You could find consolation in my faith… but you are not yet ready, if you still try to avoid the inevitable.
Octavia：我们都有一些不好的回忆，在噩梦中纠缠不休。在这样的时刻，它们也会找上来。
We all have bad memories that come to us in nightmares. Or in times like this.
Valerie：你经历过悲伤的别离，但你的心依然坚强。不要失去你的坚强。永远不要让我们的心灵被朋友的逝去击溃！
You carry a heavy burden of loss, but you still stand strong. Don't lose that now. The deaths of our friends must never break our spirit!
Jaethal：如果一个人永远活在哀悼中，他就不曾为自己而活。人生短暂，却被你徒然浪费。这般虚度的人生远比死亡悲哀。
Those who constantly grieve over others never live for themselves. You're merely wasting time that you don't have much of. Such wasted life is worse by far than death.
Kalikke：我知道这种痛苦。当它冰冷的手攫住我的心脏时，我会到风的轻歌和云的曼舞中寻求慰藉。
I know this pain. When it grips my heart with its cold hand, I look for solace in the song of the wind and the dancing clouds.

[Wipes the sweat from his pale forehead]

Stop pretending you're fine! You need help!

Linzi：如果你不肯接受治疗，那是你自己的选择。可是，你也要为你那头可怜的小狼考虑一下！他会担心你的！他会叫个不停，没法安心。这样的烦恼对他也不好呀！
If you won't let yourself be healed, that's your choice, I suppose. But think about poor wolfy! He will worry about you — he'll whine and grow restless. This kind of trouble is definitely no good for him!
Kalikke：你就像块古老的岩石：表面看起来坚不可摧，内里却藏着孔洞和裂缝。要知道：对朋友们坦诚没什么可耻的。
You are like an ancient rock: indestructible on the surface, but inside there are caves and chasms. Know this: there is no shame in opening your heart to friends.

I'd better chance my luck and go to sleep. I don't want blue rings around my eyes tomorrow!

The closer we get to the danger, the sooner we'll put an end to it. We just have to stay on guard. — Wise caution is not contrary to valor.

LowHealth

When death draws near, you start to wonder — is it possible to reunite with your lost family, somewhere beyond life?
Octavia：我们失去的挚爱其实一直都和我们在一起，他们活在我们的心里，我们的悲伤中。他们永远与你同在，艾昆。
Those dear to us who we've lost — they're always with us, in our thoughts and in our grief. They'll always be there for you, Ekun.

Your losses were terrible, yet you remain strong. When I see this, my own grief and sorrow seems to lessen.
Jaethal：等你死后，你不会看到任何东西，也不会有任何感觉。相信我，我有经验。
When you die, you will see and feel nothing. Trust my experience.
Kalikke/Kanerah：我了解这些念头……但在你寿命未尽时，他们会对你说什么呢？‘快回去，等你变成个头发花白的老头子的时候再回来！’
I know these thoughts... But what could they say to you, if you met them before your time? 'Go back, and return only when you are a gray old man!'

Our supplies grow thin. We'll have to go hunting.

It would be so nice to teach your wolf to go hunting alone, and bring meat back to us! We could just sit here, and — here comes our lunch!

Oh, oh, can I play with wolfy while you're busy? He's so sweet — such a fluffy head, and that soft fur! I just love it when you summon him!

We should make sure it's safe. Something dangerous may be stalking us nearby.

We should look around first. Be sure the camp is secure.

Yeah, easy for you! You got that dog following you around, barking if anything is off. Too bad it don't talk.

We'd better stay together. — If someone attacks, we might be too far away to help.

Oh no. We'll do like all smart adventurers do. — We'll just sit here quietly, and let the adventures come to us!
Linzi：我承认，在这种一览无遗的地方扎营，我也稍微有点不安。不过这样一来，我就可以弹鲁特琴了——反正从远处也能看到篝火的光。
I admit, it worries me a little too, when we camp in the open for all to see. But then I can play the lute — and the light from our campfire can be seen from afar anyway.

NoHunting

My wife would cook venison stew like no one else in the world. And now my life is reduced to eating dry rations.

Once, hot homemade meals awaited me every time I returned home. Now I choke on dry rations.
Octavia：我知道你经历过很多困难，不过别这么丧气！没准你还会遇到一个真心在乎你的人！
I know you've been through a lot, but don't be so gloomy! You may yet meet that special someone who cares about you!
Amiri：你发起牢骚还没完了？赶紧吃完睡觉！

It feels strange to hear stories about such comforts. Home-made meals… Things familiar to everyone, but unknown to me.
Jaethal：我真受不了这些鳏夫。明明摘掉了奴隶的锁链，结果他还忧伤一辈子。
I can't stand widowers. Spare a slave his chains, and he will mourn them to the end of his life.
Kanerah：命运无常啊。一个人昨天可能还在家里过夜，今晚就得躺在肮脏的阴沟里，明天说不准还能进宫殿睡呢。
Fate is unpredictable. She who spent the night at home yesterday, may sleep in a dirty gutter tonight, and in a palace tomorrow.
Harrim：在我被放逐以后，我的人生陷入了低谷。我变成了一个可悲的酒鬼，用廉价的酒精来冲洗自己那无能为力的挫败感……讽刺的是，格罗图斯的教条却给我的人生带来了意义！
I sank to such lows after my exile. I was a pathetic sot, drowning my feelings of uselessness in cheap drinks... How ironic is it that the teaching of Groetus would bring sense to my life!
Harrim：末日终将来临。我的末日，我们的末日，这整个世界的末日……
The end is inevitable. My end, our end, the entire world's end...
Harrim：每个人都嘲笑我。有的人带着善意，有的人却充满轻蔑。但是谁会笑到最后呢？
Everyone laughs at me. Some do it kindly, some laugh with contempt. But who will laugh last?
Harrim：嗯……可能是他们吧。不过……等他们笑不出来的时候，他们就会知道我是对的！
Hmm... I suppose they will. But I – I will be proven right when all the laughter fades!
Harrim：哦，我的一枚纽扣掉了……所以就连最渺小的事情也预示着那终将到来的末日……
Oh, I lost a button... Thus the inevitable end of the world is seen even in littlest of things...
Harrim：要是能有瓶酒那可就太好了……两瓶更好……不过，不行。不不不，行军时不能喝酒！
A bottle of wine would be nice... Or maybe two... But no, no, not on a march!
Harrim：等我回到都城以后，我需要来杯啤酒。一杯就好。我不想举杯销愁愁更愁……
And when we get back to the capital, I better just have a mug of beer. Just one. Don't want to make things worse...
Kalikke&Kanerah
Harrim：人们是如此执着于毫无意义的俗世浮华……他们应该带着尊严、用短暂的人生来接受那末日的来临。
People are so busy with pointless worldly vanity... They'd be better off spending their short lives accepting the end with dignity!
Kalikke/Kanerah：[大笑] 尊敬的哈瑞姆，我觉得你那些关于死亡的梦，就跟孩子梦到长大后可以在晚饭前吃糖果差不多，都是些甜蜜又傻气的愿望——时间久了就忘了。
[laughs] I think, most honorable Harrim, that your dreams of death are like a child, dreaming of becoming an adult and having candy before dinner. Sweet, silly hopes — that pass with time.
Harrim：我是如此疲倦……有的时候我想要伸出手去抓住最后的一根救命稻草……不过那算是有尊严地死去吗？不。我觉得我应该要活下去，看看这个世界究竟会走向何方……
I'm so tired... Sometimes I feel like reaching for a rope... But is that a dignified end? No. I suppose I should live a little longer, and see where the world is rolling...
Kalikke/Kanerah：至少有东西能排解你的阴郁，尊敬的哈瑞姆。哪怕是在幻象中看到世界走向未知。
At least there's something that dispels your gloom, most honorable Harrim. Even if it's a vision of the world rolling into the unknown.
Harrim：我牙疼。我应该想办法治一治……不过，反正我也快要死了，这还有什么意义呢？
My tooth hurts. I should get it healed... Though what's the point if I'll die soon anyway?
Kalikke/Kanerah：尊敬的哈瑞姆，如果你治好你的牙疼，在死时你就不会被琐事烦恼了！牙疼可是件无比烦人的事儿，即便在临终前也一样……
If you heal your toothache, most honorable Harrim, you will be able to die without being distracted by trifles! Toothaches can be an awful distraction, even on one's deathbed...
Harrim：安米芮，我知道你一定明白死亡是在所难免的，而且你终究会去面对它……
Amiri, I know you must understand that death is inevitable, and eventually you will have to face it...
Amiri：你又要开始念叨你的破锣图斯了？呃啊啊……你看到那块石头了没有？快去用你的脑袋撞它！你不去我就去了啊！
Are you going to babble on about Broetus again? R-r-r... Do you see that rock? Go and hit your head on it. Or I will!
Harrim：你有好大一把剑……为什么你挥舞它就不会累呢，安米芮？
You have such a huge sword... How is it that you don't tire of swinging it, Amiri?
Amiri：你有好大一张嘴……你一直这么多话为什么也不会累呢，嗯，哈瑞姆？
You have such a busy tongue... How is it that you don't tire of wagging it, heh, Harrim?
Harrim：六熊部落信奉哪位神祇呢？
What god do the Six Bears venerate?
Amiri：那些男人们吗？当然是格鲁姆啦。因为他们都是伟大的战士，你懂的。至于女人嘛，她们都信奉我们的祖先，或是艾若斯提。他们不许我信奉格鲁姆，于是我摔了几个杯子，然后他们就不再来烦我了！
The men? Gorum, of course. Because they are all such great warriors, you know. As for the women, they worship either their ancestors or Erastil. They tried to stop me from praying to Gorum, but I smashed a couple of mugs and they left me be!
Harrim：酗酒和惹是生非……安米芮，你难道不明白这些行为毫无意义、非常荒唐吗？
Drinking and fighting... Amiri, don't you understand how empty and ridiculous all this is?
Amiri：你的脑袋才毫无意义！你的脸才非常荒唐！说真的，你整个人都无聊透了。爽快的战斗和美酒，人生如此，夫复何求呢？
Your head is empty! And your face is ridiculous. In fact, you are ridiculous all around. A good fight and some booze – what else do you need to be happy?
Harrim：你见到的死亡还不够吗，你造成了如此多的死亡，你难道没去想过你的下场是什么吗？
Haven't you seen enough death – been the cause of so much death – that you've thought about what awaits you?
Amiri：有什么好想的？活一天算一天，要是死了那就一了百了。我就能去参加格鲁姆的盛宴了！
What's there to think about? I'll live while I'm alive, and when I'm dead, it's the end. I'll go to Gorum's feast!
Harrim：居然有人信仰托拉格，那个背叛矮人的神祇……这岂不荒谬？
Isn't it ridiculous for people to worship Torag – the god who betrays dwarves?
Ekun：并不是的。真正荒谬的是去信仰毁灭和末日。
It's not. What's ridiculous is to worship destruction and ashes.
Harrim：托拉格是个徒劳与无事自扰的守护神。他教导信徒去铸造金属，但是却没有想过锈蚀最终会将金属化为砂砾……
Torag is the patron of pointless fuss. He teaches the forging of metal, without thinking of the rust that will eventually turn it to sand...
Ekun：然后一名新的铁匠会去铸造新的金属。托拉格会像帮助这个孩子的父辈一样去帮助他。
Then a new blacksmith will forge new metal. Torag will help the grandson the way he helped his grandfather.
Harrim：是什么力量在推动你？在我们当中，你一定最能理解生命的脆弱。
What drives you? You of all men must feel the frailty of life.
Ekun：只要有能让我为之活着的东西，我就会活着。那么你呢，祭司？又是什么让你不断前行的？
I live while there is something to live for. What about you, priest? What keeps you going?
Harrim：格罗图斯是非常有耐心的。他轻蔑地俯视着脚下这些可悲的人在小题大做,心中清楚最终的结果早已注定。这难道不是神圣智慧的体现吗？
Groetus is patient. He scornfully watches this pathetic fuss at his feet, knowing the outcome has already been decided. Isn't that a manifestation of divine wisdom?
Ekun：也许格罗图斯确实有耐心。我就没有这个耐心了。请不要再向我传教了。
Groetus may be patient. Me – not so much. Spare me your preaching.
Harrim：既然你崇敬托拉格，为什么不去信奉其他的矮人神祇呢？安格拉德？马格瑞姆？
If you worship Torag, why not honor the other dwarven gods? Angradd? Magrim?
Ekun：我尊敬一切非邪恶的神灵，即便是你的格罗图斯。你也应该这样。
I respect all gods who are not evil. Even your Groetus. You should do the same.
Harrim：你已经死了，但是还留恋这尘世的浮华。这有什么意义吗？不死生命或许可以延续更久的时候，不过最终也会消逝。
You died but you still cling to your worldly existence. Is there sense in this? The undead exist for a long time, but even they don't last forever.
Jaethal：看来你对于不死生物缺少了解啊。要真的说起来，我可是咱们这队人中间唯一有机会可以亲眼见证世界末日的人。
It looks like you don't know much about the undead. For what it's worth, I'm the only one among us who has a chance of witnessing the end of times.
Harrim：你摒弃了曾经的生活，背弃了你的同胞和神祇，并转投另一名神祇的怀抱……我们很像啊，耶萨尔。
To reject your former life, your people and their gods, and find yourself in service to another deity... We have much in common, Jaethal.
Jaethal：别给自己脸上贴金了。你以为自己只有个头比不上我吗，不，你什么都比不上我。
Don't flatter yourself. You are not my equal in anything, any more than you are equal to me in height.
Harrim：一个人要么就活着，要么就死了。可是你却像是卡在了两个世界之间。
Your body is neither dead nor alive. It's like you're stuck between two worlds.
Jaethal：这种事情让你很不安吗？世界末日的祭司也会害怕死亡？
Why does it bother you so much? Is the priest of the world's end still afraid of death?
Harrim：我经常在想人们为什么会执着于自己那虚妄的理想。对他们来说，熟悉的东西就是对的。新事物则会让他们感到惊恐。
I often wonder that people cling to their false ideals. Whatever is familiar seems right to them. Everything new frightens them.
Jubilost：我本来想赞同你的观点，但那样的话你又会开始滔滔不绝地讲你的格罗图斯。
I'd agree with you, but then you'd just launch into another speech about Groetus.
Harrim：你一直想要重新找回侏儒丢失的永恒生命，这难道不是很愚蠢吗？这世上没有什么是永恒的。活十年和活一百万年，这在末日面前真的有差别吗？总有一天，整个第一世界都会面临终结……
Isn't it folly, trying to recover the gnomes' lost immortality? Nothing is eternal. Is there truly a difference in the face of the oblivion – to live for ten years or a million? The whole First World will come to an end at some point...
Jubilost：世界会不会终结我可不知道，不过你这唠叨确实是永远不会终结。你这也可以算是一种活着的奇观。
I don't know about the end of the world, but your nagging is certainly endless. This makes you somewhat of a living phenomenon.
Harrim：你知道吗，朱比罗斯特，我觉得你的‘脱色’不是一种诅咒，而是一种祝福。你能活多久取决于这个世界能让你感兴趣多久，然后等你厌倦了，你就会死去。
You know, Jubilost, I believe your 'Bleaching' is not a curse but a gift. You live exactly as long as the world seems interesting to you, and then you leave when you tire of it.
Jubilost：你这个观点很奇特……我承认，我还从来没有从这个角度考虑过这个问题！虽然我可能不赞同你的观点，但是你确实抛出了一个很不错的议题，我可以好好思考一下了！
A curious observation... I admit, I haven't looked at this problem from such a viewpoint! I think I must disagree with you, but you've given me some food for thought!
Harrim：你信奉德丝娜，对吗？
You serve Desna, right?
Jubilost：不尽然……不过话说回来，你懂的，按照我这种生活方式，运气可是万万不可缺少的。有的时候我也会向伟大的梦者的神殿捐献，但是绝不会全身心投入！
Not quite... But still, you know, with my way of life you can't have too much luck. Sometimes I donate to a temple of the Great Dreamer. But I don't go overboard!
Harrim：你知道吗，朱比罗斯特，我觉得你可以成为一名伟大的祭司或者传教士。你的人生充满目的，而且你能言善辩……
You know, Jubilost, I think you would make a great priest and preacher. Your sense of purpose, and your eloquence...
Jubilost：祭司？我？呸！要让我日复一日地重复那种虚伪的说教，我宁愿一个星期没有事情做把自己无聊死！我也有可能会往里面夹带一些自己的观点，然后很快我就会因为宣扬异端邪说被他们剥夺神职！
A priest? Me? Pff! I'd die of boredom in a week, if I had to repeat the same cheerless sermons day after day! Or I'd add some new ideas, and in no time I'd be expelled from the priesthood for heresy!
Harrim：你为什么可以这么开心，还可以畅想未来？每当你在开怀大笑或安详地享受人生，在别的地方却有人正咽下最后一口气。
How can you be happy, and make plans for your future? Every moment you laugh or peacefully enjoy your life, someone somewhere else is breathing their final breath.
Jubilost：每当你开口说话，我想要过一个轻松夜晚的希望就落空了。你可千万别再来忽悠我了！
Every time you open your mouth, my hope for a relaxing evening fades. Please don't get all wound up.
Harrim：你用了这么长时间学习写作，还如此努力地研究艺术，但是他们却把你赶出了大学院。但尽管发生了这一切，你依然没有放弃。为什么？
You studied writing for such a long time, put so much effort in your art, and they threw you out of the Academy. Yet in spite of all this, you didn't give up. Why?
Linzi：可能是因为如果我真的放弃了，那么他们就实现了他们的目的……当然也有可能是因为没有写作的人生会超级无趣啊！嗯……不过说实话，这个问题我还没有认真想过。
Maybe because if I did give up, it would mean they got what they wanted... Or no — maybe because life without writing would be so boring! Hmm… To be honest, I never really thought about it.
Harrim：你为什么盯着我看？你是否在我身后看到了那终将到来的末日在虎视眈眈？
Why are you staring at me? Do you see the shadow of inevitable death behind my shoulder?
Linzi：不，我看到你的脸上被熏出了一个印子……看上去像一头独角兽！哈瑞姆，你是不是从来没有洗过脸啊？
No, I saw a spot of soot from the fire on your cheek – looks like a unicorn! Harrim, do you ever wash your face?
Harrim：琳翠，你一直在写啊写啊的……但是当你死了以后，人们就会忘记你，这有什么意义呢？
You write and you write, Linzi... But what's the point if you will be forgotten after you die?
Linzi：正因为如此我才要写作啊，这样人们就会永远记住我！我的故事将会流传千秋万世！是的，千秋万世……要是时间短了我可不接受！
That's why I'm writing, so I won't be forgotten! My stories will belong to the ages! Yes, the ages – I won't accept anything less!
Harrim：你在被赶出大学院之后，依然痴迷于写作，这真是奇怪。
It's strange that you held on to your love of storytelling after you were expelled from the Academy.
Linzi：一点也不奇怪！我写作是为了我自己和其他人……而不是为了学院里那些大腹便便的油腻中年！人们喜欢我的故事，有这点我就够了！
It's not strange at all! I write for myself and for other people – and not for those bloated toads at the Academy! People like my stories – and I couldn't ask for more!
Harrim：当我失去一切时，我选择向格罗图斯求助。你也失去了一切，琳翠。但是你为什么还要执着于过去的生活？
I turned to Groetus when I lost everything. You lost everything too, Linzi. Why do you keep clinging to your old life?
Linzi：您为什么这么问？我的双手萎缩了吗？我的眼珠掉出来了吗？也许有一天我会忘记所有的词汇，或者我的墨水瓶遭到了诅咒，每一次我打开盖子，里面的墨水都会凭空消失。但是只要这些事情还没发生，那我就会不停地写作，始终如一！为什么你会觉得我已经失去了一切呢？
Why do you ask? – Have my hands withered? Did my eyes fall out? Maybe I forgot all the letters, or got a cursed inkwell that evaporates whenever I reach for it? As long as none of that happens, I'll keep writing, just like always! Why do you think I've lost anything at all?
Harrim：对于你源源不绝的动力，我感到十分惊奇。你经历了这么多事情，却还能保持积极的性格，真是奇怪。
I'm amazed by your inexhaustible energy. It's seems strange you can be so joyful after everything that happened.
Linzi：而你也总是阴沉而且消极，哈瑞姆。笑一笑嘛！如果我们真的有一天会死去，我也希望在被埋掉的时候脸上还带着微笑，而不是犹豫地皱着眉头。
And you're always sullen and pouting, Harrim. Smile a little! If we do die at some point, I'd prefer to be buried with a smile on my face, not with a glum frown.
Harrim：似乎地精更应该去崇拜洛瓦古格。你们喜欢毁灭，总是不停在破坏一切。为什么你和你的部落会转而去祀奉怪兽之母呢？
Goblins seem as if they would do better to worship Rovagug. You adore destruction and smash everything you can. So why do you and your tribe follow the Mother of Monsters instead?
NokNok：我们喜欢去破坏其他东西。而我们自己造的东西……太好了，不能破坏。而且拉玛什图会赐给部落健康的宝宝，就算被关在笼子里也能自己活下来！
We like breaking others stuff. Things we built — too good to break. And Lamashtu gives tribe hardy babies — hardy enough to survive in their cages!
Harrim：我经常发现你用余光看着我，眼神中还带着恐惧。你不希望我成为你的同伴吗？
I often catch you looking at me sideways with fear in your eyes. You do not want me as a companion?
Octavia：不，哈瑞姆，你怎么能这么说呢！？没错，你的信仰确实让我很害怕。不过我还挺喜欢你这个人的。你和其他的牧师不一样，你不傲慢，而且你对这个世界总有一种独特的看法。和你一起旅行是非常有趣的。
No, Harrim, how can you say that!? Your faith frightens me, that's true. But I still like you personally. You're not arrogant — unlike many other clerics — and you have a peculiar view of the world. Traveling with you is always interesting.
Harrim：你承受的痛苦一点也不比我少。难道你还没有意识到这个世界有多么可悲、多么残酷？
You've endured no less suffering than I. Haven't you realized by now how pathetic and cruel the world is?
Octavia：这个世界的好坏是由我们自己决定的。只要我们努力，也可以把它变得更美好。所以不要抱怨了，开始努力！
It's as bad as we allow it to be. And it's as beautiful as we make it. So stop grumbling and get to work!
Harrim：就算这个世界上充满了痛苦，但是像你这样的朋友让我对它有了更多容忍。
This world might be full of suffering, but friends like you make me put up with it.
Octavia：谢谢你。我也很珍惜你这个朋友，尽管你每天都在发牢骚！
Thanks. I value you a lot too, no matter how much you grumble!
Harrim：我的外套本来都快穿破了，不过昨天晚上有人把它缝好了。是你吗？谢谢你，虽然我不值得你对我这么好。
My jacket was all torn up, but someone mended it neatly last night. Was it you? Thank you, although I am not worth such efforts.
Octavia：不客气！我知道这个世界迟早有一天会化为虚有，不过至少我可以让你的外套多穿一段时间！
You're welcome! I know the world may crumble to dust sooner or later, but meanwhile your jacket can serve you a bit longer!
Harrim：真可惜你无法听到格罗图斯的低语。我真希望你能够明白我说的话……
It's a pity you cannot hear Groetus' whisper. I would so like you to understand what I'm talking about...
Octavia：我懂你……真的！不过如果我也每天忙着听某个声音跟我说什么死亡之类的事，那谁来给你加油打气呢？
But I do understand you – I do! But if I was busy listening to some whisper telling me about death, then who would cheer you up?
Harrim：你被科技联盟奴役了那么长时间，那一定非常难以忍受。告诉我，你有没有想过要结束这种痛苦？
You had it tough, enslaved by the Technic League. Tell me, didn't you ever think about putting an end to all the suffering?
Regongar：你是说自刎吗？呸！弱者才这么做。绝对不可能，我更喜欢去砍掉别人的脑袋！
You mean cut my throat? Pff! That's for weaklings. Hell no, I prefer cutting other people's throats!
Harrim：瑞贡尕，我……
Regongar, I...
Regongar：哈瑞姆，你能不能闭嘴？！每一次你张嘴就如同滔滔江水连绵不绝。你还是去找别人唠叨吧！
Harrim, shut up, will you?! Every time you open your mouth, some screed comes tumbling out. Go whine to someone else!
Harrim：兽人的凶猛举世闻名。不过你，瑞贡尕……你只是个半兽人。但是你为什么心中充满兽性呢？
The fierceness of orcs is famous all over the world. But you, Regongar – you're only half-orc. How is it you have so much animal rage?
Regongar：虽然我天资不足，但是奴隶主的鞭子却在我的心中填满了愤怒！
What I lacked at birth, the slavers' lashes more than made up for!
Harrim：我不明白，你为什么喜欢用低劣的享乐来满足自己的肉体。这种愉快转瞬即逝，只会在你的嘴里留下苦涩的味道，让你头疼欲裂。有时间思考一下永恒不是更好吗？
I don't understand why you entertain your flesh with base pleasures. They fade quickly, leaving only a headache and a bad taste in your mouth. Wouldn't it be better to ponder the eternal?
Regongar：嘿，我想过永恒的事！只要那些祭司没有撒谎，那么我死了以后就会去极乐境，去和卡丽斯翠整日开怀畅饮！你以为我现在喝太多了，整天醉醺醺的？那你应该想象一下，等我到了欲望女神的宴会上会变成什么鬼样子，哈哈哈！
Hey, I think about eternity! Unless the priests are lying, I'll go to Elysium after I die – to an eternal feast with Calistria! You think I drink too much and sleep around? Imagine what goes on at the feast of the goddess of lust, ha ha ha!
Harrim：就算你可以找到你那些科技联盟的敌人复仇又能怎样？反正他们迟早都会死。
What does it matter if you bring vengeance to your enemies from the Technic League? They'll all die sooner or later anyhow.
Regongar：也许你不太在意你的敌人是以什么方式下地狱的，不过我在意。我更希望能尽快送他们下去，而且要亲手，而不是让他们躺在温柔乡里安详地死去！
You might not care about how your enemies are sent to the hereafter, but I do. I prefer it sooner, and by my hand – not later in a warm bed!
Harrim：你会逃脱科技联盟的追捕吗？是他们抓到你，还是你抓到他们……你和他们，谁会先死呢……这些事情在永恒面前都不过只是短暂的一瞬罢了，所以谁输谁赢又有什么关系呢？
Will you escape from the Technic League's agents? Will they catch you, or will you catch them... Will you die sooner or later than them... This is all happening in a moment before eternity – so what does it matter who wins?
Regongar：永恒的寂静和虚无又有什么价值呢？你所毫不关心的这段短暂的生命，这才是我所唯一拥有的东西！所以是的，我要如何度过一生，这确实很重要！
What is an eternity of dust and ashes worth? That short moment of life that you don't give a damn about – that's all I have! So yes, it does matter how I spend it!
Harrim：有的时候当我看到你战斗的样子，我会很羡慕。你就像是一团完全失控的混沌力量，带领着这个世界走向即将到来的末日。有的人死于火焰或风暴，而有的人却死在瑞贡尕的剑下……
Sometimes I admire you when I watch you fight. It's like you embody the uncontrolled power of chaos, leading the world to its imminent end. Some die from the fire or storm, while others from Regongar's hand...
Regongar：哈哈，你终于明白了！是啊，哥们儿，这就是我！
Ha ha, you got it! Yeah, man, I am what I am!'
Harrim：你嘲笑死亡，但总有一天你会面对它……
You laugh at death, but you will face it someday...
Regongar：不，不会的。你应该这么想。只要我还活着，我就不用面对死亡。而等我死了以后，我已经什么都不知道了，哈哈哈！
No I won't! Think about it. So long as I'm alive, there's no death. And then when it comes, I'll already be gone, ha ha!
Harrim：莎恩芮教导我们要仁慈。而死亡可以终结所有脆弱肉体和不安灵魂的苦难，这难道不就是最大的仁慈吗？
Sarenrae teaches mercy. But isn't death the greatest mercy, an end to the suffering of frail flesh and the restless mind?
Tristian：我不这么想。死亡可以让我们摆脱苦难，这确实没错……不过死亡也会剥夺我们赎罪的机会！
I don't think so. Death releases us from suffering, it's true... But it also takes away our chance to atone for our sins!
Harrim：有的时候我也会问我自己，格罗图斯为什么会赐给我治疗法术。去治疗那些终将死亡的人又有什么意义？
Sometimes I ask myself, why does Groetus give me healing spells. What's the point of healing those who are meant to die?
Tristian：要是照你这么说，反正这个世界终将毁灭，那么他为什么要给他的信徒赐予力量呢？所以即便是对于格罗图斯来说，这个世界总还是有一些重要性的，不是吗？
Why does he give any powers to his followers, if he's entirely impartial to them, as you say? Perhaps this world is important in some ways, even for Groetus?
Harrim：每个人都在各自拼搏着，渴望着一些东西，争取着一些东西……就像是没头的苍蝇一样，漫无目的地徒劳着。
Everyone is striving somewhere, wanting something, fighting for something... Like the silly fuss of little blind mice.
Tristian：那你不还是在和我们并肩作战！难道这是因为你不懂得你的神祇的教诲？还是说参与到俗世的事情里来也是有一定道理的……
But you fight beside us! Is this because you don't understand your own god's teachings? Or perhaps there's wisdom in participating in worldly matters...
Harrim：这个世界充满了污秽、愤怒和痛苦。等末日来临以后，大家都会变得更快乐。
This world is full of filth, anger, and suffering. When it's gone, everyone will feel better.
Tristian：你总是在胡说八道。要是这世界上都没人了，谁还能感到快乐？所以我们还是要为了那些活着的人努力把这个世界变得更美好吧！
You say strange things. Who is there to feel better, if no one exists? Let's instead try to make the world better for those who are still alive!
Harrim：即便是诸神也终将会消失。那么我们呢？在虚无面前，神灵与凡人同等……
Even the gods will disappear sooner or later. What about us? Oblivion even makes gods equal to mortals...
Tristian：莎恩芮并不惧怕虚无。就如同我并不惧怕死亡，尽管死亡无法避免。凡人在各个方面都以神灵为榜样，这就是信仰存在的意义。
Sarenrae does not fear non-existence. Nor am I afraid of death, inevitable as it is. Mortals imitate the gods in many ways – that's the point of faith.
Harrim：你又来找我了。又想要告诉我这个世界不会终结吗？又想说我无法在格罗图斯的教诲中得到安息吗？
So you came to me again. Another attempt to convince me that the world won't end? That I'll not find peace in the teachings of Groetus?
Tristian：我只是来给你送馅饼，午饭剩下的。慢慢吃，至于我们的辩论，改天再说。
I just brought you a piece of pie that remained after lunch. Enjoy, and we'll save the arguments for later.
Harrim：你对于雪琳很失望，就如同我对托拉格失望一样。你仔细听，也许你也可以听到一个微弱的声音……也许格罗图斯也会对你低语？
You are disappointed in Shelyn, as I am in Torag. Listen closely – maybe you, too, will hear a barely audible whisper... Perhaps Groetus will speak to you too?
Valerie：没有人在对我低语，哈瑞姆。如果我开始凭空听到声音，那么出问题的一定是我的精神，而不是我的信仰！
No one is talking to me, Harrim. And if I start hearing whispers, it means I have problems with my head, not with my faith!
Harrim：你很美，瓦尔瑞。要让这样的美丽逝去，我十分痛心。
You are very beautiful, Valerie. I will feel regret when such beauty dies.
Valerie：嗯……我听到过无数居心不良的奉承，也许你这个是最值得怀疑的。
Hmm... Of all the dubious compliments I've ever heard, this one is probably the most doubtful.
Harrim：我完全搞不懂你，很抱歉。你是如何毫无信仰地生活下去的？
I don't understand you, I'm sorry. How can you live without faith at all?
Valerie：你自己也说过，这个世界上的一切都毫无意义。你觉得信仰就可以例外吗？
You said yourself that everything in this world is pointless. Do you think faith is an exception?
Harrim：若不是我在最消沉的时候遇到了格罗图斯的信徒，也许我也会走上和你一样的道路。
Had I not met Groetus' followers in my darkest hour, I might have taken the same path as you.
Valerie：我并不认为这是一种道路。只不过我的生活中没有给神灵留下位置，仅此而已。而如果你真的对神灵很失望的话，那么你就不应该如此热心地帮格罗图斯布道，而是应该去宣扬无神论！
I don't think of it as a path. – The gods simply don't have a place in my life, that's all. And if you were disappointed in the gods, you would be preaching atheism as passionately as you preach the teaching of Groetus!
Harrim：瓦尔瑞，你的盔甲始终光鲜亮丽。你花太多时间来清理它。这有什么意义？又不能让你的盔甲变得更坚固。
Your armor always shines, Valerie. You spend so much time polishing it. But what's the point? Your armor doesn't get any tougher.
Valerie：而你也花了这么多时间来宣传死亡，你还不是照样活得好好的。要我说，你的时间浪费得更没有意义。
And you spend much time talking about death, even while we're still alive. I'd say you spend your time even less sensibly.
生日祝福
Harrim：对您来说，今天是个特别的日子，{主角名}。年长一岁，也离所有的名利与烦恼的终结更近一步……
Today is the special day for you, {name}. One year older, one step closer to the end of all the vanity and trouble…
Harrim：为您献上我最衷心的祝贺。您离坟场越来越近的念头一定会给您以宽慰……
You have my deepest congratulations. The thought that you approach the Boneyard must be a relief…
C11
Harrim：不要因为我总是不说自己的想法，就与我保持距离。来找我吧，我会和你讨论生命的脆弱，夜晚的耳语，伟大的神明……
Do not be set aback by how quietly I keep to my thoughts. Come to me and I will discuss with you the frailty of life, the whisper of the night, the great god...
Amiri：‘不说自己的想法’？你是指你跟块棺材板子一样僵硬的表现吗？不用了，多谢！
'Quietly keep your thoughts?' You mean how you act like a coffin stuffer? No thanks!
Tristian：当然，我会和你聊聊的。不过，我觉得我们的观点大概不会互相影响……但这并不意味着我们就不该尝试了，对吧？
Certainly, I'll talk to you. I don't think we'll influence each other's opinion... But that doesn't mean we shouldn't try, right?
Linzi：有关你那持续不断的忧愁，有些有趣的东西。如果你想的话，我会很乐意拉你一把。——毕竟，我需要好好了解你，这样才能在书里准确地描写你！
There's something funny about your incessant gloominess. I'll be happy to take you up on your offer. — After all, I need to understand you well, so I can depict you accurately in my book!
C11_TempleOfTheElk
Harrim：被遗弃的庙宇十分宁静、安详。格罗图斯的追随者并不建立自己的神庙，而是在其它神庙的废墟上聚会。
The deserted temple was peaceful. Serene. The followers of Groetus do not build their own shrines, but gather to worship on the ruins of the other gods.
Linzi：真是神奇的传统！但我想，这样一来，其它神袛的信徒大概就不怎么喜欢格罗图斯了吧……
What an amazing tradition! But I imagine that makes Groetus unpopular among the followers of other gods...
Amiri：这个鬼鬼祟祟的神一直等到其它的神袛都离开了老房子，然后他自己搬了进去，统治他们。哈！
The rat god waits until other gods leave their old houses. Then he moves in and rules them himself. Ha!
Regongar：‘我很喜欢这座废弃神殿的外表，因为里面可真是无聊死了！’——啊，我倒一点都不惊讶，哈哈哈！
'The look of the deserted temple pleased me, because I feel like dying of boredom in it!' — Well, I'm not surprised at all by that, ha, ha, ha!
C21_FromMiddle
Harrim：皈依吧！接受无可避免的事实吧！向格罗图斯打开你的灵魂，放弃希望的假象，接受我的信仰所承诺的绝望！
Submit! Submit to the inevitable! Open your souls to Groetus, renounce the illusion of hope, and embrace the despair my faith promises!
Valerie：当我还在侍奉雪琳的时候，圣典禁止我在战斗中先出击。幸运的是，我已经不再侍奉雪琳了。哈瑞姆，在你再次向我宣讲你那沉闷的布道之前，记得这一点。
When I still served Shelyn, my codex prohibited me from attacking first. Fortunately, I serve Shelyn no longer. Think on this, Harrim, before you preach your depressing sermons to me again.
Ekun：万事万物必将灭亡，你似乎对此很高兴。你真是让我打心眼里恶心。
You seem happy that everything and everyone must die. You disgust my soul.
Tristian：只要有生命，就有希望。只要我们还活着，就一定要抱有最好的希望，永不放弃——为了我们自己，也为了我们所珍视的一切。
Wherever there is life, there is hope. So long as we yet live, we must hope for the best and never give up — for ourselves, and for those we hold dear.
Kalikke/Kanerah：尊敬的哈瑞姆，你这么拼命地想要说服我们。我都差不多快被说服了——或者至少，快要假装一下了。
You try so desperately to convince us, most honorable Harrim. I'm on the verge of being convinced – or at least, playing along.
C21_TrollsNotAfraid
Harrim：巨魔不再怕火了。即使有了魔法的帮助，改变一个人的本质仍然需要勇气。
Trolls stopped fearing fire. It takes courage to change one's essence, even with the aid of magic.
Tristian：也许假以时日，他们还会有更多改变呢，说不定，他们甚至能学会与其它种族和平共处。
Perhaps in time they could change yet more. They might even learn to coexist in peace with the other races.
Amiri：我喜欢先砍掉他们的头，再想之后的事。
I prefer to cut off their heads first, and think about the rest later.
Ekun：人是不会改变的，动物也不会。
People don't change. Animals don't either.
C22_AfterCapitalAttack
Harrim：混乱、疯狂、血。神的意志传递的方式还真奇怪。
Chaos, insanity, blood. The will of the gods comes to pass in strange ways.
Jubilost：听起来你有点不高兴。格罗图斯所教谕的是缓慢、悄默无声的腐朽，而我们面前的却是一场血腥的骚乱！
Sounds like you're unhappy about it. The teaching of Groetus is all slow and quiet decay, whereas what we have here is bloody pandemonium!
Valerie：不管这是不是诸神的意愿，我们都要守护城市，阻止开花。
The gods' will or not, we defended the city and put an end to this Bloom.
Jaethal：神是残酷的。但拥有了这样的力量，谁不会变得残酷呢？
The gods are cruel. But who wouldn't be, possessing such power?
C22_OldDwarfCastle
Harrim：如果可以的话，我想再次回到那座被遗弃的要塞。在拱门下面走一走……感受大厅和拱廊中的安宁……倾听寂静……
If we may, I would like to return once more to that forsaken fortress. To walk below its arches... to feel the tranquility of its halls and arcades... to listen to the silence...
Octavia：你想去就去吧，不过没必要非带上我不可。灰尘，废墟……呕！
Do as you wish, but you can do it without me. Dirt, ruins... Ughhh!
Valerie：我发现你对安宁和寂静的定义真奇怪。对于我来说，我帐篷里的安宁就很让我满足了。
I find your notions of tranquility and silence rather strange. For me, I'm content with the calmness of my tent.
Tristian：这也许对你有好处。待在那样的地方，你眉间的忧愁似乎都减轻了……
It might do you good. Such places seem to lift the gloom from your brow...
C31_CyclopusTomb
Harrim：独眼巨人……一个伟大的帝国，强大的力量，这么多的生命，现在都消失了——只剩下碑石和坟墓。我在其中看见了格罗图斯的意志。
Cyclopes... A great empire, great power, so many lives. Now, all gone — and all that remains are tombs and graves. I see the hand of Groetus in this.
Amiri：一定是因为他们太弱了，所以才都死绝了。如果你想生存，就必须和一切斗争，不管是什么。
They must have been weaklings, so they all died out. If you want to survive, you must fight no matter what.
Octavia：但我们还在这里，在他们离世许久之后。生命，向善的挣扎——这一切终将转圜，最后战胜死亡与邪恶。
But here we are, long after they're gone. Life, and the struggle for good — this will always return and prevail in the end.
Tristian：他们变得冷酷无情，被他们最黑暗的情感所淹没——然后死去。所有陷入疯狂和邪恶中的人，都会面临相同的命运。
They grew callous, overwhelmed by their darkest passions — and died. Thus is the fate of all who sink into insanity and evil.
NokNok：那些死了的臭肉有什么好的？地精才好呢！——你想杀就来杀我们啊，我们会潜行，隐藏，活下来！
What's so great about some dead meat? Goblins are great! — You can kill us all you want. We will sneak, hide and survive!
C31_FirstWorldFeedback
Harrim：一个没有凋敝、没有悲哀的世界。一个格罗图斯不曾染指的世界！我们所见的一切真是震撼人心。
A whole world free of wilting and sorrow. A world untouched by Groetus! What we have seen is truly impressive.
Jubilost：他觉得印象深刻！简直就像是你为你的部族史上最伟大的事件找到了个目击者，结果你从他的口中得到的不过是些乱七八糟的胡言乱语！
He's impressed! As if you'd found an eye-witness to the greatest event in the history of your people, and all you managed to get out of him was some disjointed gibberish!
Ekun：希望你找到了思考的素材，而不是散播你那黑暗思想的又一处田地。
Hope you found food for thought, and not another field to sow your dark ideas.
Jaethal：真是个惊人的地方……还有同样惊人的居民。好了，等我感觉这里的一切都无聊透顶之后，我就知道我要去哪里了。
An amazing place... and equally amazing inhabitants. Well, now I know where I'll set off to, once I've grown hopelessly bored of everything here.
C41
Harrim：沃达凯躺进了坟墓——一处充满了平静与沉思的所在——跟他的激情和毫无意义的虚荣一起。我觉得……有些不安。
Vordakai filled a sepulcher – a place of peace and contemplation – with passion and pointless vanity. I found it… disturbing.

For once we think alike — Vordakai got what he deserved!

Well, it's his sepulcher. Anyone is free to do whatever they wish in their own house. – Even if they're dead!
C41_AfterTristiansReturn
Harrim：莎恩芮忠诚的仆人被抓住了——他的女神任他落入命运的摆布。光之神真的像祭司所说的那样仁慈吗？还是她也拒绝了他，就像托拉格拒绝了我一样？
Sarenrae's loyal servant was captured — and the goddess left him to the mercy of fate. Are gods of light as kind as their priests say? Or did she reject him as Torag rejected me.
Ekun：钟爱孩子的父母不会帮孩子解决所有的问题，而是教孩子怎么自己解决问题。这就是托拉格想做的。而因为这一点，你拒绝了他。并不是他拒绝了你。
Loving parents don't solve all their children's problems. They teach them to do it themselves. That is what Torag wanted, and for that, you rejected him. Not the other way around.
Regongar：哈！有新发现了。
Ha! Some discovery.
Octavia：我不明白。如果他去侍奉某些神秘的黑暗力量，莎恩芮为什么不剥夺他的魔法呢？
I don't understand. If he served some mysterious dark power, why didn't Sarenrae take away his magic?

C51

To forge a spirit into a sword! The things mortals do to prolong their useless struggle to remain in this sick world...

Armag's was a sad fate, you're right. But how does that prove we should all just give up and accept the inevitability of the end?

Rulers make war, fight for their lands and subjects, only to realize much later that their hands can grasp nothing but ashes.

DeadInParty
Harrim：我们都不过是尘埃，只是还没获得永恒的安息。只要记住这一点，就没有任何悲剧可以动摇你的冷静。
We are all just ashes and dust that hasn't yet attained eternal rest. Remember this, and no tragedy can shatter your calmness.
Harrim：死亡不可避免。一切事物都终将消亡。为什么要为注定的命运悲伤呢？为什么要逃离那不可逃离的结局呢？接受死亡吧，你将获得真正的平静。
Death is inevitable. All that exists must perish. Why mourn that which is already written? Why ponder ways to fend off the inevitable? Accept death for what it is, and you will gain true serenity.
Valerie：你的话……让人安慰。真不可思议。
Your words sound… comforting. Strangely enough.
Ekun：不要再说了。没人能真正准备好接受死亡，无论是自己的死亡，还是别人的。
Say no more. No one can truly prepare for death. Neither one's own nor another's.
Amiri：‘你们全都去死吧！’——你是不是这个意思？！
'Fall down and die, all of you!' — Is that what you're trying to say?!
Tristian：不，哈瑞姆，生命永远会战胜死亡，没有例外。在这样的时刻，尤其要记住这一条真理！
No, Harrim. Life always defeats death — time after time, again and again. It is especially important to remember that truth in times like this!
NokNok：你又来了！你话好多！别说了！快停！诺克诺克要睡着了。
You again! With all your extra words! Stop! Now! Nok-Nok will sleep instead.

We have a saying: 'it's better to be rich and healthy than poor and sick'. But your faith seems to teach otherwise, doesn't it?
Debuffed
Harrim：我感受到了格罗图斯的抚摸。
I feel the touch of Groetus.

I feel the breath of the Great God. He is near. He is here!

I just itch to yell, Leave our friend alone, you graverot! — But you're the one who keeps calling him, dwarf.
Linzi：你应该休息一下，哈瑞姆。你看起来气色不太好！
You should rest, Harrim. You don't look well!
Kanerah：你总是在祈求苦难。等到真的大难临头时，你会不会惊讶呢？
You ask for trouble all the time. Should you be surprised when it finally arrives?
Fatigued

I need rest.
Harrim：我需要睡觉。
I must sleep.
Octavia：也许睡一觉可以让你心情好一点？
Maybe some sleep will cheer you up a little?

Find a good dream, and draw some strength from it. There is much to do tomorrow.

Some day death will find us all. But so what? Life's cruelty is neither a divine revelation nor cause to lament.

Kalikke&Kanerah
Jaethal：享乐就是生命的意义。短命的种族会拥有雄心壮志，打造自己的事业，争夺权力……就好像他们有足够的时间来实现这些目的似的！
Enjoyment is the essence of life. The short-lived make plans, build careers, strive for power – as if they have the time for this!
Jaethal：如果我的生命也如此短暂，那么除了享乐我什么都不会去考虑！
If my life were so terrifyingly short, I would think of nothing but pleasure!

And how do you think we should spend our short lives? With no plans, no struggle for a better lot, no dreams? Ha!

Yeah, that's right! I wanted to be a fighter, and I did! And what did you want to be? A rotten fish with bulgy eyes? Well, congratulations!
