If you lunge into the thick of every battle, why don't you wear armor? At least we could bury you in one piece that way.

Here we go, now the dead one is planning on burying me. Not before I bury you!

I wonder, is it the constant cold that makes northern barbarian brains work so slowly? – Or is it that the tribes are so secluded that they've become incestuous?
Amiri：我也在想。我们的老人一到九十岁就开始犯糊涂，流口水。不过你们这些精灵却可以活好几百年。在这么漫长的岁月里，你们的脑子里会积攒多少愚蠢的想法呢？
I wonder too. Our old ones start drooling around ninety. But you elves live for hundreds of years. How much idiocy do you save up in your heads over the ages?
Jaethal：你不应该为你的家人感到悲伤。家庭的束缚只是为凡人设下的陷阱，是挂在自己脖子上的镣铐，是为了把你变成陌生人的奴隶。
You shouldn't grieve for your family. The bonds of family is a trap set for fools, a yoke they hang on their necks, to make you slave to, essentially, strangers.

You know nothing about a family, abomination. You never had one.

You know, Ekun, when I gutted my family, I felt great, unparalleled pleasure. It was so wonderful... I wish I could feel that bliss again!
Ekun：你是想要惹怒我吗？我可以不听你的胡言乱语，我也不会让你如愿以偿的。
Are you trying to insult me? I'm not listening. I won't give you the pleasure.

Urgathoa's gift could return your family to you... Although they probably look terrible by now. But what wouldn't I do to help a friend?
Ekun：你居然厚颜无耻说自己是我的朋友。连一座墓碑都比你更懂得什么叫友谊。
Don't you dare call me a friend. A gravestone knows more of friendship than you.
Jaethal：你实在是太无聊了。 你不会生活，你只是存在在这个世界上，就像是路上的一颗石子。即便我现在这个鬼样子都比你要更有生命力。我享受着自己存在于这世上的每一刻！
You are incredibly boring. You don't live, you merely exist. Like a pebble on the road. Even now, I am more alive than you. I enjoy every moment of my existence!
Ekun：这个世界很不公平。否则你就会对自己存在于世的每一秒钟都感到无比痛苦。
The world is unfair. Otherwise you'd suffer every moment of your existence.
Jaethal：你想不想和你的家人见一面？我可以帮你把他们的灵魂召回来。
Would you like to talk to your family? I could call their souls, as a favor.
Ekun：我可不想和你扯上关系。就连狼都看得出来，你的肉都是腐肉。
I won't deal with you. Even the wolf sees your meat is carrion.

When you do die – and it will happen sooner or later – I'll probably make a roast of your doggy. For aesthetic reasons.

A dwarf and an elf, exiles who've rejected the gods of their people, fighting side by side... How strange! You're like a reflection in a distorting mirror.
Harrim：可惜你的女神没有教会你要谦逊一些。我可不是别人的映像。我是我。你活了这么长时间，也应该明白了，这个世界不是围着你转的。
Too bad your goddess doesn't teach you humility. I'm not anyone's reflection. I am me. And you might have realized over your long life that the world doesn't exist for you.
Jaethal：告诉我，哈瑞姆，你想要看到世界的终结吗？如果你信仰乌伽娑婀，也许你就可以获得这样的机会。
Say, Harrim – wouldn't you like to see the end of times? Serving Urgathoa could offer such an opportunity.
Harrim：无论有没有我，世界都会走向终结。为什么我要用不死生命的方式来延长我的痛苦，加倍我的哀痛呢？
The end of times will come with or without me. Why multiply my grief, and prolonging suffering in unlife?
Jaethal：你所说的世界末日会是什么样子呢，哈瑞姆？反正你是很难亲眼见到那一天了，而我却想要知道该如何做好准备。
What will your end of times look like, Harrim? You'll hardly live to see it, and I'd like to know what to prepare for.
Harrim：你的话语里充满了傲慢和自大……你想要永生不死吗？格罗图斯会嘲笑你的想法，因为你的生命可能在下一天、下一个小时、甚至下一瞬间就会转瞬即逝……
So much arrogance in your words, so much smugness... Do you wish to live forever? Groetus laughs at your plans – for they might be cut short in a day, in an hour, or the very next moment…
Jaethal：我不明白，为什么你每天都要为去死做准备。享受生活吧，哈瑞姆！等你死了以后就没有机会了。
I don't understand, what's the point of preparing to die every day. Enjoy life, Harrim! None of it will exist after you die.
Harrim：我可不需要用廉价的愉悦来让自己感到生命的价值。再说了，能够明白每一条道路都有尽头，这本身也是一种乐趣。
I don't need cheap pleasure to convince me that my life has value. Besides, it's a pleasure to know that every path has an end.
Jaethal：你的宿命论想法还没有害死你，这可奇了怪了。
It's odd that your fatalism hasn't killed you yet.
Harrim：为什么会害我丧命呢？每个人都以为我一心求死……不过你仔细想想，我何必去努力实现那本就注定发生的事呢？
And why should it? Everyone thinks I strive for death... But think about it – why would I force the inevitable?
Jaethal：我们之间没什么好谈的。我们完全不一样。你觉得生命没有价值，而我则沉醉于其中。
We have nothing to talk about. We hold nothing in common. You see no value in life — while I am intoxicated by it.
Harrim：真是神奇。无论你的人生有多么可悲，你依然紧紧抓住它不愿放手。你以为你拥有自由的灵魂，但是你只是个奴隶罢了，因为惧怕真正的自由而将镣铐视若珍宝。
Amazing. However miserable your being is, you still cling to it. You think you are a free spirit, but you are merely a slave, cherishing her chains for fear of true freedom.
Jaethal：朱比罗斯特，你去过那么多地方。你有没有在旅途中遇到过不死生物？
Jubilost, you've traveled quite a lot. Haven't you ever met an undead on your journeys?
Jubilost：像你这样的……从没有过。正因如此，耶萨尔，我才会认为你拥有着如此高的学术价值！
One like you – never. That's why you, Jaethal, are of such serious academic interest to me!
Jaethal：你知道吗，朱比罗斯特，我觉得我可以想办法解决你的脱色问题。
You know, Jubilost, I think I know a way to solve your problem with the Bleaching.
Jubilost：把我也变成不死生物？呸！‘朱比罗斯特·纳斯罗泊，僵尸学教授和阴间科学博士！’我会没脸待在安布萨朗的！
By turning me undead? Pff! 'Jubilost Narthropple, professor of zombology and doctor of otherworldly science!' They'd laugh me out of Absalom.
Jaethal：看来你真是去过不少地方……但是你去过琼宁吗？
So you traveled a lot... But have you ever been to Kyonin?
Jubilost：精灵的土地？当然！我甚至还在他们的都城爱雅达拉受到过盛大的欢迎呢！
In the land of elves? Of course! I've even had the honor of attending a reception at the capital, Iadara!
Jaethal：我很喜欢你的辛辣批判。至少我的同伴里还有一个明白人，知道只有弱者和蠢货才会喜欢空泛的夸赞和毫无意义的礼仪。
I like your acridity. At least one of my companions understands that empty praise and pointless politeness are only for weaklings and fools.
Jubilost：我们终于能够在这一点上达成一致了，我很欣慰啊。
I'm glad we've come to an understanding at least on that matter.
Jaethal：当你取笑比你更强、更危险的人的时候，无疑是在玩火自焚。
You walk on thin ice, when you make jokes at someone who is stronger and more dangerous than you.
Jubilost：真是非常珍贵的社交礼仪忠告！特别是这话是从一个被无数刺客追杀的人嘴里说出来，尤其令人开心。为了能得到你的礼仪教学，人们真是无所不用其极啊。
Quite valuable advice on how to behave in society! Especially pleasant to hear from someone who is being hunted by an army of assassins. – The lengths people will go to, for an etiquette lesson from you.
Jaethal：你话太多了，就像是沉迷于自己的声音一样。不过这只是一种掩饰，一种自欺欺人的手段……因为在你内心深处，你极度害怕自己和其他人一样无聊、毫不重要。
You talk so much, intoxicated by the sound of your own voice. But this is merely a mask, an attempt to deceive even yourself... For deep inside, you greatly fear that you are as boring and insignificant as everyone else.
Jubilost：你什么时候又变成这方面的专家了？接下来你还要说些什么，要聊聊我和我父亲的关系？
And who made you the expert? What's next, you ask me about my relationship with my father?
Jaethal：琳翠，你想不想听一听我人生中发生的一些故事？这些故事一定会成为非常优秀的素材……
Would you like me to tell you a couple of tales from my life, Linzi? They'll make your stories much more interesting...
Linzi：听了你的故事，所有当母亲的人都会未老先衰，而她们的孩子长大以后也会变得口吃。
After stories like yours, mothers will turn gray before their time, and their children will grow up with stammers.
Jaethal：我在想，当农民们见到一名半身人不死者时，他们会产生什么样的情绪，是恐惧还是会觉得可笑呢？
I wonder what feeling an undead halfling would evoke from the peasants – fear or laughter?
Linzi：我觉得一定是恶心。但是我可不想要做这种实验。
I think disgust. But I don't feel like testing the theory.
Jaethal：为什么短命的种族都这么喜欢书？难道你们就不能和同胞多聊一聊你们感兴趣的话题吗？
Why do the short-lived like books so much? Can't you just speak with your peers about what interests you?
Linzi：是这样，我们没有好几百年的寿命去坐在树荫下思考那些华而不实的事情，然后懒洋洋地互相争论。所以我必须要把我的想法诉诸笔尖。
Well, we don't have hundreds of years to think about lofty matters under the shady trees, bickering with each other lazily. So I have to commit my thoughts to paper.
Jaethal：琳翠，我最后告诉你一次，不要再来观察我是在睡觉还是在冥想。我已经跟你说过无数次了，我不需要睡觉。
Linzi, I'm telling you for the last time – stop checking whether I'm sleeping or meditating. I've told you a hundred times I don't need sleep.
Linzi：好吧，好吧……我只是好奇，你会不会有一天在冥想的时候打呼噜！
Fine, fine... I'm just curious whether you'd snore someday during meditation!
Jaethal：琳翠，你死了以后，我会想你的。要不要把你也变成不死生物呢？
When you die, I will miss you, Linzi. Maybe I should make an undead of you?
Linzi：耶萨尔，你是被哈瑞姆咬了还是怎么的？首先我还没那么快死，而且我丝毫也不想要变成不死生物。
Jaethal, did Harrim bite you or something? I'm not going to die. And I don't feel the slightest desire to become undead.
Jaethal：你只是在浪费纸张。有谁会相信你这种人说的话？
You are just wasting paper. Who would believe a word from someone like you?
Linzi：有人会的！也许人们会更愿意相信一大群永生不死的精灵历史学家，不过我们这个世界里并没有。所以为了世人我还是得笔耕不辍啊。
Someone will! They'd probably put more faith in an army of immortal elven chroniclers, but I don't see any here. So I'll just have to suffer for all of them!
Jaethal：谁能想到我，一名原琼宁法官，会不得不和这个东西成为队友。
Who would have thought that I, the former Kyonin judge, will be forced to share company with this… thing.
NokNok：是啊，你适应适应吧。不过{主角名}还没那么糟糕，完全可以忍受。
Yes, need to get used to. But {name} is not that bad actually, quite bearable.
Jaethal：诺克诺克，如果你真的喜欢到处瞪眼，那你最好是去瞪别人，否则我就把你的眼睛给挖出来。
Nok-Nok, if you're going to stare, stare in another direction or I'll pluck out your eyes.
NokNok：诺克诺克也控制不住，第一次看会走路的尸体，还会骂人。以前只见过活人骂人。
Nok-Nok can't help it, first time see corpse walking around and insulting him. Usually only living do it.
Jaethal：你又化妆了吗？可圈可点！当然了，不是每个人都天生丽质，不过至少你还算有追求。
Are you putting on makeup again? Commendable. Of course, not everyone has natural beauty, but at least you try.
Octavia：你有什么资格谈论美丽？也许你在活着的时候拥有过美丽的外表，不过现在你只能用你的脸来吓人！
Look who is talking about beauty! You might have been pretty while you were alive, but now you're only good for scaring crows!
Jaethal：你拥有一张美丽的面容。我要不要把你变成僵尸呢？只不过那样的话你也不会获得爱慕，只会获得耻笑。
You have a pretty face. Should I make a zombie out of you? Though you'd cause more laughter than admiration.
Octavia：我觉得活着和朋友们欢声笑语挺好。但是你又能让他人有什么感觉？顶多是恶心。
I like being alive and laughing with my friends! But what feelings do you cause? Retching at best!
Jaethal：你的母亲是人类，对吗？看来你的父亲一定是非常英俊。只可惜他选择玷污自己的血统，如果你的母亲是一名精灵，那么你一定会是个大美人。
You mother was human, right? So your father must have been very handsome. Too bad he decided to dilute his blood – with an elven mother you might have been a beauty.
Octavia：我的家关你什么事？你杀了你的家人，现在又来对别人的家人品头论足。
What do you care about my family? You got yours killed, and now you gossip about others?
Jaethal：你战斗的本领还不错。继续努力，也许你就可以赢得我的尊重。
You fight pretty well. Keep at it, and you might earn my respect.
Octavia：说得好像我需要你尊重似的！我对尸蛆都比对你更尊重。说到这里，你身上怎么没生蛆？
Like I need it! I have more respect for grave worms than for you. Speaking of which, I think they're calling you!
Jaethal：看着你努力让自己变得更美，真是有趣。谁会留意你这张涂脂抹粉的脸呢？半兽人……还是半巨魔？
It's so amusing how you try to seem beautiful. Who's going to set their eyes on your little painted face? Maybe some half-orc – or a half-troll.
Octavia：你知道吗，如果有人杀了你，我会找到那个人，和他热情地握手！
You know, whoever killed you, I'd like to find him and shake his hand!
Jaethal：你曾经经历过奴隶的生活，对吗？这就对了。你们这种人就是需要在服从命令的生活中接受教育。挨上几鞭子就可以让你们明白事理。
You used to wear slave's shackles, didn't you? That's right. Your sort always benefits from lessons in obedience. With a few lashes to drive the point home.
Octavia：哼，我学习到的教训深刻得很呐！你根本不明白一个在鞭子下承受痛苦的人会对挥鞭的人有多么深刻的了解！不过不要搞错了，我会通过我的考验。
Oh, I learned my lessons well. You've no idea how much the one under a whip learns about the one who holds its handle! Make no mistake, I will pass my test.
Jaethal：真是讽刺啊。在我们这些人里，只有死人可以真正地评判生命。
How ironic. Of all of us, only the dead can truly judge life.
Octavia：你这也叫生命？
You call this life?
Kalikke：没有牵绊和爱添光增彩的生命是无滋无味的——是腐烂的味道也说不定。
A life not brightened by attachment and love — such a life has no taste. A taste of decay, perhaps.
Kanerah：当你在精灵都城纸醉金迷时，你对生命的评判还说得过去。现在你不过是在安慰自己罢了。
You judged life well enough, while you lived in luxury in the elven capital. Now you're just comforting yourself.
Jaethal：告诉我，混血儿，你觉得你会不会成为一个超越你的祖辈，而不像他们一样仅仅当个愤怒的莽夫？
Tell me, halfbreed, do you think you could ever become more than a raging brute like your ancestors?
Regongar：至少我还是个活人，而你……你这巫妖！
At least I'm still alive, you... lich!
Jaethal：告诉我，瑞贡尕，奥克塔维雅这样拥有高贵精灵血统的人怎么能忍受和……
Tell me, Regongar, how does Octavia, with her noble elven blood, put up with...
Regongar：再多说一个字，我就把你的脑袋拧下来！
Another word, and I'll rip your head off!
Jaethal：你对于人生的看法非常正确。你对于人生予取予求。享受每一刻！因为也许有一天你也会感受到乌伽娑婀那冰冷的触摸。
You have the right view on life. Take whatever you can from it! Enjoy every moment! Perhaps sooner or later you, too, will be touched by Urgathoa's hand?
Regongar：卡丽斯翠会把我从那样的享乐中拯救出来的！嘿，大家听我说，听到了吗？如果我死了，把我的尸体烧成灰，然后把灰撒掉！这样不死生物就没法利用我了！
Calistria save me from that sort of pleasure! Hey, all of you – you hear? If I die, burn my corpse and scatter the ashes! So that none of the undeads get me!
Jaethal：告诉我，你的纹身和你在战斗中施放的闪电有关系吗？还是只是你在无端玷污自己的身体发肤？
Tell me, are your tattoos somehow related to the lightning you cast in battle? Or are they just pointless marring of your skin?
Regongar：关你什么事？如果你再问这么多问题，我就给你留下一个同样的印记！
Why do you care? If you ask too many questions, I'll give you a brand to match!
Jaethal：瑞贡尕，为什么每次我一靠近你就这么紧张？你怕我会咬你吗？
Regongar, why do you get so tense when I come close? Are you afraid I'll bite you?
Regongar：我是害怕你会插手我的事，然后教训我该如何过活。况且我现在还不能杀掉你……不过有的时候我真的、真的想要杀了你！
I'm afraid you'll stick your nose in my business and try to teach me how to live. And I can't kill you yet... Even though sometimes I really, really want to!
Jaethal：和短命种族结伴前行真的很令人疲累。你们经常打扰我而不自知！
The company of the short-lived is very tiresome. You annoy me more than you know!
Regongar：和高贵的恋尸癖成为伙伴也很令人头疼啊！连打扰我都不想打扰！
As opposed to the company of noble corpse lovers! Nothing annoying there at all!
Kalikke/Kanerah：噢，再说一次吧。你的悲叹在我听来就是天籁之音！
Oh, say it again. Your sobbing is music to my ears!
Jaethal：你可以成为一名很优秀的仆人。你强壮而且充满愤怒。我要不要向你展示一下乌伽娑婀的力量呢？她的魔法比你的要致命多了。
You would make a good servant. Strong, and angry. Should I reveal the gifts of Urgathoa to you? Her magic is far deadlier than yours.
Regongar：没有用的，你这团腐肉，我已经投入了卡丽斯翠的怀抱。你觉得她肯放开我这种相貌英俊的小伙子吗？哈！为了我，她会去揪你家乌伽娑婀的头发的！
Won't work, rotten meat, I already pray to Calistria. You think she'd let a handsome boy like me go? Ha! She'll scratch your Urgathoa's face for me!
Jaethal：你已经见到了这个世界的残酷真相。你明白只有强者可以得到最丰盛的美食，而弱者只能噤口不言。你并不像其他人一样对这一切视而不见……那么你还在等什么呢？你为什么不去争取属于自己的一切？
The cruel truth of this world has revealed itself to you. You know that the strong get the most succulent cuts, and the weak had best keep their silence. You are not blind, like the others... So what holds you back? Why don't you seize what is yours?
Regongar：不关你的事，死尸。要是让我知道你插手我的事，全世界的神灵加在一起也救不了你。
That's none of your concern, corpse. If I catch word that you're meddling in my business, all the gods in the world won't be able to dig you up.
Jaethal：你懂的，崔斯提安，我们之间有很多相似之处。你常说每个人都有赎罪的机会。死亡之后的生命不也是这样的机会吗？
You know, Tristian, we have much more in common than you think. You say everyone deserves a chance to atone. But isn't life after death such a chance?
Tristian：也许吧。如果真是这样，那么我希望你可以利用这个机会为自己赎罪。
Perhaps. If so, I pray that you do use this chance to atone at last.
Jaethal：你一定觉得自己是个圣人。你对于自己的言行和礼节如此一丝不苟，实在是荒唐。难道你的人生中就没有别的东西了吗？
You must think yourself a saint. It's ridiculous how meticulous you are about your vows and observations. Is there nothing else in your life?
Tristian：没错，耶萨尔，我的人生是为了提莎恩芮效力，还有那些需要我帮助的人。你活着是为了什么？或者更准确的说，你为什么还要延续自己的不死生命？
Yes, Jaethal, I live to serve Sarenrae, and those who need my help. And what do you live for? Or better yet, why do you persist in your unlife?
Jaethal：你难道不明白吗？你无法治完这世上所有的病人，也不可能宽慰所有的痛苦？尝试一下享受人生吧，而不是像肩上扛着千斤重担一样匍匐前行。
Don't you understand you can never heal all the sick or console all the afflicted? Learn to enjoy life, not crawl through it like you have a boulder on your shoulders.
Tristian：我的誓言并不是千斤重担，而是我希望一生勉力承担的珍贵责任。对你来说这或许是一件容易的事，但那也只是因为你的存在既空虚又毫无意义。
My vows are no boulder, but a precious load I carry through my entire life. Living is easy for you, but only because your existence is empty and pointless.
Jaethal：你喜欢听罪恶的故事，是不是？那么请听我告解一下……
You like stories about atrocities, don't you? Listen then to my confession...
Tristian：等你真的准备好要告解了，我会洗耳恭听的。不过现在请收起你的吹嘘。
When you are ready to confess, I will hear you. But spare me your boasting.
Jaethal：你太善良了，太天真了……可这世界上充满了邪恶的人。你的女神可以在他们面前保护你吗？
You are so kind, so naive... But there are many evil people in the world. Will your goddess be able to protect you from them?
Tristian：我向她祈祷是为了获得忍耐的力量，而不是保护。有一天这一切都会终结，而到那时，需要保护的人是你。
I pray for her to grant me patience, not protection. One day all this will end, and then you will be the one in need of protection.
Jaethal：想象一下，崔斯提安，这会给人带来多少新的机遇啊。你的青春永远不会消逝，你永远不需要睡觉或者休息，你所有的时间都可以用来拯救无辜的生命，或是向莎恩芮祈祷。
Just imagine, Tristian, how many new opportunities this would bring. Your youth would never fade. You'd never need sleep nor rest. All your time could be devoted to saving innocent lives and praying to Sarenrae.
Tristian：你心里清楚，我的回答永远都是‘不’。你为什么还要不断地问我一样的问题呢？耶萨尔，你只是在用侮辱我和莎恩芮的方式来为自己取乐。
My answer is 'no' and you know it. Why are you asking me over and over? It seems that you just find pleasure in offending me and Sarenrae, Jaethal.
Jaethal：你太美了，瓦尔瑞，特别是作为一名人类。这可不是我在奉承你，我只是在阐述事实。要让这样的美丽离开这个世界真是一种遗憾。
You are beautiful, Valerie, especially for a human. This is not meant as a compliment, merely a statement of fact. It would be a pity if this beauty leaves the world.
Valerie：你的意思是说我应该追随乌伽娑婀的道路，困在生与死之间？我可不想这样。
Do you suggest I follow Urgathoa's path and get myself stuck between life and death? I don't think I'd like it.
Jaethal：雪琳的圣武士尊崇美丽和艺术……不过可笑的是，他们对二者完全一无所知。
Paladins of Shelyn venerate beauty and art... But the amusing part is that they don't have the slightest clue about either.
Valerie：在这点上我倒是赞同你。这也是为什么我会离开永恒玫瑰骑士团。那是一群金玉其外败絮其中的人。
I'd agree with you here. That's why I left the Order of the Eternal Rose. Behind their colored silks and lofty words hides common gray mediocrity.
Jaethal：能够同时拥有高贵血统和高尚灵魂的人并不多。特别是在你们这种人中间更少。能有这样的一个人作为同伴真是太荣幸了。
Not many can boast both good lineage and true nobility of spirit. Even fewer among your people. It is good to have such a person as a companion.
Valerie：谢谢你的夸奖。这片土地上属于贵族阶层的人不多，而且也没人特别看得上他们。
Thank you for the compliment. There aren't many of the aristocratic class in these lands – and they are not greatly missed.
Jaethal：告诉我，瓦尔瑞，你为什么会走上佣兵的道路？即便是你选择自我放逐，你也不必和普通人并肩战斗，你完全可以从事更体面的事业。
Tell me, Valerie, why did you choose the path of a mercenary? Even in self-imposed exile, you could find a more enjoyable pursuits than fighting side-by-side with the common folk.
Valerie：什么是体面的事业呢？是要成为一名供人取乐的女子吗？还是去找一个有钱的糟老头子当情妇？根本没门！我可不想成为别人的玩物。
I wonder what that might be? Perhaps I should have become a lady of pleasure? Or the mistress to an odd duke? Not a chance! I've no interest in being someone's toy.
Jaethal：你非常美丽。而且年轻，人人都迷恋你。遗憾的是，你并没有利用命运赐给你的这些天赋。
You are beautiful. And young, desired. It's a pity you do not use what gifts fate has bestowed upon you.
Valerie：你的战斗技巧还不错，你的意志力也很坚强，而且你不受疾病和年龄所拖累。遗憾的是，你也没有善加利用你的这些天赋！
You know how to fight, your spirit is strong, and you fear neither disease nor age. It's a pity you haven't found better use for your own gifts!
生日祝福
Jaethal：我想说一下我的祝酒词。敬我们的首领和庇护主，{主角名}。愿您在以后的年月里安享生命里的最高欢愉。
I'd like to make a toast. A toast to {name}, our leader and patron. May you enjoy all life's brightest pleasures in your many years to come.
Jaethal：愿那些弱者和贱民都嫉妒您，就如他们嫉妒所有高他们一等的人一样。
And may the weaklings and the rabble envy you, as they always do their superiors.
C11
Jaethal：鹿王用这么粗鄙的方法居然取得了这样的成果，真让人惊叹。想象一下，如果我们手中有合法的权力，我们能做什么……
It is amazing what the Stag Lord has achieved with such crude methods. Imagine what we might do, with lawful power in our hands...
Harrim：蠕虫为腐烂的尸体而战，毫无意义。
Worms fighting over a decaying corpse. Pointless.
Amiri：土匪欺软怕硬，我们三两下就能解决他们。
The bandits prey on the weak and flee from the strong. We'll kill them in no time.
Valerie：只有服从法律的权力才是合法的。——当权力扭曲了人心，让人一心只想往国库里填满黄金的时候，它就变得邪恶了。
Power is lawful when it obeys the laws. — Evil, when it twists them to fill the treasury with gold.
C11_Fog
Jaethal：这雾……起雾一定有什么原因。我在琼宁的王廷见识过各种各样的阴谋诡计。我不知道这些笨蛋土匪是怎么做到的，不过他们常常用雾气来隐藏自己的行踪。
This fog... it's here for a reason. I've seen much subterfuge, hearing cases at Kyonin's courts. I don't know how these dimwit bandits managed to do it, but they use the fog to hide their trails.
Linzi：也许这雾和土匪没关系，而是有完全不同的来源呢？
Maybe the fog has nothing to do with the bandits, and has a completely different origin?
Octavia：不管它是怎么起来的，总之我们被困住了，直到雾完全散去前没法离开这片地方。
No matter where it came from, we won't be able to leave these lands until it clears away.
Regongar：哈，我都怀疑他们请不请得起能施这种魔法的法师。
Hah. I doubt they could afford to hire a wizard capable of such magic.
KassilAldori
Jaethal：卡希尔·阿尔多瑞可不是个好选择——他是个既没意思又没骨气的理想主义者。如果他连守护自己的领土都做不到的话，他又怎么能捍卫这片封地/王国的利益呢？
Kassil Aldori is a bad choice — a boring, spineless idealist. How will he defend the interests of the barony/kingdom, if he can't even stand on his own?
Jubilost：他显然缺乏技巧和智慧——而这些恰恰是外交官所需要的素质。
He's certainly lacking in artifice and wit — much needed qualities in a diplomat.
Ekun：这算哪门子建议？雇用一个不诚实的仆人，只为了更好地欺骗自己的邻居？
What kind of advice is that? Hire a dishonest servant, to better deceive one's neighbors?
Valerie：他不缺勇气和荣誉这些对任何人来说都最为重要的品质。
He's not lacking in valor and honor, the most important qualities for any person.
C21
Jaethal：我希望{主角名}能明白，现在她/他面前的道路有多么……令人惊异……
I hope {name} realizes what... amazing... new paths are open to her/him now.
Linzi：没错！所有的道路都向我们敞开！
That's right! All roads are open to us now!
Valerie：一些人认为，权力是一种中饱私囊的方法；还有一些人觉得，权力是一件重大的责任。
For some, power is a way to line one's pockets. For others, a grave responsibility.
Tristian：不管你能用这种力量做什么，我没有兴趣看……
I wouldn't want to see what you might do with such power...
C21_Trobold
Jaethal：狗头人帝国，还有不怕火的巨魔，一定会结成不错的同盟。
An empire of kobolds and trolls, unafraid of fire. They would make fine allies.
Regongar：有些盟友，你一转过身，就咬掉你半个屁股。哈！
Some allies. Turn your back and get half of your ass bitten off. Ha!
Amiri：然后他们又跟臭乎乎的螨怪结盟，避免战争，把他们钉在地上，就这样了。
Then they make allies with stinky mites to avoid war. Peg them to the ground and be done with it.
Ekun：这意味着与邪恶联盟，听你这么一说，更是清楚得很了。
It would mean an alliance with evil. Hearing you speak the words, it becomes all the clearer.
Kanerah：唔，如果一个不死精灵可以做盟友，为什么狗头人和巨魔不能呢？
Well, if an undead elf can be an ally, then why not kobolds and trolls as well?
C22_Bloom
Jaethal：我一点也不喜欢流血和瘀伤，但看到这么多人被瘟疫夺走性命，而我却不必畏惧，真是有趣极了。
I find no pleasure in blood and gore. But it's amusing to see so many mowed down by plague, knowing I myself have nothing to fear.
Tristian：你的灵魂是不是毫无底线？你那颗黑心是不是对所有的罪恶都来者不拒？
Is there no limit to how far your soul may fall? Is there any vice your dark heart will not indulge?
Harrim：一切都有结束的那一天，你不过是在拖延那不可避免的结局，延长你的痛苦。

What region of Qadira would you like to write about, master Jubilost? Our land is vast and diverse, and there are as many tribes living there as there are in all the River Kingdoms.

I did! In my homeland, hoarfrost forms on the stones after cold nights, and wizards cast frost spells — not to mention my own elemental abilities... But true winter and snowfall is something else!

We certainly have much in common, but it saddens me that your interest in map-making and discovery is purely materialistic. While I only care to serve knowledge and science!
Kanerah：那又怎样？如果没了资金，哪还有你的科学？你需要机灵一点。把你的金子变成发现，再把你的发现换成金子！
So what? Where would your science be without proper financing? You just need a little ingenuity. Turn your gold into discoveries, and your discoveries – into gold!
Jubilost：为什么要偷偷拿走我的书呢，你可以大大方方的找我借啊。
Rather than snatching my books when no one is watching, you could have just asked.
Amiri：我只是……我只是想看一看里面的图画！一本封面画着两把剑的书里有各种战斗场面的图画，都很有意思。另外一本书的封面上画着一个女性战士的形象。我喜欢她。
I was… I just wanted to look at the pictures! The one with the two swords has many pictures of different battles — all very interesting. And the second has a picture of lady warrior on the cover. I like her.
Jubilost：我听过一个理论，说北方的野蛮人是霜巨人的后代。看看你，安米芮，我觉得这个理论说不定真有些道理！
I've heard a hypothesis that northern barbarians are descended from frost giants. Looking at you, Amiri, I tend to think there's a modicum of truth in this!
Amiri：巨人？哈！这可是小看我们了。我来告诉你，我们其实就是大山的后代！
Giants? Ha! Aim higher. I'll have you know, we descended from the mountains themselves!
Jubilost：我一直在想，为什么在大多数情况下，头脑和四肢会互成反比呢？
I wonder why in the vast majority of cases, brains and brawn are reciprocally proportional?
Amiri：反比？反比是什么意思？啊，侏儒，我明白了，你是不是又在说我的坏话！神啊，可千万别让我有一天能听懂你说的胡话！
Pro-pro-ti-what? Oh, gnome, I feel it in my gut you just said something bad about me! Gods forbid one day I understand what you're babbling!
Jubilost：嘿，谁拿了我的‘怪物大全’？还在上面留下了油腻的手印！
Hey, who touched my 'Monstrum Compendium Universalis'? – With greasy fingers!
Amiri：呃……唔，是我。我不能看看你的书吗？里面的图画都很有趣。而且我还在皮裙上擦过手了！真的擦过了！
Em... Well, me. Was I not supposed to? The pictures are funny. And I wiped my hands on a hide first! I did!
Jubilost：你为什么用这么大的一把剑？你有没有想过，有得必有失？
Why do you need such a huge sword? Have you ever considered that you might be compensating for something?
Amiri：怎么，你想要我把你挂在上面去看看风景？
You want to see the view from on top of it?
Jubilost：你的狼看上去比你要和善得多啊。而且话也多得多。
Your wolf seems twice as friendly as you are. And twice as eloquent.
Ekun：要让我说，我也希望你不要总是口出恶言，也能说出些至理名言啊。
And I say it would be better if your tongue were dripping wisdom — rather than venom.
Jubilost：人们都说，宠物如其主。我本来觉得这个话十分荒谬，知道我遇到了你，还有你的狼。它简直和你一样阴沉！
They say pets look like their owners. I always thought this statement was ridiculous until I met you, Ekun – and your wolf. It's as sullen as you!
Ekun：那么你一定也养了一条响尾蛇当宠物。既吵闹，又有剧毒。
You should have a rattlesnake. Noisy and venomous.
Jubilost：你知道吗，艾昆，虽然你有些粗鲁，不过你的旅行经验可以拯救许多人的性命……只要有人能够用简单易懂的语言将其写成书。
You know, Ekun, you might be rude, but your traveling experience could save many lives... If only there were someone capable of writing it all down in simple and straightforward terms.
Ekun：你是在说你自己吗？不要指望了。我觉得我们两个人永远不会促膝长谈。
Are you hinting at yourself? Don't get your hopes up. I don't think we'll be having any long conversations.
Jubilost：艾昆达尤……这是你的全名，对吗，艾昆？这个名字有什么含义吗？也不是说我非得知道，只是你懂的，我这个人对任何事情都很好奇。
Ekundayo... This is your full name, right, Ekun? Does it mean something? It's not that it's important that I know it, but you see, curiosity makes me restless.
Ekun：所有的名字都有一定的含义。你名字的意思大概可能是‘牙尖嘴利’。或者是‘胡说八道’。
All names mean something. Yours probably means 'acid-tongued'. Or 'pestilence'.
Jubilost：终于可以休息了！我终于可以坐下来安静地读读书了！
Ah, rest at last! Finally I can sit and read quietly!
Ekun：不。不要读书。去打水。要不然你就饿着肚子读书吧。
No. No reading. Fetch water. Or you can read and stay hungry.
Kalikke/Kanerah：朱比罗斯特，为什么不从你的书里抬起头来跟同伴聊聊天呢？火堆旁的夜晚可是最好的交流时光。
Why don't you pull your nose out of your books and talk to one of your companions, Jubilost? An evening by the fire is the best time for conversation.
Jubilost：嗯，格罗图斯那边有什么新消息吗？这个世界的末日什么时候会到来呢？这周内，还是下周？
Well, is there any news from Groetus? When should we expect the end of the world? This week – or maybe next?
Harrim：格罗图斯不会谈论他的想法。不过一切的终结是难以避免的……具体是什么时候又有什么关系呢？
Groetus does not speak of his plans. But the end of times is inevitable – does it matter when?
Jubilost：我还记得神秘学院的一门课，那名教授对于大脑、以及大脑的各种……病症，都十分了解。他为我们展示过一个病人，他的大脑颞区中过一箭，自那之后他就会产生幻听。
I recall a lecture at the College of Mysteries… The professor was well-versed in the brain, and various of its... deviations. He demonstrated a patient who'd begun to hear voices, after being hit by an arrow in the temporal region.
Harrim：为什么你和其他人都拼命想要证明我疯了？是不是因为你内心深处明白我是对的，而又害怕承认，甚至不敢面对自己？
Why do you and the others try so hard to convince me I'm crazy? Is it because you secretly know that I'm right, and you're afraid to admit it, even to yourself?
Jubilost：你是我认识的唯一一个一心求死的活人。所以你是一个非常奇特的样本，我应该把你写进我的书里。
You are the only living creature I know, whose entire purpose is its own death. This makes you quite an amusing curiosity, well worth mentioning in one of my works.
Harrim：如果你觉得我会感到很光荣，那你可就错了。你爱在书里写什么我可不关心。再怎么写书也阻止不了末日的来临！
If you think I will be flattered by this, you are deeply mistaken. I don't care what you write in your books. There are no words that can change the inevitable!
Jubilost：你还真是个另类。一名背弃了托拉格的矮人，一名连钉子都造不出来的矮人……如果你告诉我你不喜欢喝酒我也不会吃惊！
You're quite a rarity. A dwarf rejecting Torag, a dwarf unable to forge a nail... I wouldn't be surprised to hear you don't enjoy ale!
Harrim：我……不，有的时候我也会喝上两杯。但是比我消沉的那段日子喝得少多了，那时我刚逃出了故乡，找不到人生的目的，也不知道自己该去哪里。如果我的意志力够强，也许我已经完全戒酒了。
I... No, I sometimes do bend my elbow. But much less now than in my bad days, after I ran away from my home, knowing no purpose, or where I should go. If I had a stronger will, perhaps I would forgo drinking completely.
Jubilost：即便以一个不死生物的标准来看，你都算是非常奇特的样本。不如把你的尸体捐献出来做科学研究呢？当然，是在你死掉以后……
You are quite an unusual specimen, even by undead standards. Why don't you bequeath your body to science – after your final death, of course?
Jaethal：你也挺聪明，至少你自己是这么认为的吧。那么为什么你不把你的大脑捐献出来研究一下呢？我很乐意帮你把它取出来。
In your own mind, at least, you seem to be quite a famous intellectual. Don't you want to bequeath your brain to science? I'd be happy to extract it for you.
Jubilost：请满足一下我的好奇心。在你成为……嗯哼……不死生物以后，你发现的第一个不同之处是什么？
Indulge my curiosity. After you've become, ahem, undead, what changes would you notice first of all?
Jaethal：第一个？让我想想……我的心脏不再跳动了，血液也不在血管中流动了，也不需要呼吸了。然后就有一个不懂礼貌的侏儒开始跟踪我，问我一些愚蠢的问题。
First of all? Let me think... Your heart isn't beating, blood isn't running through your veins, there's no need to breathe. And insolent gnomes keep stalking you, asking tactless questions.
Jubilost：虽然这么说很奇怪，不过我们俩之间似乎还有不少相同之处。你活着是为了得到快乐，追求新的体验和感受……而侏儒们也是为了这些而活，否则我们就会遭到脱色的侵蚀，并最终死去。
It feels strange to say it, but you and I have more in common than it seems. You live for pleasures, new feelings and sensations... And gnomes live for the same thing – otherwise we're overtaken by the Bleaching, soon followed by death.
Jaethal：这个观点很奇特……也许你说的没错。既然如此，为什么你不投入乌伽娑婀的怀抱呢？我可以保证你会获得大量的新奇体验！
A curious perspective... You might even have a point. If so – why don't you submit to Urgathoa? I guarantee you a host of new sensations!
Jubilost：等我们的探险结束以后，你和我一起去安布萨朗吧？那里的医生们会为了研究一个独特的不死生物样本蜂拥而至，这可是千载难逢的机会，我们一定可以挣不少钱！
After our expedition is over – why don't you come to Absalom with me? Our medics will flood us with gold, for the privilege of studying such a unique undead specimen!
Jaethal：你这个提议真是抬举我了，不过我还是想拒绝。如果你真的想要为你的医生提供研究样本，我倒是可以把你变成一个不死生物！
A flattering proposition, but I think I'll decline. Although if you want to supply your medics with an undead specimen so badly, I could certainly make one of you!
Jubilost：怎么，现在已经是秋天了？割麦子的季节到了？可今天我偏偏忘记带上我的镰刀了！但你正好有一把，可以派上用场。
Why, is it fall already? Hay-making time? And I forgot my scythe, today of all days! But we could put yours to proper use!
Jaethal：你这个玩笑水平还挺高。如果你死了，我一定要把你复活。当然，不用整个的你……只要脑袋就好了。除了你那聪明的小脑袋之外，其他的部分都没什么用。
Your jokes are quite amusing. If you die, I'll have to bring you back. Not in your entirety – just the head. Everything below your clever little head is quite useless anyway.
Jubilost：我曾经遇到过一个叫艾兹仁的老人，他说他想要学习魔法，成为一名开拓史学家。以他的年纪，他早就应该退休了。
I once met an old man named Ezren, he said he wanted to learn magic and be a Pathfinder chronicler. At his age he should be considering retirement.
Linzi：不过我觉得他想要尝试新的东西这一点确实精神可嘉。
I think it's commendable that he wants to try something new.
Jubilost：你知道吗，琳翠，你写作的出发点是非常值得夸奖的。不过你具体的写作手法嘛……没有接受过正式的学术教育，你永远只能为市井俗民编写一些廉价的故事！
You know, Linzi, your writing aspirations are quite commendable. But the way you're going about it... Without a proper academic education – you'll never rise above writing cheap tales for peasants!
Linzi：我见过你的‘学术教育’啊。一群自命不凡的骗子争相恐后地用他们那不切实际的言论互相诋毁。还是算了吧，我宁愿去写我自己的故事，也不想变成那些‘教授’一样的井底之蛙！
I've seen this 'academic education' of yours. A pack of arrogant peacocks trying to outshine each other with their highbrow talk. Hell no, I'd rather write my tales than become a smug toad like those 'professors'!
Jubilost：琳翠，你实在不该离开大艺术学院。你在写作上的天分毋庸置疑，可是天分就像是一块原石——未经雕琢便一文不值。
You shouldn't have quit the Academy of Grand Arts, Linzi. You definitely have a talent for writing, but talent is like an uncut diamond – worthless without skill.
Linzi：又不是我要退出学院的。是学院开除了我。不过我可一点也不后悔！
It wasn't me who quit the Academy. The Academy quit me. And I don't regret it a single bit!
Jubilost：琳翠，并不是说我不愿意把我的墨水和羽毛笔借给你……不过你应该先问一问我！
It's not that I would mind you borrowing my ink and quills, Linzi... But you could at least ask first!
Linzi：嗯，你在睡觉，我又不想把你叫醒。而我的脑海里突然灵光一现……我想要立刻把这个故事写下来，而且倒霉的是，我的纸也用光了……
Well, you were sleeping, and I didn't want to wake you. And I had such a story in my head!.. I was so excited to write it down, and unfortunately I ran out of paper, too…
Jubilost：琳翠，我在想……等我们的冒险结束以后，我可能会需要……这么说吧，一名秘书。我需要一个聪明的人，可以把我的宝贵想法写下来！
I was thinking, Linzi... Maybe when our journey is over, I'll need, let's say, a secretary. Someone smart who can write down my invaluable thoughts!
Linzi：你是说我吗？哦，我不知道……当然了，你是一座知识的宝库，写作大师，称号一大堆……不过我还是比较习惯于自己想写什么就写什么。
You mean me? Oh, I don't know... Of course, you are a bastion of science, master of the quill and so on, but I'm kind of used to writing whatever I want.
Jubilost：这支队伍里除了我之外还有别人也懂得文字的力量，这真是太棒了！琳翠，也许有一天你也可以成为举足轻重的人物。
It's great that at least someone in this party besides me understands the power of words! Perhaps you will really amount to something in time, Linzi.
Linzi：你真的这么想吗？这……你真是太抬举我了。谢谢！
You really think so? It's... That's very flattering. Thank you!
Jubilost：你又在写你那小女孩的秘密日记了吗？而且我猜你一定会把我描写成一个坏脾气的大恶霸，一个口出狂言的老愤青？
Are you writing in your girl's diary again? And of course, I've no doubt you'll picture me as this bilious bully, and an acid-tongued cynic?
Linzi：当然。我当然要实话实说！再说了，你是个大名人！如果我在描写你的时候营造出一种反差，读者的反响一定会非常强烈！
Of course. I wouldn't distort the truth on that score! And besides, you're such a well-known figure! If I depicted you any differently, they'd all notice right away!
Jubilost：你的学院虽然不是什么最优秀的教育机构，但是对你的教导也太缺乏了。相信我，没有接受过合适的教育是无法走上写作这条道路的。
Your Academy might not be the best institution of learning, but you are so greatly lacking in education. Trust me, a career in writing is impossible without it.
Linzi：所以你的书反映的不是你自己的才华，反而是你的老师的功劳？太棒了！没想到你也能这么坦白啊！
So your books are not a reflection of your own merit, but your teachers'? How about that! I never expected such a confession from you!
Jubilost：有人把我的羽毛笔弄坏了！等等……嘿，是被咬坏的！诺克诺克！
Somebody broke all my quills! Wait a second… hey, they were chewed! Nok-Nok!
NokNok：啥？哪里？诺克诺克听不到，诺克诺克很忙！
Wha? Where? Nok-Nok can't hear, Nok-Nok is very busy!
Jubilost：看来你也不像我想象的那么废物嘛，诺克诺克。要是有人把你洗干净，再用链子栓起来，肯定会有一大群学者跑来研究你的。
Perhaps you are not as useless as I first thought, Nok-Nok. If somebody washed and leashed you, you would make an interesting specimen for a gathering of scholars.
NokNok：朱比朱比又开始说大话了。话又多！又难懂！还说要洗干净，你自己才该洗干净。肥皂可以杀死你，部落告诉我的！
Jub-Jub with his big words again. Too long! Too hard! And about washing — you go do it yourself. Soap makes you dead, tribe told me so!
Jubilost：该死！你为什么会觉得我讨厌所有人呢？我只是想确保大家都不要做错事，这是为了大家，为了我们的事业！看看你们都是怎么报答我的？
Curses! How did you get it into your head that I hate everyone?! I'm just making sure everyone does everything right — for the sake of everyone else. For our cause! And what do I get in return?
Octavia：我，觉得你讨厌所有人？哈，真是无稽之谈！我非常清楚，你根本不关心任何人，除了你自己。而且你对我们的事业也无所谓，你只是喜欢指出其他人的错误，来彰显自己的优越感。
Me, thinking that you hate everyone? Ha, what nonsense! I know full well that you don't care one whit about anyone besides yourself. And you couldn't care less about our cause — you just like to point out people's mistakes so you can feel superior.
Jubilost：我曾经认识一个女人，她十分钦佩我的人格，对我极度迷恋。不过她居然狂妄地说我‘有资格成为易洛理的信徒’！我绝对不能忍受和如此自大的人在一起！
I once knew a woman who seemed infatuated with my esteemed personality. Although she had the audacity to call me a worthy disciple of Irori! I couldn't stand being with anyone that ignorant!
Octavia：你会不会也让你的情人戴上口钳呢？舔你的脚会不会让你很享受？
Do you also make your lovers wear a bridle? Or is kissing your feet enough for you?
Kalikke：可是为什么？你或许能帮到她——教她一些东西。如果你不将学问与他人分享，那么知识渊博的意义何在？
But why? You might have helped her — taught her something. What's the point of being knowledgeable if you won't share your knowledge with others?
Kanerah：曾经有个追求者弄混了我最喜爱的和最讨厌的葡萄酒。我毫不犹豫地把他赶了出去！
A suitor once confused my favorite wine with one I detest. I threw him out without another thought!
Jubilost：不要一直打扰我！在我们的队伍里，我是旅行经验最丰富的人。我去到过这个世界的各个角落，在有的地方你连一分钟都活不了！所以我说要怎么做对我们所有人最好，那一定是正确的！
Stop interrupting me all the time! Of everyone in our party, I have the most vast travel experience. I've been to such corners of the world – where you wouldn't have survived even for a minute! So believe me when I say that I know what will be better for all of us!
Octavia：你所说的‘我们所有人’一定是在说你自己和你那大脑袋里一直听到的夸赞声吧。
By 'all of us' you must mean you and the chorus of praise you must constantly hear inside that thick skull of yours.
Jubilost：我一直孜孜不倦地努力着，以求成功完成我们的任务。我鞠躬尽瘁、考虑周全，就是为了让我们能够实现这次旅程的目标。但是我听到的都是什么报答的话？都是无端的辱骂！
I concern myself tirelessly about our mission's success. I think three steps ahead, just so that our expedition can reach its goal. And what do I hear in return? Unprovoked insults!
Octavia：你只关心你自己的安危和快乐，朱比。你并不关心其他人。你这样的人我见多了。
All you care about is your hide and your comfort, Jubi. You don't care about anyone else. I've seen too many like you.
Jubilost：我发现一个问题。我们扎营的方式完全错了。大家都靠过来，我来给你讲一讲应该怎么做……
I'll tell you what. We've organized our encampment completely wrong. Come here all of you, I'll explain what we need to do...
Octavia：唉，看来你还应该去买一只鼓，这样我们大家就可以跟随你的鼓点行军，好不好？你以为自己是谁啊，你个三流作家！我们又不是你的学生！
Oh, and maybe you should buy a drum so it will be easier for us to march to your commands? Who do you think you are, scribbler? We're not your students!
Jubilost：喂！不要乱用法术！别把你的酸液洒在我的著作旁边！
Hey! Careful with the spells! Keep your acid away from my writings!
Octavia：谁管你旁边的那堆垃圾啊？你继续浪费纸吧。这样你还能安静一会儿！
Who cares about any of your piles besides you? Go ahead, waste your paper. – Maybe it'll keep you quiet for a while!
Jubilost：你能不能告诉我，你在科技联盟时的生活是什么样的？我纯粹只是好奇。
Could you please tell me, out of pure interest, how life was structured during your time with the Technic League?
Octavia：哦，你有兴趣想了解一下？我不止可以告诉你，我还可以给你演示一下！首先我需要准备一根又粗又大的棒子！
Ah, you're interested? I won't just tell you – I'll show you! First I just need to cut a nice, thick rod!
Jubilost：纽玫瑞亚真是个神奇的地方。那片土地深处坠落的太空飞船碎片产生出许多神奇的自然和社会现象……
Numeria is an incredible place. The fragments of the starship lying in its depths generate the most amazing natural and social phenomena...
Octavia：如果你有钱又有闲，那么你就可以在纽玫瑞亚随意游荡，享受生活。不过如果你是一个身无分文的逃犯，那么你就只会想要逃离那里。
It must be great to wander Numeria when you're free and rich, and just enjoy life. But when you're a fugitive without a coin in your pocket, all you want to do is get away.
Jubilost：咳咳！我……我一直想要把科技联盟在他们的奴隶身上纹的符号描下来！如果有人愿意坐下来给我临摹，我愿意付给他钱……
Ahem. I've... always wanted to draw the symbols that the Technic League tattoo on their sorcerers! I even have money to pay anyone who would agree to sit for me...
Regongar：给我闭嘴，侏儒……否则我就把你的牙敲掉。
Bite your tongue, gnome... while you still have teeth to bite with.
Jubilost：告诉我，你的纹身只是用来装饰的吗？还是有什么意义呢？
Tell me, are your tattoos just decorations or do they mean something?
Regongar：啊哈！这个纹身的意思是有一个矮个子又讨厌的侏儒……而这个纹身表示他可以滚下去的地方，哈哈！
Uh huh! This one means a short annoying gnome... And this one shows the place where he can get off, ha ha!
Jubilost：嘿，我真想读一读科技联盟的笔记啊……他们做了那么多的实验！他们一定积累了海量的科研数据。
Heh, I wish I could pore over the Technic League's notes... They've done so many experiments! They must have a trove of scientific data.
Regongar：实验？你应该去体验体验我经历过的实验，你连一个小时都坚持不下去！不过没关系。我们一定会再遇见那些实验人员的，然后我就会徒手把他们都撕成碎片！慢！慢！撕！
Experiments? You should try the experiments I had to go through, you wouldn't hold up for an hour! But never mind. We'll meet those experimenters again. And I'm gonna rip them to pieces with my bare hands! Slo-o-o-wly!
Jubilost：我必须要承认，纽玫瑞亚游牧民的习俗在我们看来虽然有些可怕，不过也只有这样才能在那片民风彪悍的土地上生存下来。
I have to admit, the customs of Numerian nomads may seem horrible to us, but they may be the only way to survive among other hostile peoples.
Regongar：我才不管他们有什么习俗！不管他们的生存有多么艰难，把自己的孩子卖去当奴隶都是件非常卑鄙的行为！
I don't give a shit about their customs! Selling your children into slavery is a nasty business, no matter what they're up against!
Jubilost：我们来玩骰子怎么样？来玩几局吧？
So, how about a game of dice? A couple of rounds, hunh?
Regongar：不不不。你喜欢作弊！除非你答应教我用你的那些不入流招数，否则我才不要让你把我兜里的钱给赢光。
Oh no. You're a cheat! And until you teach me how you do your dirty tricks, I'm not letting you clean out my purse.
Jubilost：小子，你这人藏着很多秘密。我不知道你到底在隐瞒什么，不过你要明白，这个世界上没有什么秘密是朱比罗斯特·纳斯罗泊无法查明的！
There's a riddle inside of you, lad. I don't know what you're hiding, but be sure of this: there's no secret in the world that Jubilost Narthropple cannot reveal!
Tristian：朱比罗斯特，我不用隐瞒任何东西。我就是我，莎恩芮的一名虔诚的奴仆，仅此而已。
I don't have anything to hide, Jubilost. I am who I am – a humble servant of Sarenrae, and nothing more.
Jubilost：崔斯提安，你不是这里的本地人。给我讲讲喀勒什怎么样？我一直想了解一下那个地方。
You're not from these lands, Tristian. Why don't you tell me about Kelesh? I've always been interested in learning about this land.
Tristian：那么你为什么不自己去一趟呢？我相信你会在那里受到热烈欢迎的。
Then why don't you go there yourself? I'm sure you'll be given a warm welcome.
Jubilost：崔斯提安，我对你的善良和天真感到非常惊讶。随着人们年纪的增长，大家都会遇到各种磨难，反而不会再保持这些优点。毕竟这个世界是残酷的，你懂的……
I'm surprised by your kindness and naivety, Tristian. People rarely remain untroubled enough to hold on to such gifts, as they come of age. The world is cruel, you know...
Tristian：那么我想我一定非常幸运。女神的教诲可以让她的仆人免于磨难。
I guess I'm lucky then. The teachings of the goddess save her servants from all kinds of trouble.
Jubilost：崔斯提安，你知道你的问题是什么吗？你这个人没有野心！
Tristian, do you know what your problem is? You have no ambition!
Tristian：这算是问题吗？我有莎恩芮的慈悲。有这个就够了。
Is that a problem? I have Sarenrae's mercy. That's all I need.
Jubilost：为什么我每说一句话你都要用伤心的叹气和责备的眼神来回应？
Are you seriously going to accompany my every phrase with a sad sigh and a glance of reproof?
Tristian：我只是想要搞明白你为什么总是要伤害别人，你的内心里明明没有恶意。
I'm just trying to understand why you keep hurting people, yet harbor no evil in your heart.
Jubilost：如果要让我说，你把求爱者的情诗撕掉，这并不是你的错。有的诗歌确实不配。作诗的人也不配。
If you want my opinion, you weren't wrong to tear up your suitor's poem. Some verse deserves nothing better. — The same for their authors.
Valerie：我无法控制自己，而作为一名战士，自控是非常重要的。不过我对此也并不后悔。我不想要成为一名雪琳的信徒。
I couldn't control myself, and control is essential for a fighter. But still I do not regret what I did. My place was not with the worshipers of Shelyn.
Jubilost：你知道吗，瓦尔瑞，我觉得你不应该离开永恒玫瑰骑士团。相比于智慧，雪琳的圣武士更尊崇美丽。你应该和他们在一起。
You know, Valerie, I don't think you should have abandoned the Order of the Eternal Rose. Paladins of Shelyn value beauty over intellect. You would belong there.
Valerie：我不喜欢你的语气，侏儒。虽然我不如你聪明，但是我也能分辨夸奖和嘲笑。
I don't like your tone, gnome. I may not be as smart as you, but I can tell a compliment from mockery.
Jubilost：你来自于一个贵族家族。我不明白，你为什么不回到你父母的城堡去，而是在这片森林里游荡，杀戮各种怪物。
You're from a noble family. I don't understand why you didn't simply return to your parents' castle, rather than wandering the forests, slaying all manner of monsters.
Valerie：确实很令人费解……在这里，在失窃之地，我可以按照我自己希望的样子来生活，而不是别人想要看到的那样。大多数人只是想要一张美丽的脸孔，而从未留意到脸孔后面的那个人。
It's hard to understand... Here in the Stolen Lands I can be what I am, not who people want to see. Most simply want a beautiful façade, never noticing the human behind it.
Jubilost：雪琳的圣武士会学习歌唱、绘画、演奏乐器。等扎营的时候你也可以给我们唱一曲啊。
Paladins of Shelyn are taught to sing, draw, and play musical instruments. Why don't you sing sometime when we camp?
Valerie：我不想唱。我已经受够了演奏音乐和唱歌的日子。那就是在浪费时间。我宁愿去磨剑或者检查我的盔甲。毕竟唱歌又不能打败森林里的盗贼！
I don't care to. I've had enough of singing and playing music. It's a useless waste of time. – I'd rather hone my sword or check over my armor again. You can't fight off forest bandits with a song!
Jubilost：平时我不太会写诗，不过你的美丽给了我无比的灵感，我想要写一首赞歌。请听我念一念……
Usually I don't write poetry, but your beauty has inspired even me, to write a little ode. Here it is, listen now...
Valerie：如果你是想跟我开个玩笑，那么你这个笑话一点也不好笑。你最好把你的赞歌收起来，不然我就要生气了。如果你是认真的……嗯，我的建议还是一样的。
If you're trying to play a joke on me, I'm afraid it's gone lame. You'd better put away your ode before you make me angry. – And if you're serious... Well, my advice is the same.
生日祝福
Jubilost：这是独特之人的特殊一天。你知道我不会围着没有价值的人打转，对吧？{主角名}，你已经实现了大多数人想都不敢想的事。
It's a special day for a special person. You know I wouldn't be hanging around someone unworthy, right? {name}, you've already achieved more than most dare dream of.
Jubilost：您的生日将在编年史不可磨灭，未来的某些可怜的学者们必须牢记它，要是他们想通过北阿维斯坦的历史考试的话。所以，恭祝华诞快乐，阁下/殿下！一切安好——请别忘了给那些记述者讲一两个您的新英勇事迹。每年。
Your birth date is enshrined in the chronicles, and some poor scholar somewhere will have to memorize it if he wants to pass his Northern Avistan History exam. So, Happy Birthday, Your Grace/Your Highness! Be well – and don't forget to update those chronicles with one or two heroic deeds. Each year.
Kassil
Jubilost：这位阿尔多瑞特使是个奇怪的人，我从没想过剑爵中还有半兽人。
Envoy Aldori is a curious type. I never expected to see a half-orc among the Swordlords.
Valerie：真正的骑士不看出身门第，而看高尚与勇敢的心灵。而毫无疑问，卡希尔爵士就拥有这样的美德。
A noble and brave heart makes for a true knight, not a person's ancestry. And Sir Kassil possesses those virtues without a doubt.
Harrim：他改写了从他出生以来就注定的命运。他找到了自己的道路。真是太帅了。
He was able to change a destiny that was written upon him by his very birth. He was able to find his own way. This is admirable.
Linzi：卡希尔真是个甜心。但他应该多多微笑——那他就会变成无价之宝了！
Kassil is a sweetheart. But he should smile more often — then he'd be priceless!
C21_AfterCandlemere
Jubilost：烛湖区被谜团所笼罩。也许我应该重新整理一下这里的历史，写篇文章……甚至是一整本书！
Candlemere is shrouded by mystery. Perhaps I should recount the history of this place in an article... or even an entire book!
Octavia：真正的谜团是，我们究竟是怎么继续忍受下来这个不堪又浮华的小丑的。或者说，是为什么。
The real mystery is how the hell we continue to put up with such an unbearable, pompous buffoon. Or why, for that matter.
Amiri：愚蠢的废墟，怪异的灯光……真是胡说八道。你还不如写我们跟那些臭巨魔之间的战争呢。
Stupid ruins, freaking lights... This is total bullshit. Better you write about our war with those stinky trolls.
Linzi：这主意不错，朱比罗斯特！让我们共同努力，一起来写那本书吧！恐怕不只是一本书——我们可以写出一整个系列！
A fine idea, Jubilost! Let us join forces and write that book together! But not just a single book — we could pen a whole series!
C21_BeforeTrollLair
Jubilost：该死的狗头人！和塔图克那个卑鄙的鳞片混蛋决一胜负是多么愉快的事！
Damnable kobolds! What a pleasure it would be to settle the score with Tartuk, that foul scaly bastard!
Valerie：据说这个塔图克跟他的同类不一样……我说的不只是鳞片的颜色。
This Tartuk is rumored to be different from his kin... and I mean not only the color of his scales.
Ekun：你对狗头人的憎恨就像我对巨魔一样。我……能理解。
Your hatred for kobolds is like mine for trolls. It's... understandable.
Tristian：不要让个人恩怨影响了你的判断，朱比罗斯特。
Don't let personal quarrels cloud your judgment, Jubilost.
C21_Tristian
Jubilost：难道只有我一个人觉得，崔斯提安说不出来任何有关喀勒什生活的事，真的很奇怪吗？——尤其是，他似乎并不是那里出身的。
Am I the only one who finds it strange that Tristian can't tell us anything about life in Kelesh? – On top of the fact that he does not appear to be of native stock.
Linzi：啊……他住在修道院里——这就是为什么他见识甚少。他也提到过，他的父母不是那里人——所以他的长相也不一样。
Well... he lived in a monastery — that's why he never saw anything. And he mentioned that his parents weren't from around there — hence his appearance.
Ekun：他只是不喜欢说有关自己的事，没什么奇怪的。
He just doesn't like talking about himself. There's nothing strange in it.
Regongar：除了他的莎恩芮，他还有什么可说的吗？
Does he have anything to say at all, except about his Sarenrae?
Kalikke/Kanerah：我本应该详细问问他的身份，他从哪来……但另一方面，我又不想问——因为我根本没兴趣跟他说话。
I'd like to ask him in detail about who he is and where he's from... But on the other hand, no – I've no interest in speaking with him at all.
C22_Seeds
Jubilost：这些怪事让我既好奇又害怕。我从来没听说过还有这样的种子——连类似的都没有听说过！我们肯定就要有重大发现了。
All these strange happenings fill me with both terror and curiosity. I've never heard of such seeds — or anything remotely like them! We must be on the verge of an immense discovery.
Amiri：我可以告诉你我们会有什么重大发现，不过恐怕你不会想知道的！在弄清发生了什么之前，我们就全都死翘翘了。抿一口泡种子的水，然后你就死得透透的了。
I could tell ya what verge we're on, but you won't want to hear it! We can all drop dead before we figure out what's going on. Just sip some water with a seed in it — and you're as good as dead.
Harrim：听起来就像是你在脑海中想象一本可能会让你名满天下的新书。你的野心可能会毁灭你的，朱比罗斯特。
You sound like you're imagining a new book in your mind, along with what fame it may bring. Your ambition will be your undoing, Jubilost.
Tristian：如果你能利用自己的知识和经验为这场瘟疫找到解药，你就能拯救数以百计的生命。
You might save hundreds of lives, if you use your knowledge and experience to find a cure for this pestilence.
C22_Start
Jubilost：我想，等我彻底厌倦这个地方之后，我就会去纽玫瑞亚。那里的种种奥秘一定能满足我旺盛的好奇心。
I suppose I'll head to Numeria when I grow tired of this lands. There are enough mysteries there to captivate my inquisitive mind.
Octavia：哦，你当然要去啦！我几乎都能想象出科技联盟给你拴上铁链、把你当小白鼠的样子，虽然我觉得你大概活不了多久。
Oh, you should most certainly go! I can almost picture the Technic League maniacs clapping you in chains and making you their guinea pig. Though something tells me that you couldn't survive it for long.
Valerie：我想当地的野蛮人大概欣赏不来你那标志性的优越感。
I imagine the local barbarians wouldn't fully appreciate your signature condescension.
Regongar：哈哈哈！我就爱看你从野蛮人、科技联盟的打手——还有毛绒绒的小动物——手下抱头鼠窜的样子！
Ha, ha, ha! I'd love to watch as you flee the barbarians, the Technic Leagues' thugs — and the little furry animals!
C31_Cyclops
Jubilost：我们的努力总算有了些有用的成果！我们总算不用再过沼泽、斗土匪，而是去探索远古的遗迹了。那里也许还有未被发现的古老独眼巨人墓地。
Finally we set our efforts to something useful! Rather than crawling around swamps and battling boneheaded bandits, we turn to exploring ancient places where the undiscovered tombs of ancient cyclopes may be found.
Octavia：哦，当——当——当然了！结束鹿王的压迫统治，保护无辜的人免受怪物侵扰，与致命的疾病作斗争——这一切都完全没有意义。而帮着朱比编造他的下一本粗制滥造的读物——现在我们总算是在做实事了啊！
Oh su-u-ure! Putting an end to the Stag Lord's oppressive rule, protecting the innocents from monsters, fighting off deadly diseases — that's all completely pointless. But helping Jubie find material for his next potboiler — now we're on to something!
Tristian：这些墓葬可能是整个失落的文明仅存的遗迹，一想到这里我就十分难过……
It saddens me that these tombs may be the only remaining trace of an entire lost civilization...
Jaethal：唔，我可能会在这里有些新发现。
Well, I might see something new here.
c31_FirstWorldFeedback
Jubilost：我敢打包票，那个帮我们摧毁永恒之花的老人肯定对第一世界的侏儒迁徙有所了解。我还是无法相信，他居然什么都不肯说！
I'm fairly certain that the old man who helped us to destroy the Flower knew something of the gnomes' migration from the First World. I still can't believe he refused to give me any answers!
Valerie：我们去了另一个世界，了解了一个难以置信的秘密，赢得了空前的胜利，给我们的土地带来了和平。但你在乎的还是只有侏儒！侏儒！侏儒！侏儒！
We visited another world, learned an unbelievable secret, won an unprecedented victory and brought peace to our lands. Yet still all you care about is gnomes! Gnomes! Gnomes! Gnomes!
Octavia：你亲眼目睹了被怪物从里往外扯碎的人——而你的反应只是顾影自怜？！
You've seen people torn apart by monsters, from the inside out — and your reaction is to wallow in self-pity?!
Harrim：寻求知识就像积攒财宝一样，不过是徒劳无功。
Searching for knowledge is as vain and futile as accumulating worldly goods.
C31_Tristian
Jubilost：我不敢相信，你们居然谁都不觉得崔斯提安的行为有多不自然！没有谁是圣人。——他肯定还有所隐瞒！
I can't believe that none of you see how unnatural Tristian's behavior is! No one is a saint. — He's obviously holding something back!
Octavia：为什么你就是不相信，并不是所有人都像你一样以自我为中心呢？
Why, you can't believe that everyone else isn't as self-centered as you?
Regongar：呵，说得就像哈瑞姆有多正常一样。我们这群人就是个马戏团啊——小丑，怪胎，还有魔术师。
Ha, like Harrim is anywhere close to normal. We're quite a circus. — Clowns, freaks, and magicians.
Jaethal：我觉得那家伙什么都不可能瞒得过我。
I doubt the snotnose could hide anything from me.
C41
Jubilost：还有——伟大的帝国是怎样衰落的？它们的衰落是从何处开始的？这是一个只有伟大的头脑才能解开的谜团……
And still — how do great empires decay? How does their fall begin? This is a mystery worthy of a great mind...
Ekun：毁灭独眼巨人的是残忍，还有放荡。
The cyclopes were destroyed by cruelty. And debauchery.
Amiri：在北方，连傻子都知道答案。当一位伟大的首领征服了所有邻近的部落，宴饮比狩猎的次数更频繁，安眠的时间比战斗更久，这个部落就要完蛋了。
In the north, every fool knows the answer. The end is near when a great chief who has conquered all the neighboring tribes, starts to eat more than hunt and sleep more than fight.
Regongar：这很简单——有人就是不长眼睛，喜欢搬弄是非，哈哈哈哼……
That's simple — someone gets used to blabbing and stops keeping their eyes open, ha ha ha hmm...

Well? Didn't I tell you that no one's so saintly? Tristian was definitely hiding something? Ring any bells? Ah, but you never listen, as usual...
Octavia：如果你说什么我们都要听的话，那我们的头早就炸了。

And how many of your predictions and premonitions in the same period were false?

I must admit, I didn't expect such a monstrous lie, even from him. I sensed there was a scorpion in the party, but I never suspected a giant manticore!
C51
Jubilost：想想吧——成群的野蛮人都被毁灭了。终于有那么一次，文明与理性压倒了蒙昧与暴力。我几乎都变得乐观了呢！
Just think of it — hordes of barbarians, destroyed. Civilization and reason for once claiming victory over savagery and brute force. It almost makes me feel optimistic!
Octavia：不要为自己的缘故感到害怕。——你的血管里流淌的不是血液，而是纯粹的愤怒。命运一定会宽赦你的！
Don't be afraid for your own sake. — Pure bile runs in your veins instead of blood. This fate will surely spare you!
Harrim：你真让我失望。你看不出来吗？胜利不过是给我们带来了更多的折磨和考验，而野蛮人却找到了他们永久的和平？
You disappoint me. Don't you see that victory has merely doomed us to further torment and trials, while the barbarians have found their eternal peace?
Valerie：文明和秩序总会战胜野蛮和混乱。它们也许会输掉一两次战斗，却将赢下整场战争。
Civilization and order always prevail against savagery and chaos. They may lose a battle or two, but they win the war.
Kalikke/Kanerah：在这盘对局里，野蛮人们都只是棋子。很高兴我们的王国领袖让我们免遭同样的厄运。
In this game, the barbarians were only pawns. It's good that our kingdom's ruler saved us from the same lot.
C51_AfterWarStartKtc
Jubilost：我必须要说——伊洛维提是个狡猾又机智的魔鬼。三管齐下的攻击，却既没有先头部队，也没有将军率领。他既没有宣战，也没有集结任何部队，却深深动摇了我们的王国。
I must give Irovetti his due — he's a cunning and resourceful devil. A three-pronged attack, but with no fronts or generals. Without declaring war or assembling any troops, he's shaken our kingdom to the ground.
Amiri：什么？你到底是哪一边的？
What? Whose side are you on?
