Kalikke：我们的补给快吃完了……但没关系，在这片河流奔腾、树林葱郁的土地上，我们可以轻松找到食物！
We are running out of supplies... But no problem. In this land of bursting rivers and lush greenery, we'll have no trouble finding food!
Linzi：每丛青翠的灌木都能让你欣喜！真可爱！
You're always so delighted with every green bush! It's so sweet!
Jubilost：唉，在找到各种可食用生物的同时，附近也往往有别的生物打算吃掉我们……
Alas, the variety of edible life forms is usually found near life forms ready to eat us...
LowSpell
Kalikke：这些法术有自己的意志……我与生俱来的元素力量就不一样，它们永远不会离开我！
These spells have a mind of their own... My inherent elemental powers are another thing. – They will never leave me!
Tristian：别寄希望于永恒，世上没什么是永恒的，即便是你认为亘古不变的事物。
Don't rely on false hope. Nothing in this world lasts forever, even things you think are rock-solid.
NoBuilding
Kalikke：我们就像一块被旅人随手丢在外面的面包——还有一群鸟在头顶盘旋。
We're out here like a piece of bread, cast aside by a careless traveler — with a flock of birds circling overhead.
Jubilost：很高兴队伍里还有明事理的同伴，而我们中有些人根本看不到危险。
It's good to see reasonable companions in this party. Some of us simply cannot understand reason.
NoHunting
Kalikke：在荒漠里吃干粮我没意见，但在这片丰饶之地，明明只要伸伸手就能获得大把新鲜食物！
We can make do with rations in the desert. But in these fertile lands, one need only reach out a hand to find fresh food!
Jubilost：这叫未雨绸缪，补给品可以让我们在打猎失败后不至于挨饿。
It's called forethought. Supplies will keep us from starving in case a hunt ends in failure!
InDungeon
Kalikke：这里很危险，谁知道黑暗深处暗藏着什么？不过肯定很有趣！
It's not safe here. Who knows what's hiding in these dark depths? But at least it promises to be interesting!
Kanerah：我在雷斯托夫的接待会上结交了一些有价值的人，其中梅戈・瓦恩令我印象深刻，他可真是人中龙凤！
I made some valuable acquaintances at the reception in Restov. Maegar Varn I remember well. Truly a lion of a man!
Kanerah：当然了，他远比不上我们的首领。直觉告诉我，他最多只能做到地方男爵，不过他还是有点魅力的！
Of course, he doesn't hold a candle to our own leader. Intuition tells me he will rise no higher than a provincial baron. But he has his charms!
Kanerah：尊敬的朱比罗斯特，请问能让我看看你的地图吗？昨天我路过你时瞄了一眼，注意到有个地方或许能开辟出新的商路！
Most honorable Jubilost, do you mind letting me see your maps? Yesterday I looked over your shoulder in passing, and I noticed a place for a possible new caravan route!
Jubilost：啊哼，当然，拿去看吧！难得有人想看这个！
Ahem, ahem, of course, take them! Finally someone with eyes to see!
Kanerah：朱比罗斯特大师，我很好奇你为什么要绘制一张失窃之地的地图——时机也如此恰好？这片区域鲜为人知，却极具发展潜力……不管是谁率先为它制出地图，都会名垂青史！
I am curious, master Jubilost, how you decided to start making a map of the Stolen Lands — and at such a perfect time? A little-known region, but one ready for intensive development... And whoever makes a good map first, writes his name in history!
Jubilost：这就是科学家的直觉，小姑娘。判断要在哪里投入你的才能和资源是件非常严肃的事情！
It was a scientist's intuition, young lady. To judge correctly where to invest your talent and resources is a very serious task!
Kanerah：我不知道该如何看待失窃之地的种种麻烦，这些动荡给我们带来难得的机遇，同时这片混乱也折磨着我，有时甚至真的要搭上性命……
I don't know what to think about all the troubles in the Stolen Lands. On one hand, they open exciting opportunities for us. But all this chaos is killing me. Sometimes literally…
Jubilost：这就是我们作为文明边境拓荒者所要付出的代价。危险无处不在，威胁着我们的生命，怪物、强盗……别忘了还有那些该死的蚊子！
I's the price we pay for being the pioneers on the frontier of civilization. There are many things here that threaten our lives. Monsters, bandits… and don't forget the damned mosquitoes!
Kanerah：有人知道嘉曼蒂的真正目标吗？我听说过许多关于布雷沃自由和独立的漂亮话，但她真正想要的到底是什么呢？
Does anybody really understand what Jamandi is fighting for? We've heard many fine words about freedom and independence from Brevoy, but what does she truly hope to gain?
Jubilost：绝不能当亲爱的纳塔拉・瑟托瓦的傀儡，同时守护她那剑爵的荣耀！
Not being the puppet of dear Natala Surtova. And protecting her mighty swordlord pride!
Kanerah：我超想知道你的书里是怎么写我的……
I'm dying to know how I come out in your book...
Linzi：你知道吗……你太神秘了！而且反复多变！写你简直就像在写两个不同的角色，而不是一个。
You know... You're so mysterious! And controversial! It's almost like I'm writing two different characters, not one.
Kanerah：行啊！谁趁我睡觉时在我尾巴上系了个铃铛？！
Right! Who tied a bell to my tail while I was asleep?!
Linzi：哈哈，嘻嘻嘻，哇哈哈唔！……不是我干的！
Ha-ha, tee-hee-hee, wah-ha-ha-hm! ... It wasn't me!
Kanerah：我真庆幸那晚顺道拜访了嘉曼蒂女士，我们玩得很开心，烧死了几个刺客，还结交了贵人……
I'm so happy I dropped by Lady Jamandi's that evening. We had some fun, burnt a few assassins, and made a valuable acquaintance...
Linzi：你有什么好开心的？确实，如果没有这回事，我们可能不会相遇，但那晚可有不少人丧命！
What are you so happy about? It's true, we might not have met otherwise, but a lot of people died that night!
Kanerah：崔斯提安，你的神殿属于莎恩芮信众中的哪个分支？
What branch of Sarenrae's followers did your temple belong to, Tristian?
Tristian：我的神殿与其他神殿相隔甚远，也不属于任何派系——如果你问的是这个的话。
My temple was far away from others, and didn't belong to any factions — if that's what you mean.
Kanerah：你是我见过的第一个不知道怎样讨价还价的喀勒什人……
...You are the first keleshite I've met who doesn't know how to haggle...
Tristian：我从来不愿与人争论。跟商贩议价，无异于指责对方不够诚实，而我相信人心向善。
I never like crossing words with anyone. Trying to argue over prices is like telling the merchant that you think they're being dishonest. And I believe in the goodness in people.
Kanerah：我曾认为莎恩芮的凡人信徒都是狂信者、骗徒与伪君子，现在我知道了，就连她的天界仆从也尽是些懦夫和叛徒。
I used to think there were only fanatics, liars and hypocrites among Sarenrae's mortal followers. Now I know that even her celestial servants are cowards and traitors.
Tristian：你可以评判我，对我来说也并不冤枉。但不要因为我的个人过错，就对我所有同伴和信仰妄下定论。
You are right to judge me, and I deserve these words. But don't judge all my companions and all my faith by my own failings.
Kanerah：善心、仁慈……知道我为什么鄙视这些东西吗？因为它们永远靠不住，规则和契约就实在得多——你能预判事情的走向，便能依此行动。
Kindness, mercy… Do you know why I despise these things? Because you can never rely on them. Rules and contracts are much better — you know what to expect and you can act accordingly.
Tristian：你想用一套规则和条件来定义仁慈？恐怕不行……
You'd like a list of rules and conditions defining mercy? I'm afraid it's not how it works…
生日祝福
Kanerah：让我们举杯庆祝！今天是我们首领的生日！
Let us raise our flasks! On this day the one who gathered us here was born!
Kanerah：你已登上权力的高位——现在请允许我祝愿你免受君王的孤独宿命所困！若无人分享，再豪华的宫殿也不过是一间破败的陋室！
You climbed the heights of power. – Now allow me to wish you be spared loneliness, the bane of monarchs! Even the most luxurious palace will seem a dreary hut, if you've no one to share it with!
Shandra
Kanerah：我们的布雷沃使者珊德拉・墨维真是厉害，手段果决又头脑冷静！如果她再年轻二十岁，说不定我会爱上她。不过以防万一，我可能也会暗中杀掉她，。
Our Brevan emissary, Shandra Mervey, is quite incredible. What a steely grip, and what a cold mind! I'd fall in love if she were twenty years younger. Although I might murder her quietly and secretly, just in case.
Linzi：但她是我们的盟友！不是吗？不是吗？
But she is our ally! Isn't she? Isn't she?
C2_Lebeda
Kanerah：兰德尔・勒贝达，年少轻狂又天真的贵族继承人，是老猎手的绝佳猎物。
So, Lander Lebeda. Young and arrogant, naive heir to a rich and noble family. Excellent prey for a skilled hunter... or huntress.
Linzi：每次我看到他，都想一拳打在他鼻子上。下回我可能真的会带把凳子去王座厅，实践我的想法！
I want to punch him in the nose every time I see him. Next time I might just bring a stool to the throne room and do it!
C31_FWFeedback
Kanerah：真奇怪，第一世界就像荒漠里的绿洲，充满生机。但它的力量中暗藏疯狂，令我心生畏惧。
So strange. The First World reminds me of an oasis in the middle of the desert, a riot of life. But there's madness in its power. It frightens me.
Tristian：宇宙浩瀚无垠，有些地方，我们如何穷尽心力都难以理解。第一世界就是这样的地方。
The universe is vast, and there are places we will find hard to understand, no matter how hard we try. The First World is such a place.
C5_Evindra
Kanerah：我一直在想我们从水泽仙女伊文德拉那里了解到的事，她和无面姐妹曾深爱并信任着那个欺骗她们的人。
I can't stop thinking about what we learned from Evindra the nereid. She and the Defaced Sisters loved and trusted the one who deceived them.
Tristian：妮瑞萨懂得如何蛊魅人心，但她代表着纯粹的邪恶，是化作血肉之躯的毁灭之力。
Nyrissa has her charm. But she's pure evil, a destructive force of nature made flesh.
DeadInParty
Kanerah：队伍里人越少，剩下的人就越容易受伤……
The smaller the party, the more vulnerable are the ones who remain…
Tristian：你的言语中尽是对同伴死亡的漠然。虽说自保是本分，但也应该心怀悲悯，哀悼我们逝去的同伴。
Your words are full of indifference to the deaths of others. We'll take care of ourselves — but let us show some compassion, and grieve our losses.
Debuffed
Kanerah：我精神之火即将燃尽……让我躺在篝火边上。
The flames of my spirit flicker and burn low... I'll lie next to the fire.
Linzi：大家都想靠着篝火！你挪过去点！
Everyone wants to be next to the fire! Scoot over, you!
Fatigued
Kanerah：我今天不该这么拼的。要是今晚遭敌人袭击，我会乞求他们，‘晚点再来吧，我还想再睡半小时！’
I shouldn't have exerted myself so much today. If any enemies attack us tonight, I'll beg them, 'Come back later. — I need another half hour of sleep!'
Jubilost：小姑娘，我十分确信，当你说出这番话时，会让我们的敌人无地自容！他们会低着头狼狈地爬回去！
I'm quite sure, young lady, that you will speak those words in such a voice that will make our enemies rightly ashamed of themselves! They'll bow their heads and quietly crawl back to wherever they came from!
InDungeon
Kanerah：真是个奇特的睡觉地点，至少我们不用担心淋雨——至于真正该担心什么，只有地底诸神才知道了！
An interesting place to sleep. We need not fear rain, at least. – Only the gods of the underworld could tell us what we should be afraid of instead!
Jubilost：放轻松，小姑娘！你的乐观哪去了？至少对金子的热爱还在吧？凡是有危险的地方，往往也藏着财富……当然我们也可能一无所获，除了怪物、尘土和蜘蛛！
Easy there, young lady! Where is your optimism? Or at least your love of gold? Wherever there is danger, there may also be riches to be found... Though of course we might also find nothing but monsters, dust, and spiders!
LowHealth
Kanerah：我大意了——那些豺狼让我付出了代价，但我们让他们偿还得更多！
I was careless. — And those jackals made me pay for it. But we made them pay more dearly!
Jubilost：你管那叫‘大意’？我看是‘犯傻’还差不多！
You call that 'carelessness'? I call it 'outright foolishness!'
LowRations
Kanerah：我们的存粮已经见底，就如同晚餐时间的空荡教堂。希望我们已经有补充补给的对策！
Our food stores are as empty as a church at dinner time. I hope we have a plan for replenishing our supplies!
Tristian：教堂时常接济饥民，晚餐时间反而挤满了人。
Churches often feed the hungry, and are full during dinner time.
LowSpell
Kanerah：魔法既不可靠、又易反噬。我真不应该依赖它，毕竟我的元素力量始终与我同在！
Magic is a lazy friend, and a treacherous ally. I shouldn't have relied on it, when my elemental powers are always with me!
Linzi：哇，我能把这段话记下来吗？它跟我正在写的内容真是绝配！
Ooh, can I write that down? It's perfect for an idea I'm working on!
NoBuilding
Kanerah：我们身处毫无遮蔽的空地上，一览无余。但愿负责值守的人不要走神！
We're out here in the open, for all to see. I hope those keeping watch don't let their minds wander!
Tristian：你无法逃避命运，但我们仍会保持警惕。
You cannot hide from your own fate. But we will keep our eyes open.
NoHunting
Kanerah：得有人去打猎……别看我——我来自一片沙漠，那里能猎的只有风滚草！
Someone needs to go hunting... But don't look at me. — In the desert where I'm from, there's nothing to hunt but tumbleweed!
Jubilost：我相信你的才智和直觉，哈哈！
I have faith in your ingenuity — and your gut instincts, ha ha!
Linzi：总有一天，我会和安娜梅德·贝拉瓦拉一样出名……不，我会比她更出名！唉，希望我能够活到那个时候！
Someday I will be as famous as Annamede Belavarah herself... No, even more famous! Heh, hoping I live that long!
Linzi：我想要坐在自己的图书馆里，写一写我的回忆录……一旁有人给我递上一杯热茶……
I'd like to sit at a desk in my own library, writing memoirs… with someone bringing me a nice cup of hot tea...
Linzi：啊，我这是在骗自己吧？我这个人根本坐不住。
Oh, who am I kidding? I wouldn't be able to sit still for a day.
Linzi：刀剑铿锵、伤心绝望、沉默立誓、英勇坚强……
Clashing swords, hours despairing, vows made in silence, hoping and daring...
Linzi：啊！这个韵脚太差劲了！我熟知所有的词汇，但是为什么把它们组织在一起就这么难呢？
Ugh! That rhyme is rubbish! I know all the words – why's it so hard to put them together?
Linzi：我在想这个说书人是谁？他很奇怪，明明是个精灵，看上去却年老体衰，而且还是个瞎子，而且他还不喜欢跟我们分享故事，真是个小气鬼！
Who is this Storyteller, I wonder? He's weird — an elf, but looks old. And he's blind. And he doesn't like to share stories, what a miser!
Linzi：那么他是谁呢？整个葛拉利昂最年迈的精灵？还是一名受到诅咒的琼宁王子？哦，还是一名失忆的神灵？我太激动了，都睡不着了，可恶啊。
So who is he? The oldest elf on whole Golarion? Or an enchanted prince of Kyonin? Oh, maybe he's a god suffering a memory loss? I'm so excited I won't be able to sleep tonight, damn it.
Kalikke&Kanerah
Linzi：在我们行进时，我听到你在唱歌！实在太动听了！为什么不趁现在给我们唱一首呢？
I heard you singing as we walked! So beautiful! Why don't you sing for us now?
Kalikke：我的歌和你们的不一样，亲爱的琳翠。我为自己而唱，就像沙漠中的旅者，脸上蒙着好几层用来挡风沙的布，骑在骆驼背上长途跋涉时歌唱一样。
My singing is not like yours, sweet Linzi. I sing for myself, as travelers sing in the desert, during a long journey on a camel's back, with my face covered by three layers of cloth, to hold back the sandy wind.
Linzi：凯希，你那走进传送门后再现身的花招是怎么办到的？我从没见过那样的场面！
Kaessi, what is that trick where you go into a portal and then appear again? I've never seen anything like it!
Kalikke/Kanerah：那是……我在故乡学到的一种特殊魔法把戏。我会到元素位面去补充我的力量！
It's... a special magic trick I learned in my homeland. I travel through the elemental plane to renew my strength!
Linzi：你怎么能什么都瞒着我们呢？！你们是怎么以这种奇怪的方式连接起来的？你确定你们只有两个，而不是——我随便说个数——四个吗？你们就一个身体？你们的祖父真的是个魔鬼？！
How could you hide everything from us?! How did you become connected in this strange way? Are you sure there are only two of you, and not, like, four? Do you have one body? Is your grandfather really a devil?!
Kalikke：我们并不总能自由选择向他人坦白什么、隐瞒什么，亲爱的琳翠。但很抱歉我没法告诉你实情。
We aren't always free to choose what to reveal to others and what we must hide, sweet Linzi. But I'm sorry I couldn't tell you the truth.
Kanerah：你嘴里那一串连珠炮似的问题，就是我们从不和别人谈论自己的原因之一。
The waterfall of questions pouring from your mouth is one of the reasons we never tell anyone anything about ourselves.
Linzi：你能用你的角顶人吗？
Can you butt people with your horns?
Kalikke/Kanerah：我当然能。只要某个好奇的小吟游诗人肯帮忙，我随时可以展示……
I certainly can. And I'm prepared to demonstrate, with the help of a curious little bard...
Linzi：这种又小又红的甜浆果叫做草莓，而那种蓬松的黄花是蒲公英！噢，我喜欢给你解释东西！这让我觉得自己非常博学和重要！
The small, red, sweet berries are called strawberries. And the fluffy yellow flower is a dandelion! Oh, I like to explain things to you! I feel so knowledgeable and important!
Kalikke：唔，草莓！亲爱的琳翠，没了你我可怎么办呀？
Mmm, strawberries! Sweet Linzi, what would I do without you?
Linzi：很好，新的台词。‘他们头上顶着闪亮的钢盔，勇敢前行……’
All right, new line. 'They walked without fear, hoisting shining steel above their heads…'
Linzi：然后他们不断前行，一直前行……嘿，他们到底什么时候才会到达他们的目的地啊，嗯？
And they walked and they walked... Heh, when were they going to get anywhere already, hunh?
Kalikke/Kanerah：钢盔？顶在头上？可是为什么？他们是怕遇到空袭吗？还是他们想用刀剑的闪光打暗号?
Steel? Above their heads? But why? Were they frightened of an aerial assault? Or were they using their blades to flash a signal?
Linzi：唉，现在要是能有一杯热的格罗格酒，我愿意用任何东西来交换。要是能给我来两杯……
Heh, I'd give anything for a mug of hot grog. And for two mugs...
Linzi：不，要是喝两杯，他们就会把我赶出酒馆的。为什么我在学院的时候没有学会喝酒？
No, after two mugs they'd kick me out of the tavern. How come I never learned how to drink at the Academy?
Kalikke/Kanerah：在营地最好别冒险喝两杯——只要你有一丁点松懈，怪物就肯定会跳出来。不过等咱们回了城里，可以顺便去趟艾莉娜的酒馆。我很乐意奉陪！
It's better not to risk two mugs at camp. – As soon as you relax a little, the monsters are sure to show up. But when we return to the city, we can drop by Elina's tavern. I'll be happy to keep you company!
Linzi：你的战斗技巧挺厉害的。和我曾经读到过的一个英雄，盖伊·夏色尔·德·隆巴尔一样厉害。但是你缺少他的风范，他的气势。等你击败了邪恶的敌人以后，可不可以摆出一个充满英雄气质的造型？我觉得这样做可以为我们的队伍赢得大量的名誉！
You fight well! Almost as good as Guy Shaseur De Lombair, a hero I've read about. But you lack his style, his dignity. Could you make an epic pose once you have dispatched your wicked opponent? I think that would go a long way to help our group's fame!
Amiri：要不然我来负责杀敌，你来负责唱歌做梦如何？把你的这些大话都留给崔斯提安吧，我对你的书不感兴趣。
How about I do the killing, and you do the singing and dreaming? And save the speeches for Tristian, I have no interest in your books.
Linzi：目前的战绩是这样的：‘今天安米芮杀掉了十只狗头人、一只巨魔、一只刺蜥兽、还有两只手的手指头都数不清的地精！’您知道吗……我觉得我们的读者可能不会相信这些数据。
So, here's what we got so far: 'Today Amiri killed ten kobolds, a troll, a hodag, and more goblins than the fingers on two hands can count!' You know... I don't think our readers are going to believe all this.
Amiri：不过这是真的呀！我可以轻松把他们都砍死。只是我们要先找到这么多的敌人。
But it's the truth! I could cut them all down easy. We just need to find them!
Linzi：我听说在一个遥远的地方，有一个部落喜欢扔原木来当做娱乐休闲活动。能够把原木扔得越远，就代表你越强壮。
I heard that in a far away tribe people entertain themselves by throwing huge logs. Whoever throws it the farthest is the strongest.
Amiri：扔原木？哈！真是一群软蛋。猛犸大王部落的人都扔猛犸象！我曾经一次举起来过两头！
Throwing logs? Ha! What weaklings. The Mammoth Lords lift mammoths! I used to lift two at once!
Linzi：安米芮，你有没有数过你杀死了多少敌人？
Amiri, have you ever tried to count how many enemies you've killed?
Amiri：很多！都没有那么大的数字可以算！要是有人想要数清，他们就必须要发明新的数字！
A lot! There aren't even such high numbers! If someone wanted to count that high, they'd have to start making up new numbers a long time ago!
Linzi：我在想，猛犸大王部落是不是有自己的文字？他们一定有很多故事可以写……
I wonder, do Mammoth Lords have their own writing system? They must have much to tell...
Amiri：我的同胞的传奇代代相传。如果有人没留下传说，那说明他就是个弱者，因为这个人没什么好说的。
The legends of my people are passed from generation to generation. If someone doesn't get into a legend – then he's a weakling and there's nothing to talk about.
Linzi：嘿，安米芮，你在想什么？
Hey, Amiri, what are you thinking about?
Amiri：呃……呃呃呃……我在想，如果你把一个半身人的腿打折，他是不是就变成四分之一身人了？
Gr-r-r... I'm thinking, if you tear off a halfling's legs, would she be a quarterling?
Linzi：你干掉那些巨魔的手法简直妙极了！你和故事里的英雄人物简直一模一样。我感觉我们一定能创下丰功伟业，就像是英雄盖伊和阿维斯坦大骑士们一样。你给我的感觉和他有一点相似……
The way you finished off those trolls was spectacular! You really have what it takes to be a hero just like in the stories. I feel like we are making a difference, just like Guy and the Archknights of Avistan. You sort of remind me of him a little...
Ekun：我必须要这样做。他们违背了法律，而现在他们付出了代价。在这片蛮荒的土地上，这是我们所能选择的最接近于正义的做法。我觉得没问题。
I did what I had to. They broke the law and now they have paid the price. Here in this wild land, it is as close to justice as we are likely to see. And that is fine by me.
Linzi：盖伊·夏色尔·德·隆巴尔和你一样，也是一名森林居民。不过他选择使用两把精灵曲剑，而不是弓箭。他的同伴贝洛克努尔是一名高阶骑士，而他则是一名技艺精湛的神射手，传说他可以从百米外射掉蚊蝠的翅膀……
Guy Shaseur De Lombair was a woodsman not unlike you. However, he favored dual elven curve blades instead of the bow. Beloq-Nur was his fellow Archknight, and he was an expert marksmen. It is said that he could shoot the wings off a stirge at a hundred meters...
Ekun：胡说八道……既然他射术如此精湛，那为什么不干脆杀死那只蚊蝠？
Minotaur shit... Even if he was that good with a bow, why not just kill the stirge?
Linzi：你是从苏维亚来的吗？那个地方怎么样？那里的文化是什么样的？那里的东西好吃吗？有没有什么特别的仪式？那里离得远吗？有没有什么奇异的动物？有什么精彩的故事吗？
Are you from Thuvia? Is it nice there? What's the culture like? Do you have good food? Special ceremonies? Is it far? Do you have any exotic animals? Any interesting stories?
Ekun：是的。
Yes.
Linzi：哦，艾昆！我还记得你为我们猎杀的那只麋鹿。我特别喜欢你去给我们打猎的时候，你总是能打回来特别美味的猎物！
Oh, Ekun! I still remember that elk you downed for us. I love it when you hunt — you always bring back something delicious!
Ekun：我……嗯……嗯……嗯。谢谢？
I... mmm... hmmm... hm. Thank you?
Linzi：艾昆，你的灵魂里除了仇恨真的没有别的东西了吗？
Ekun, do you really have nothing left in your soul but revenge?
Ekun：有仇恨就够了。我不需要别的东西。
It's enough. I don't want anything else.
Linzi：艾昆，我想说……对于你的家人所发生的一切，我真的很难过。不过生活还要继续！
Ekun, I meant to say... I'm truly sorry all this happened to your family. But life isn't over!
Ekun：那是艾昆要做的事。那天夜里他已经死了。现在和你谈话的是另一个人。过去的艾昆也不会认得的一个人。
For that Ekun it is. He died that night. The man you're talking to now is a different man. The old Ekun wouldn't even recognize him.
Linzi：艾昆，你长大的那个村子……请给我讲讲那里是什么样的！
Ekun, the village you lived in... Tell me what it was like!
Ekun：那里很不错。人们诚实善良，食物很新鲜，天空也很晴朗……有着人们所梦寐以求的一切。你可以把这段写进书里。
It was good. Honest people, good food, clean sky... Everything a person needs. You can write that down.
Linzi：你总是很阴沉、很安静……读者就喜欢你这样的神秘角色！
You're so sullen, quiet... Readers like mysterious characters like you!
Ekun：我并不是一个角色。也不是一个传说中的英雄。我只希望这一切能够快点结束。
I'm not a character. Not a hero from legend. I just want everything to be over.
Linzi：你的射术真不错！告诉我你有什么诀窍？
You shoot so well! Tell me, what's your secret?
Ekun：我拿起弓，拉开弓弦，瞄准，然后把箭射出去。没有诀窍。
I take the bow, draw the string, aim. Shoot. No secret.
Linzi：我能喂你的狗吃点东西吗？他太可爱了！
Can I feed your dog? He's so sweet!
Ekun：他是头狼，不是只狗。而且他会自己捕食。我们就是这么相处的。
He's a wolf, not a dog. And he feeds himself. It's how we do things.
Linzi：你为什么如此擅长追踪各种蛛丝马迹？就好像你能和地面交谈似的！你到底在地上找什么？是某种魔法吗？
How did you get so good at reading tracks? It's like the ground talks to you! What do you look for? Is it some kind of magic?
Ekun：我在观察地面，一切都很明显。多看，少问。然后你就会明白了。
I look at the ground and everything is clear. Look more and ask less. – Then you'll understand.
Linzi：艾昆，你是从安布萨朗来的，对吗？但是你说话为什么有口音？
Ekun, you're from Absalom, right? Why do you have an accent?
Ekun：我的故乡是苏维亚。我的母语是欧西里昂语。我是在已经学会说话很久之后才搬到安布萨朗的。
My homeland is Thuvia. My native language is Osiriani. I moved to Absalom long after I learned how to speak.
Linzi：你能不能给我讲一个你的旅行故事？我想要把它写下来！
Will you tell me a story from your travels? I'd love to write it down!
Ekun：你的声音太吵了。这样你会吸引来许多猎食者和怪物。故事还是等我们到安全的地方以后再讲吧。
You make a lot of noise. You're liable to draw predators or monsters. Let's save our stories for later, when we are somewhere safe.
Kalikke/Kanerah：我的旅行经验没那么丰富。从卡蒂拉到布雷沃有些熙来攘往的商路，我不过是沿着这些路走，中途到处歇歇脚罢了。
I'm not such an experienced traveler. There are well-trodden caravan roads from Qadira to Brevoy, and I just followed them, staying here and there along the way.
Linzi：哈瑞姆，你真的喜欢让自己不重要吗？像是你的整个人生都毫不重要似的？
Harrim, do you really prefer to feel insignificant? Like your whole life is useless?
Harrim：我喜不喜欢？嗯……有关系吗？只要这是真实的，那就够了，写书的小子。我来这里是为了了解真相，我会谦卑地接受它，然后和其他人分享。然而你始终在用肤浅的希望故事宽慰你自己！
Do I like it? Hmm-m-m... Does it matter? All that matters is that this is the truth, little storyteller. I came to learn the truth, I humbly accepted it, and now I share it with others. Yet you keep consoling yourself with shallow tales of hope!
Linzi：是谁在这儿闷闷不乐的呀？像一头饿了三天的巨魔似的。哈！是臭脸的老哈瑞姆！
Who's so sullen there, like a troll after starving for three days? Ha! – It's grumpy old Harrim!
Harrim：唉，琳翠……这支队伍里全是做白日梦的人和自欺欺人的人，必须要有人能够提防永恒的来临。
Alas, Linzi... Someone in this party of dreamers and make-believers must be mindful of the eternal.
Linzi：你知道吗，哈瑞姆……我觉得你的格罗图斯并不关心他的信徒。崇拜他有什么用？
You know, Harrim… I don't think your Groetus cares about his congregation. What's the point in worshipping him?
Harrim：那么崇敬其他神祇又有什么用呢？所有神灵都向他们的信徒承诺一些毫无意义的东西。快乐、战争、魔法……而格罗图斯则教导我们要带着尊严接受那终将到来的末日。
And what's the point in venerating the other gods? All they promise their followers is empty fuss. Pleasure, war, magic... For his part, Groetus teaches us to accept the inevitable with dignity.
Linzi：你一直在说末日的到来，说我们都会死……你从来就没有想过要找些乐子吗？
You constantly talk about the end of times, that we'll all die... Don't you ever just want to have some fun?
Harrim：当我看着你们徒劳地想要躲开那不可避免的末日，那就是我的快乐。因为真的很可笑。
I have fun when I watch your futile attempts to avoid the inevitable. It's truly funny.
Linzi：我问你，哈瑞姆，你有没有想过要回到五王山脉去？重新拿起铁匠的锻锤？再次感受到熔炉的炙热？
Listen, Harrim, would you ever want to return to the Five Kings Mountains? To take up a hammer again? To feel the heat of the furnace?
Harrim：不要再去揭开旧伤疤。我已经找到了我的道路，而我不会再偏离轨道。那片山脉已经不再是我的故乡了，即便那里还有人希望我回去。
Don't reopen old wounds. I've found my path, and I'm not turning from it. The mountains are my home no more, even if someone would like to see me there.
Linzi：你有没有想过把格罗图斯的教义写下来？如果真的很有道理，那为什么不和别人分享呢？
Have you ever thought about writing down the teachings of Groetus? If it's all so wise, why not share it with others?
Harrim：写在纸上的不一定是真相，只是飘落的灰尘罢了。凡是注定能理解格罗图斯智慧的人绝不会从书本上学习到他的教诲。
What can be written on paper is not the truth, but only the ashes falling from it. Anyone destined to understand Groetus' wisdom will not learn it from a book.
Linzi：我正在写一首有关盖伊·夏色尔·德·隆巴尔的歌。他……给我带来了灵感。
I am writing a song about Guy Shaseur De Lombair. He... inspires me.
Jaethal：你听上去有些不情愿。在我的心还依然会为这样的爱恋而跳动的时候，我喜欢身材高大的爱人。你不是提到过有个挥舞着斧子的高大凯利德人吗？但是现在我觉得这样的人很美味，你懂我的意思的……
You sound a bit smitten. I preferred large lovers when my heart still beat for such trysts. Wasn't there a large, axe wielding Kellid that you mentioned? Now he sounds delicious, if you know what I mean...
Linzi：耶萨尔，你……你不仅是冷漠，你简直是冰冷无情！就像是冬天的夜晚，没有月亮也没有星星！
Jaethal, you... You're not just cold – you are icy! A winter's night, without the moon and stars!
Jaethal：你这是想要夸我吗？小姑娘，我就是我。我不在意短命种族对我的看法，就像我不会在意林间吹过的风一样。
Is that an attempted compliment? Girl, I am what I am. The opinions of the short-lived interest me even less than the howling of the wind in the tree branches.
Linzi：耶萨尔和鲜血之河……耶萨尔和破碎的心……不……相比于去伤别人的心，她更喜欢把别人的心挖出来……
Jaethal and the Rivers of Blood... Jaethal and the Broken Hearts... No... She prefers ripping them out over breaking them...
Jaethal：如果你非得在你的乱写乱画里提到我，你能不能别用我的真名？我可不希望自己和廉价小说扯在一起。
If you must mention me in your scribbles, would you care to replace my name? I've no wish to be associated with cheap novels.
Linzi：为什么你的女神让你成为她的奴仆，却不告诉你她究竟想要做什么？
How come your goddess made you her servant, but didn't explain what she wanted you to do?
Jaethal：为什么你非要用这样的问题来考验我的耐心？你没必要为了体会骨折的感觉就把手指头伸向捕鼠器里。
How come you persist in trying my patience with such questions? One shouldn't put one's fingers into a mousetrap, just to learn how their bones crack.
Linzi：看我看我，我是耶萨尔！哇！我是一只恐怖的不死精灵，我的镰刀在哪里？啊呀呀，乌伽娑婀会赐予你死亡，为你带来启发！你们都如此可悲，你们这些可笑的短命鬼……哎呀……
Look at me, I am Jaethal! Boo! I am a horrifying undead elf – where's my scythe?! Aaaah, Urgathoa will give you the wisdom of death! You are so pathetic, you ridiculous short-lived... Oops.
Jaethal：给我小心点。我可能会把你变成我私人拥有的小丑。让你永远永远给我一个人讲笑话。
Careful. I might make you into my own private jester. You'll be telling me your jokes forever and ever.
Linzi：你是如何做到把这些都藏在心里的？你是如何做到这样残酷的？
How do you keep all this in your heart? How could you be so cruel?
Jaethal：残酷？小姑娘，不要把我当成怪物或者疯子了。我可不是把自己的快乐建立在别人的痛苦上。我并不是靠杀戮和虐待来为自己取乐。我只是很清楚我想要什么，而且对其他东西都不感兴趣。
Cruel? Don't confuse me with monsters and maniacs, girl. I don't enjoy the suffering of others. I don't maim and kill for fun. I just know what I want, and I don't care about the rest.
Linzi：朱比罗斯特，跟我说说。你写的书获得如此巨大的成功，有什么诀窍吗？
Jubilost, talk to me. What's the secret of the incredible success of your books?
Jubilost：哈！你想要知道我是如何彻夜不眠、反复失败又笔耕不辍的吗？好吧，没人阻止你去买一本《朱比罗斯特·纳斯罗泊写作的秘密》来看看……
Ha! You wish to know about sleepless nights, failed experiments, and painstaking work? Well nothing is preventing you from buying 'The Secrets of Writing by Jubilost Narthropple'....
Linzi：你的书举世闻名，所有人都会读，甚至在有些地方还遭到了封禁……你是如何获得这样的认可的？有什么诀窍吗？
Your books are known everywhere, read everywhere – even forbidden in some places... How did you achieve such recognition? Or is it a secret?
Jubilost：小姑娘，没有诀窍。首先，你要付出大量的努力。夜以继日，不眠不休，孜孜不倦。除此之外……我有天分。
It's no secret, girl. First of all, it's a lot of hard work. Day and night, without closing your eyes, tireless. And second of all... I'm just talented.
Linzi：我看过了你关于香料的论文……古伊萨·诺德库亚妮是谁，为什么她的名字会在封面上，跟你并列？
I've looked through your Essays On Spices... Who is this Guisa Nordequanni and why is her name on the cover next to yours?
Jubilost：因为有些法官都是贪污腐败的混蛋，根本不尊重版权！狗屁的合著！她只是偶尔来帮我打磨笔尖！
Because some judges are corrupt scoundrels who know nothing about copyright! Co-author indeed! She scarcely sharpened my quills!
Linzi：朱比罗斯特，跟我讲讲神秘学院的事，那里是什么样子？你觉得我会不会喜欢那里？
Jubilost, tell me about the College of Mysteries – what's it like? Do you think I'd like it there?
Jubilost：你？当然会啦。不过你懂的，他们也不是什么人都收！即便是在那里找一份清洁工的工作，你也必须要先发表一系列的著作！
Would you? Surely. But you know, they don't just accept anyone! Even to get a cleaning job there, you have to submit a list of publications!
Linzi：嘿……我是不是永远也不可能像你一样出名啊？
Heh... Will I really never be as famous as you?
Jubilost：像我一样出名……可能是做不到。不过不要绝望，你的面前还有一整段人生要去度过！请永远记住，一定要不断地写作！而且永远不要把你的著作束之高阁！你写的任何东西都是要给人阅读、给人去讨论的！不管是得到夸赞还批判……那都不重要！
As famous as me – probably not. But don't despair, you have your whole life ahead of you! Always remember – keep writing! And never for the desk drawer! Write everything for the purposes of being read and discussed! Praised, cursed – that part doesn't matter!
Linzi：那么你在做什么？为新书写一些笔记？
And what are you doing? Writing notes for a new book?
Jubilost：我在画一幅地图。如果我们迷路了，必须要有人能够指明方向。
I'm drawing a map. Someone needs to know where to go when we finally get lost.
Linzi：跟我讲一讲你的过去吧。你是在哪里长大的，你的童年是什么样的？
Tell me about your past. Where did you grow up, what kind of childhood did you have?
Jubilost：这些事情都写在我的自传里了。去图书馆找一本来看看。就在‘朱比罗斯特’区。
It's all in my autobiography. Look it up in the library. It's in the 'Jubilost' section.
Linzi：为什么你是如此闷闷不乐呢，诺克诺克! 我欺负你了吗？
Why, why are you so obnoxious, Nok-Nok! What have I done to you?
NokNok：琳翠翠总是在说话。说太多话了。脑子里全是话。不喜欢，所以请安静，拜托拜托！
Linzee talks all the time. Many extra words. Head full of words. Don't want that, so please be quiet please please!
Linzi：诺克诺克，你为什么发出那种嘶嘶的声音，而且你为什么把武器也拔出来了？
Nok-Nok, why did you make that hissing sound — and why are your weapons out?
NokNok：书。琳翠翠的书。我看到它在看我，好像要攻击我……所以我要先下手为强。
Book. Linzee's book. I see it looking at me, like it about to attack – but not if I get it first.
Linzi：哦不！我的草稿！都被撕成碎片了！
Oh no! My drafts! They're torn to shreds!
NokNok：哦，不不不不！琳翠翠的灵感都没了！哼，现在知道不应该写在纸上了吧！
Oh noooo! Linzee lost her think-thoughts! Hnh — now know better than keep on paper!
Linzi：我真的很钦佩盖伊和阿维斯坦大骑士。我希望我们也可以像他们一样，为这个世界做一些好事。但是我们有一些……我也不知道。我们这群人和我想象中那些大骑士们不一样。
I really admire Guy and the Archknights of Avistan. I hope we can do some good in the world like they did. We just seem so... I don't know. We are not the heroes I imagine the Arch-Knights to have been.
Octavia：不要因为我们没书里写的那么好就闷闷不乐嘛。就算那本书写的是真人真事，但是我想也不完全真实吧。别老是把目光放在我们的缺点上，而是要写我们的长处。
Don't be sad just because we don't measure up to some book. Even if it was based on real people, I am sure they didn't always agree. Don't focus on what we struggle with; write your story about what we do well.
Linzi：命运时代真是令人神往啊。
The Age of Destiny was truly fascinating!
Octavia：我最喜欢的时代是满满是肉的食袋。
My favorite age is the saus-age.
Linzi：噢！又漏墨了！而且还是在这一页的最下面！我现在只能把这整篇文章再重写一遍了！
Oh! Another inkblot! And at the very bottom of the page! Now I need to write the whole thing over again!
Octavia：等等！先别撕……让我看看。你知道吗，我们可以接着这滩墨迹画一幅图！这样。这里画条线，那里也来一条……现在我们就可以画出一个……
Wait! Don't tear it out yet... let me see. You know, we could make a picture from this inkblot! There. A line here and there — and now it's a...
Linzi：你是怎么做到发型一直不乱的？我的头发就总是乱糟糟的，像个鸡窝！
And how do you manage to always keep your hair so nice? Mine always sticks out like a nest...
Octavia：那是因为你的脑子里怪念头实在是太多了！这些念头都装不下了，所以你得把它们都写下来。
It's because you have too many thoughts in your head! They won't even all fit, so you have to write them down.
Linzi：你和瑞贡尕是如此不同……不过你知道吗，你们真是天造地设的一对！我都有点嫉妒了。
You and Regongar are so different... But you know, you're a great couple! I'm even a little jealous.
Octavia：不要嫉妒来，来加入我们吧！我们一直很想要试一些新花样呢……哈哈哈，别脸红嘛，我只是开个玩笑！
Don't be jealous, join us! We're always excited to try new things... Ha ha ha, don't blush, I'm just kidding!
Linzi：奥克塔维雅，你有纸吗？不知道哪个混蛋把我备用的笔记本给偷走了。
Octavia, do you have a few pieces of paper? Some scoundrel stole my spare notebook.
Octavia：哦……原来那个本子不是用来生火的吗？我真的很抱歉！我是认真的道歉！
Oh... So that wasn't kindling? I'm so sorry! Of course I do!
Linzi：我们能相遇真是太好了。要是没有你，这里都没人愿意和我聊天了！
It's good that we met. Without you, there wouldn't be anyone to chat with around here!
Octavia：是啊，你话太多了！不过我喜欢。我随时洗耳恭听！
Yes, you're big on chatting! But I'm glad of it too. My pointy ears are always at your disposal!
Linzi：奥克塔维雅！能不能再给我表演一下你的那个火焰法术，就是你在都城用来逗乐那些孩子耍的那招！
Octavia! Show me that fire trick again, the one you did to entertain the children at the capital!
Octavia：你是说真的？你又不是五岁……好吧，不过我要先忙完一件事情，然后再给你表演。
Seriously? You're not five, you know... All right, I have to finish something, but then I'll show you.
Linzi：你是如何在战斗中保持冷静的？我总是会感到非常担忧！要是出了什么差错，我们都被吃掉了怎么办？
How do you manage to keep your cool in battle? I always get terribly worried! What if something goes wrong and we all get eaten up?
Octavia：我也会担忧啊……不过有个勇敢的小吟游诗人一直在用战歌鼓励我！
I'd worry too... But I'm encouraged by the battle songs of brave little bard!
Linzi：这支队伍的表现越来越好了！明天我们一定要更加努力。如果我平铺直叙地描述我们的战斗场面，人们一定会觉得我们是一群外行。你难道就不能摆几个有气势的造型吗，瑞贡尕？你总是脾气那么坏。
This group is shaping up nicely! We just have to try a little harder tomorrow. If I wrote a true account of our battles, people with think we're a bunch of amateurs. Would it hurt to strike a heroic pose now and then, Regongar? You grump.
Regongar：别再说你的英雄了，那个什么盖伊鬼知道叫什么名字的家伙，好吗。琳翠？那只是个故事。没人可以那么厉害的。只有傻子和死人会在战斗的时候装模作样的。别相信这些胡说八道的故事了！
Enough about your hero, Guy-what's-his-face, alright Linzi? It's just a story. No one can be that good. Only idiots and dead men flourish when they fight. Stop with the nonsense already!
Linzi：每当我在战斗中听到电闪雷鸣的声音，我就会四处张望。而当我看到你挂着微笑的脸，被闪电所照亮。而在你的眼中……鲜血、钢铁和利爪。战斗真的能给你带来快乐……
When I hear thunder in the middle of the fight, I always look around. And each time I see your smiling face, lit by the lightning. And in your eyes — blood, steel and claws. Battle brings you true pleasure...
Regongar：哈哈，说的没错。我就喜欢到处甩闪电！因为我喜欢危险。鬼知道为什么……有的时候我觉得我就属于那里……属于白热化的战斗，四周都是野兽和怪物……哈哈。那么晚上吃什么？
Ha, that's true. I do like slinging lightning! And I like danger. Devil knows why... Sometimes I feel I belong there — in the thick of a battle, among the animals and beasts... Ha, ha, hmm. So, what's for dinner?
Linzi：瑞贡尕，你为什么总是那么刻薄？
Regongar, why are you always so mean?
Regongar：温和的人在这个世界上可活不长。我在很多年的鞭笞下学到了这个教训。要是你想活命，那就先下手为强！就是这样！
Kindness doesn't survive this world for long. I was taught that lesson under the whip for many, many years. If you want to live, strike first! That's it!
Linzi：你知道吗，瑞贡尕，我觉得你只是装作愤怒而且粗鲁的样子。但是你的内在非常和善！
You know, Regongar, I think you only try to seem like you're angry and rude. But you're kind on the inside!
Regongar：宝贝儿啊，我的内在和所有人都是一样的，心脏、热血、肠子、还有一肚子的屎。
On the inside, baby, I'm the same as everyone – blood, heart, guts, and a little shit.
Linzi：瑞贡尕，告诉我，你和奥克塔维雅是如此不同……你们是如何能走到一起的？
Regongar, tell me, you and Octavia are so different... What keeps you together?
Regongar：你应该问有什么能把我们分开！我们从小就在一起，被铁链拴在一起。而现在我们获得了自由，所以我们又在一起战斗！
Ask what doesn't! We've been together since we were kids – in chains. And now that we're free, we're together in battle!
Linzi：瑞贡尕，你的部落有什么传说吗？神话？
Regongar, did your tribe have any legends? Myths?
Regongar：等我找到他们，就会有了。屠杀所有人的瑞贡尕的传说。很酷的传说，不是吗？
They will once I track them down. The Legend About Regongar, Who Slaughtered Everyone. Cool legend, right?
Linzi：瑞贡尕，你又在吃什么？额，真恶心！那是什么，老鼠吗？生吃？呃啊啊啊，我以后再也无法忘记这一幕了！
Regongar, what's that you're chewing? Eww, nasty! What is that, a rat? Raw? Aaaagh, how can I forget that I saw that?!
Regongar：哈，你这个软蛋！在纽玫瑞亚，我们要是晚饭能吃上老鼠那就算是改善生活了！而且失窃之地这里的老鼠又肥又嫩，真好吃！
Ha, you wuss! In Numeria we were glad when we had a rat for supper! Besides they're so fat and tender here in the Stolen Lands – yummy!
Linzi：我想问问你，你会不会用你的闪电来……呃，比如说，烤鸭子？串起来烤？
Listen, could you roast, like, a duck – with your lightning? On a skewer?
Regongar：我的闪电是用来殊死战斗用的，不是用来开发新菜品的。不过嘛……我觉得可以一试。也许可以烤熟，也可能会直接烧成渣！
My lightning is for fighting to the death, not for cooking experiments. Although... I could, I guess. Either roast it or burn it to ashes!
Linzi：老瑞，我需要一些灵感。给我来个帅气的造型！
Reg, I need some inspiration. Strike a heroic pose!
Regongar：你需要我摆造型？哈哈哈……我喜欢摆造型。不过我的造型可能会给你一些其他方面的灵感，嘿嘿……
A pose you say? Ha ha ha... I like poses. The ones I like would inspire you for something quite different, though...
Linzi：你知道吗，我最爱的一本书的作者是晨花的信徒。他的名字叫埃希。他和你不一样，崔斯提安，他不是一名传教士；他专精的是奥法领域。不过他确实是把莎恩芮的教条当作自己的人生准则。
You know, the author of my favorite books was a follower of the Dawnflower. His name was Ash. He wasn't a man of the cloth like you Tristian; he dabbled in the arcane arts. But he did find Sarenrae's teaching an ideology to live by.
Tristian：听你说的这个埃希还挺有意思的。看来我也得把他的书找来读一读。是阿维斯坦大骑士的传说吗？
I like the sound of this Ash. Perhaps I will have to give those books a read. Legend of the Archknights of Avistan was it?
Linzi：没人可以阻止我们的首领。不过也没人能阻止伊洛维提国王……
Nothing can stop our leader. Though King Irovetti will stop at nothing...
Tristian：既然我们和他都不可阻挡，如果二者相遇会如何呢？这其中有矛盾……
Wait, if nothing can stop us — is that the same nothing that he'll stop at? That can't be right...
Linzi：崔斯提安，你为什么这样看着我？我的脸上又沾上墨迹了吗？
Tristian, why are you staring at me like that? Do I have an ink stain on my cheek again?
Tristian：嗯……确实是。不过我倒不是在关注这一点。我只是喜欢看你写作而已。你是如此……专注。就好像你是想把自己的生命都倾注在这些书页上一样。
Um... Yes, actually. But it wasn't about that. I just like to watch you write. You're so... focused. As if you're trying to put a part of yourself down on the pages.
Linzi：嘿，崔斯提安，你的女神教导大家要仁慈和宽恕。但是有没有什么事情是你无法原谅的呢？
Hey, Tristian, your goddess teaches mercy and forgiveness. But are there things you wouldn't be able to forgive?
Tristian：除了你这尴尬的幽默感之外吗？
Besides your bitter humor?
Linzi：崔斯提安，告诉我！是不是喀勒什的所有人都像你这么无聊？还是说你就是因为说教太无聊才被他们赶出来的？
Tristian, tell me! Is everyone so boring in Kelesh? Or were you banished from there for your boring sermons?
Tristian：我是带着莎恩芮的祝福，遵循自己的自由意志走上这趟旅程的……你为什么要笑？我说的话很好笑吗？
I went on this journey of my own free will, with Sarenrae's blessing... Why are you giggling? Did I say something funny?
Linzi：你的故乡喀勒什是如此遥远……你一定在旅程中见过很多事情！你为什么从来不聊起呢？
Your native Kelesh is so far away... You must have seen so much in your journeys! Why don't you ever talk about it?
Tristian：我……事实上我没什么可说的。不是每一名旅行者都会经历刺激的冒险。
I... Actually I have nothing to tell. Not everyone who travels gets to have adventures.
Linzi：你的信任让你……毫不设防。你难道不怕恶人利用你的平和性格来伤害你吗？
Your faith makes you so... vulnerable. Aren't you afraid that evil people will take advantage of your peaceful disposition, and hurt you?
Tristian：不是这样的。我的誓言和信仰是一种力量，而不是一个弱点。不过如果真的有人借此做恶，那绝不是我的所作所为。
Hardly. My vows and faith are a strength, not a weakness. But if evil is done, it won't be my doing.
Linzi：是什么事情让你开始信仰莎恩芮的？我来猜一猜。你一定是犯下了严重的罪行！而现在你是在赎罪。我说得对吗？
What brought you to your faith in Sarenrae? Let me guess. You committed a terrible atrocity! And now you're atoning for it. Am I right?
Tristian：我只是看到了这个世界上，罪恶太多，而仁慈太少。我只是想要把它变得更好，难道这样还不够吗？
I was just looking at the world. There is such evil in it, and too little mercy. Is it not enough to simply want to change it for the better?
Linzi：嘿，你有没有什么阴暗的过去？比如曾经是个酒鬼，或者是个老赌棍。主要是如果我要给你写本书，那么你这个人物就会有点……平淡。不够自然。
Hey, do you have some horrible secret? Maybe you like to drink, or you used to be an inveterate gambler. It's just that when I'm writing about you, you just come out... flat. It's unnatural.
Tristian：我……说实话，我不是很理解你什么意思。
I... truth be told, I don't really understand what you mean.
Linzi：我们的冒险真是太棒了！很快我就可以写出一部鸿篇巨制，就像我最喜好的书——《阿维斯坦大骑士传奇》一样！你知道吗，瓦尔瑞，并不是所有人都像你一样大喊大叫、猛劈猛砍。盖伊·夏色尔·德·隆巴尔就非常灵巧、优雅、有风度。他会在他的对手四周舞蹈，只在迫不得己的时候才会痛下杀手。你可以跟他学一学，呆子。
Our adventures are so great! Soon I will be able to write an epic tale just like my favorite books, Legend of the Archknights of Avistan. You know Valerie, not everyone flails about screaming like you do. Guy Shaseur De Lombair had finesse, style, grace. He danced around his opponents killing only when he had to. You could learn from him, oaf!
Valerie：照你这么说，他是当时最伟大的剑士？真是开玩笑。像是酒馆里的表演一样在战场上跳舞？我从来没见过有人会在战斗中跳舞，小姑娘。听起来像是捏造的……
&#x20; EN: [未找到英文]
Linzi：你知道吗，有的时候我会想象我如果是你会怎么样。穿着闪亮盔甲的骑士，还有一见倾心的王后……该有的都有了。你的人生就像是一部小说！
You know, sometimes I try to imagine what it's like to be like you. A knight in shining armor — and the queen of hearts, all rolled into one... Your life is like a novel!
Valerie：粗略一看，确实非常梦幻，不是吗？
From a distance it looks like a blessing, does it not?
Linzi：瓦尔瑞，你难道不喜欢故事吗，连小时候也不喜欢？我真不敢相信。所有的小孩都喜欢听故事！
Valerie, didn't you like stories, even as a child? I can't believe that. All children like stories!
Valerie：难道你还没发现吗，我已经不是小孩子了。你是不是也该成熟一点呢！
In case you haven't noticed, I'm not a child any more. Maybe you should grow up at last too!
Linzi：真可惜我不会画画。我真想画你……给你画一幅肖像，或者画一幅漫画！
Too bad I don't know how to paint. I'd like to draw you... Maybe a portrait, or a caricature!
Valerie：这可真是新鲜事。还从来没有人给我画过漫画……不过我觉得这样最好。
That's something new. I don't think anyone has ever drawn a caricature of me before... Though I think that's for the best.
Linzi：瓦尔瑞，你为什么总是那么孤僻？这个世界上一定有什么你喜欢的东西吧！
Valerie, why are you so mean? There's got to be something in this world that you like!
Valerie：我喜欢你不说话的时候。如果这个世界上真有什么我觉得美的事情，那就是安静。
I like it when you stay silent. If there's anything beautiful in this world, it's silence.
Linzi：我以后一定要写一部有关我们的冒险的舞台剧……而你就是我的主角！
Someday I'll write a play about our adventures... And you'll have one of the main parts!
Valerie：那就看看有没有哪个女演员有胆子上台去扮演我……当她知道我就坐在第一排看戏的时候！
Then we'll see if there's at least one actress brave enough to go on stage and impersonate me... Knowing that I'll be sitting in the front row!
Linzi：你难道不想让自己的名字在多年以后受众人景仰吗？要做到这一点，就必须有一名吟游诗人来歌颂你的事迹！你可以想象一下：高贵的瓦尔瑞，失窃之地那英勇无敌的守卫者……
Don't you want your name to be glorified through the ages? Without a bard to trumpet your feats, that simply wouldn't happen! But just imagine: noble Valerie, daring protector of the Stolen Lands...
Valerie：那是什么，一首诗的标题吗？听起来像是墓志铭啊。我开始担心你会害得我不得安息。
What is that, the title of a poem? Sounds more like an epitaph. I'm starting to think you're not even going to let me rest in peace.
Linzi：你选择了战士的道路，而现在你的剑刃总是沾满鲜血……怎么，你喜欢杀戮吗？
You chose a path of a warrior, and now your sword is always wet with blood... What, do you like killing?
Valerie：战争并不比其他的事业低贱。至少不会比画画、或将字词排成行这种工作更差！至于我的敌人嘛，又不是我逼他们拿起武器的。和我一样，他们也选择了自己的道路。
War is no less honorable a trade than any others. It's definitely not worse than drawing, or arranging letters into lines! As for my enemies – I'm not the one who puts weapons in their hands. Like me, they've chosen their own path.
生日祝福
Linzi：是今天，对吧？我把它记下来了，所以我不会忘，但是我忘了把记录本放哪儿了！
It's today, right? I made a note so I won't forget, but then I forgot where I put the note!
Linzi：噢，它在这儿呢……我们的女爵/男爵/女王/国王今天有个特别的日子！我祈祷您会有些新的冒险和刺激的活动！因为，有什么能比得上冒险呢？生日快乐！
Oh, here it is… Our {baroness/baron/Queen/King} has a very special day today! I wish you many new adventures and exciting events! Because what could be better than adventures? Happy Birthday!
Jaethal：哈，他的卑鄙意图简直明明白白写在脸上了。在琼宁的王廷里，这样的冒险者我已经见得够多的了。但我想，这只会让他更容易为我们所用……
Ha. His vile intent was written in red upon his forehead. I've seen enough such adventurers in the courts of Kyonin. But I suppose this only makes it easier to use him for our benefit...

C11_Bokken

And I like Bokken. He reminds me of a book seller I know... Cold and tart at first, but if he likes someone, it's forever.

C21_DelicateSituation
Linzi：人们面前浮现的画面！一群身材高大、毛发旺盛的伐木工人，任由一个小巧玲珑却胆大包天的妖精摆布……栩栩如生！这非得写进书里不可！
The things one sees! Huge, hairy woodsmen at the mercy of a small but daring fey... What a vivid image! That just begs to be included in a book!
Jubilost：让我们来看看谁会最先写出这个桥段——谁又会最先发表！
We shall see who writes of this episode first — and who publishes it first!
Harrim：要让你印象深刻也太容易了。
How easily you are impressed.
Octavia：我真的对伐木工人陷入的麻烦感到十分同情，可我也能感受到妖精的痛苦。她的林子就要被摧毁了！
I'm truly sorry for the woodsmen's trouble. But I can also sympathize with the fey's suffering. Her grove was about to be destroyed!
C21_PaizoCollector
Linzi：好吧，我需要你帮我出出主意！那个住在都城的说书人究竟是谁？一个精灵怎么会看上去那么老迈呢？这不可能呀！
All right, I need your ideas! Who is that Storyteller who's living in the capital? How could an elf look so old? It shouldn't be possible!
Regongar：你遇到过各种各样的怪人，他肯定不是最怪的。
You meet all kinds of freaks. He's not nearly the worst.
Jaethal：我也想过，他会不会是找到了延长寿命的方法，而代价是他的青春美貌？如果只能栖居在如此丑陋又衰老的躯体之中的话，看来死亡比生命更慈悲，我永远也忍受不了。
I thought about that. Did he find a way to prolong his life, but paid for it with his youth and beauty? Death is more merciful than life, in such an ugly old body. I could never stand it.
Octavia：他是个说书人，不是吗？他有很多故事可说，这可能也是其中之一。而且，我觉得，他只是一个普通的半精灵，就这么简单。
He's a Storyteller, is he not? He has many stories to tell. This may be one of them. Meanwhile, I'd say he's just a common half-elf, plain and simple.
Tristian：我觉得他比看上去更复杂。我感觉得到，他的内心有真正的力量，但不是被邪恶污染的力量。不管他是谁，他都可能成为一个很有价值的盟友。
I feel that he's much more than he appears. I sense true power within him, but not power corrupted by evil. Whoever he is, he might make a valuable ally.
Jubilost：我一直在问自己同样的问题，可没有科学文献能提供任何有关精灵衰老的确凿信息。我们的说书人要不就是一个强大魔法的受害者，要不就是一个骗子，就这么简单。
I've been asking myself the same question. No scientific treatises hold any solid information about the aging of elves. Either our Storyteller has fallen victim to a powerful magic, or he's a liar, plain and simple.
Kalikke/Kanerah：也许他是个假装成凡人的灵魂，好监视我们？但他究竟是个善良的灵魂，还是个邪恶的呢……
Maybe he's a spirit pretending to be mortal so he can watch us? But is he a good spirit, or an evil one...
C21_Start
Linzi：想象一下！就在不久之前，我遭到了驱逐，我的作品也被删节了——而现在，我又成为了御用记述者！我很想知道，未来还有什么等待着我！
Just imagine! Only a short while ago I was expelled and my works censored — and now I'm a court chronicler! I wonder what more awaits!

You are such a child sometimes! I'd be happy to give you my share, if we had anything to share...

As an author, I cannot approve of such a foolish artistic solution. On the other hand, as a hungry traveler...

If Linzee disappears everyone notices. Shame. Would make great sacrifice.
NokNok：曾经我听说在一个离这里很远的地方，地精几乎杀掉了所有的长腿怪。从森林里杀出来，烧掉无数村庄，几乎征服了一切。
I heard that once, far away from here, goblins nearly killed all longshanks. Marched from forests, burned down villages, conquered almost everything.
NokNok：只可惜最后还是输掉了。长腿怪睡醒了，把所有人都打跑了。和从前一样。
Although lost all in the end. Longshanks woke up and bashed everyone. As usual.

I need to think of a title for myself. Nok-Nok Allterrifying. Or Nok-Nok Allconquering.
NokNok：恐怖地精诺克诺克？嗯。需要再多点脑子想想。
Nok-Nok Fearsomefullering? Hmmmm. Needs more think-thoughts.

I had a dream recently. That I am a Hero already. And battle a dragon! On the back of owlbear!
NokNok：然后我从它背上摔下了，把自己吓醒了。当时正是战斗最精彩的时候！
Then I fell off his back and woke up. During the most interesting part!
Kalikke&Kanerah
NokNok：原来我在村子里的时候，我什么都没见过。和你在一起，诺克诺克一天见到的东西比一辈子见到的都多！
When I stayed in village, I saw nothing. With you, Nok-Nok sees more during one day than during the whole life!
NokNok：这就是为什么地精大王永远也无法成为英雄，因为只顾着看着眼前！
That's why goblin king would never become a Hero. He sees nothing beyond his hind!

I came from a tribe of nomads. I saw much when I lived with them. — Although not as much as I saw once I set off into the world...
NokNok：安米芮吃东西像脚拌机一样！
Amiri eat like Kronkshaft!

You bet! I'm not some Brevic damsel to whimper and play dress-up! Wait... did you just call me a goblin?!

Hey, Amiri! Teach me how to slash like you do. Hero will find use for that.

Oh boy… You are bold like a Kellid but too weak to fight like us.

If NokNok had mane like Amiri, nobody would notice bumps that others left on NokNok’s head.

Pff! Not a big deal! Find yourself a bear, tear a piece of his fur out, and slap it on your head! Done!

Amiri, who do you worship? Which god likes stabbings like yours?

I worship Gorum, Our Lord in Iron. He laughs at toothpicks like these kukris of yours — he has a better way about a bloodshed!

NokNok have great respect for Amiri. She dosen’t even reek like other longshanks.

Did Nok-Nok just… approve the way I smell? Damn, I need a bath indeed.

Are all longshanks in your homelands like you, Amiri? If so, NokNok would love to go there.

Nah. In my lands it's… like in your tribe, Nok-Nok. They don't respect heroes.

You shoot well. But bow is a weapon for cowards, not Heroes.

I saw your kind hiding from enemies in dirt or pretending to be dead. Don't speak to me of cowardice.

Hey! You shot a deer a while ago, yes? Where are his insides? Longshanks don't eat those, I know that!
Ekun：我把内脏拿来煮汤了。你和其他人都吃了。所以别来我这儿要吃的。
I used entrails for a stew. You ate with others. So don't beg.

Hm… now you look more like a follower of Rovagug than Lamashtu. But I like your pursuit of the final end.

Tavia likes shiny thingies. On her fingers, in her ears. Nok-Nok likes also. Look, made myself a thingie on a rope.

Yuk! Pshaw! Nasty! Why does Tavia eat this? Smells of flowers! Faugh!
Octavia：哦不！那是我的唇膏！这可是从安布萨朗买的啊，是用玫瑰油做的！你居然把它从我这里……偷走了？不，你别还给我了，你自己留着吧。
Oh no! That's my lipstick! From Absalom no less, made with rose oil! And you stole it… from me? No, I don't want it back, you can keep it.
NokNok：你和琳翠翠很像。也喜欢把脑袋埋在书里。不过还是比半腿怪好。
You are like Linzee. Also stick your nose in a book. Still better than halfshank.
Octavia：喂！别这么说琳翠！你张口闭口都是她……你不会是爱上她了吧？
Hey! Don't talk of Linzi like that! Can't stay quiet about her… you are not in love with her, are you?
NokNok：塔维雅，你身上都是肥皂味。为什么？如果你不洗澡，你就会闻起来像四周的环境一样……就没人会注意到你！
Tavia, you smell of soap. Why? If you don't wash yourself you start to smell like surroundings — less noticeable!
Octavia：这我要怎么解释？你可以想象一下，如果你一辈子被人逼着要洗澡。然后你终于可以不用洗澡了！你绝对不会做你不喜欢做的事情，对不对？我就是这样。只不过我的情况是反过来的。
How do I explain this? Imagine you were forced to wash yourself all your life. And then suddenly you don't have to! You wouldn't do something you don't like, would you? So it is with me. Only the other way.
NokNok：诺克诺克在扎营的时候摔倒了。摔进了水坑里。然后……变干净了。
Nok-Nok fall when making camp. In big puddle. Got… clean.
Octavia：看来诸神真的能听到我们的祈祷。
Maybe the gods do answer our prayers.
NokNok：你有的时候说话很奇怪。你的话都堆在一起了。不过很好笑。是魔法吗?
You speak strange sometimes. Mix up your words. But in a funny way. Is it magic?
Regongar：哈！这家伙居然懂幽默！ 你们没一个人懂！
Ha! This fellow has a taste in humor! Not like all of you!
NokNok：你差点把我的屁屁烤熟了，长腿怪！你扔电电的时候小心点！
You nearly cooked my hind, longshank! Watch where you throw your zaps!
Regongar：那就别挡在我面前，小虫子，不然我就把你整个烤熟！
Then don't get in my way, gnat, or I'll fry you for good!
NokNok：我的耳朵里还在响瑞瑞的电电。好厉害的东西。再来点！
Still rings in my ears from Reg-Reg's zaps. Mighty stuff! Want some!
Regongar：呃，不行。你已经给我们带来太多风险。你要是身上带电，那我晚上就更睡不着了！
Ugh no. You impose enough risk on all of us already. Would you have lightnings, I wouldn't be able to sleep at all!
NokNok：曾经和兽人战斗过。愚蠢的长腿怪。强壮，但是愚蠢。从他两腿之间滑过去，跑到他身后，捅他背……兽人就死了！
Fought with orcs once. Stupid longshanks. Strong. Stupid. Slide between legs, get behind, stab in back — orc is dead!
Regongar：你还没有见过那些来自强大部落的真正的兽人。那些才是值得一战的对手！他们的背都是用石头做的，你根本捅不穿！什么？是真的，我保证！
You haven't seen real orcs from mighty clans. Those are the worthy opponents! And their backs made from stone you can't stab! What? That's true, I tell you!
NokNok：瑞贡尕是长腿怪里最会说话的。没那么多缠在一起的字。差不多和我部落的地精一样好。
Regongar is the best with words among other longshanks. Doesn't mix words as much. Almost as good as goblins from my tribe.
Regongar：什么？你这是……在夸我？你的‘夸奖’还是留给你自己吧，你这个屎腿怪！
Did you just, what, complimented me?! Keep your 'praise' to yourself, shatshank!
NokNok：诺克诺克喜欢营地。罐子里有什么？
Nok-Nok like camp. What is in the pot?
Regongar：地精，你再问一个问题试试看。
Goblin, if you keep asking.

Is your Sarenrae better than Lamashtu? Let's compare!
Tristian：诺克诺克，我不会无缘无故的把我的女神的名字挂在嘴边。根据我对拉玛什图的了解来看，你也最好别这样做。
I don't want to use my goddess' name in vain, Nok-Nok. Judging by what I know about Lamashtu, you shouldn't as well.
NokNok：艾斯提安总是在咕叽咕叽。早上也是。晚上也是。他应该去献祭，这样才对！
Eestian mumbles mumbles mumbles all the time. At dawn. At dusk. Should sacrifice something, would made more sense!
Tristian：我的名字叫崔斯提安，诺克诺克。在你纠正我的宗教仪式知识前，你应该自己先学一学。
My name is Tristian, Nok-Nok. At least learn it before lecturing me about the proper religious rites.
NokNok：你可以治疗。太棒了！英雄需要你这样的辅助！
You can heal. That good! Hero needs sidelings like you to help!
Tristian：大家都懂得慈悲的语言……即便是地精这种低级的生物。
Everyone understands the language of mercy — even such outrageous creatures as goblins.
NokNok：艾斯提安，莎恩芮住在哪里？在太阳上吗？
Eestian, where does Sarenrae live? On the sun?
Tristian：不，诺克诺克。不过我曾经听过一个故事，有一名强力的法师在太阳上安家。
No, Nok-Nok. But once I heard a story of a powerful wizard that made his home on the sun.
NokNok：如果我崇拜火焰女神，那我就不会去治疗。我会把一切都烧掉！砰！呼呼呼！都烧成灰！
Would I worship fire goddess, I would not heal. I would burn everything! Bam! Whoosh! All become ashes!
Tristian：有许多莎恩芮的信徒都更信仰她的……其他方面。不过她肯定不会希望有一名纵火犯成为她的信徒。
There are followers of Sarenrae that prefer… her other aspects. But she definitely won't rejoice at an arsonist joining her worshipers.
NokNok：看，艾斯提安！我做了一个拉玛什图神符！和你的一样！
Look, Eestian! I made Lamashtu symbol! Like yours!
Tristian：诺克诺克，这……非常漂亮。我特别喜欢你在这只老鼠的头上插进去的那块碎玻璃。现在请你把它从我眼前拿开，谢谢。
Very… very beautiful, Nok-Nok. I especially like this piece of glass you plunged in the middle of rat's head. Now would you please take it away from me.
NokNok：闪亮亮的刀。闪亮亮的头发。闪亮亮的脸。嘿！
Shiny metals. Shiny hair. Shiny face. Bweh!
Valerie：我真希望我那些不请自来的追求者中有一半能够像你一样，对你刚才说的这些东西都感到恶心。
I wish half of my unwelcome suitors share your disgust with everything you mentioned.
NokNok：你，和我一样，不是在笼子里养大的。所以你是处旅行。冒险。诺克诺克和鸡窝头一样。
You, like me, wasn't raised in a cage. So you wandered. Adventured. Nok-Nok and strawhead are alike.
Valerie：嗯……我不太赞同。
Hm. I prefer to think otherwise.
NokNok：鸡窝头穿着这么多铁片，把自己藏起来。地精身上就没这些东西。地精不用这些东西也知道怎么藏。
Strawhead wears these metals on herself to hide. Haven't seen such things on goblins. Goblins know how to hide without those.
Valerie：你是说我的盔甲么？这不是用来藏人的，是用来防护的。就像你那厚脑壳一样。
Do you mean my armor? It's not for hiding, it's for protection. The same function your thick skull provides.
NokNok：这么多铁片……可以做出多少把锐器来啊！
So many metals… would make a lot of stabbers of those!

I warn you, Nok-Nok… any piece of my armor goes missing, you will regret it!

Auch, a bruise. But the ear is still here at least.
Valerie：我知道我这么说只是在白费口舌……不过你下一次投入战斗的时候能不能别那么疯狂？上一场战斗里，你的手差点被剁掉！
I know that I just waste my words… but didn't you think of entering combat with less abandon? You almost got your hand cut off in the last fight!

Let me see to your wound. And don't tear off the dressing, please. And don't hang dead mice on it either.

I wish you good treasures, and all the tasty things to eat. – And some scary monsters to slice. That's how one Hero honors another hero!

Without the king, goblins need their own hero. Not longshank or halfshank. Their own. Like me.

Mother of Monsters doesn't give voice to simple critters. Gives fangs. Gives talons. Gives extra limbs. But not voice.

I am afraid that the bird we met is a herald of another kind of evil. Perhaps Sarenrae herself sends me this ominous premonition as a warning.

That female back at the barbarian camp... strange. Smells funny. Walks funny. As if broken.

Greatbeasts rough the land again. Everyone blames Mother again. But that is not her! She would have told me!

Whoever is to blame, I pity the simple folk that will become beasts' prey. They just began forgetting about that terrible Bloom — and now it strikes again!

Such are the Stolen Lands. One disaster follows another, and we never know which ancient evil to blame. There are so many that call this place their home!

C51_AfterRushlight

DeadInParty

Once we get to your stinky city we'll find five more adventurers, instead of dead ones.

Maybe, if we pick up and move right now, he will fall behind and leave us in peace? Does somebody else like this plan like I do?
Valerie：别朝我这边打呵欠，地精。你嘴里的臭气能把雪琳的追随者熏晕过去！
Do not yawn in my direction, goblin. The stench of your breath can knock out some Shelyn followers!
Octavia：你打哈欠揉眼睛的样子，简直就像个小孩子！一个小小的……爱吃肉的……丑小孩。
When you yawn like that and rub your eyes, you look like a child! Small... carnivorous... hideous child.
Regongar：哈哈哈哈，你们看他！都累成这样了，就差在地上赖在地上不动了！
Bhahahaha, look at him! So exhausted he's about to plant himself in the ground!
InDungeon
NokNok：我不担心野兽。我会趁它吃你们的时候逃走。
I don't worry about beasts. I will run away while they eat all of you.
NokNok：诺克诺克很狡猾，诺克诺克跑得快。如果野兽冲过来，诺克诺克先拜拜！
Nok-Nok is sly, Nok-Nok is fast. Nok-Nok will flee if beast attacks!
Valerie：至少你没有假装在乎我们的性命。
At least you don't try to pretend that you value our lives.
Regongar：我向你保证，你跑不远！你敢迈一步试试，就等着闪电烤屁股吧！
I promise you won't get too far! Just try and you'll get a lightning bolt in your hind!
Ekun：我们能解决敌人。然后我一定会追到你。
We'll manage. Then I will track you.
Linzi：好极了！你跑得越远越好！
Perfect! The farther the better for all of us!
LowHealth
NokNok：上次我被打得这么惨，还是不听臭家伙酋长话的那次。骨头都从皮里穿出来了，跟现在一样！
Last time I got beat up this bad was when I disagreed with chief shatshank. Bones sticking through skin, just like now!
NokNok：哇，眼珠差点被打爆。换个新眼珠贵不贵？算了，我不用，瞎的英雄更帅气！
Ow, almost got an eye out. Is it pricey to get a new one? Nah, don't need it, eyeless Hero is better than intact Hero!
Tristian：听你这样说，真让我担心。我来治疗你的伤口吧。
You worry me when you talk like that. Let me tend to your wounds.
Harrim：明明是将死之人，话却一点不少。
Too much hassle for the one who is so close to his end.
Amiri：伤疤是珍贵的奖章！当你从精彩的战斗中获得新的伤疤，你应该感到自豪！
Scars are valuable trophies! You should be proud when you get new ones from a great fight!
Jubilost：别把你身上的每块伤口给我看了！看完以后我晚上该做噩梦了！
Stop showing me every last of your bruises! I will be seeing nightmares after these demonstrations!
LowRations
NokNok：喂！口袋里的食物呢？原本有好多的。
Hey! Where is food in the bag? Used to be a lot.
NokNok：诺克诺克饿了。有口粮吗？
Nok-Nok hungry. Got a ration?
Valerie：你今天吃的粮食已经有三个你那么重了！你扔进嘴里的口粮都变到哪去了？
You ate three times your weight today! Just what happens to all those rations you toss in your maw?
Jubilost：当然，我知道地精最有名的特性就是永远处于饥饿状态……不过我从来没想到我会在队伍里遇到这么一头猛兽，并且因此忍饥挨饿！
I, of course, am aware that goblins are famous for their unsatiable hunger... but I certainly wasn't aware that I'll have one of those beasts in my group and will suffer as a result!
Jaethal：乌伽娑婀肯定会喜欢你的胃口——只可惜你是个信仰拉玛什图的垃圾。
Urgathoa would have been pleased with your gluttony. Would have — if you weren't Lamashtu scum.
Ekun：如果你继续那么吃下去，你就自己去打猎吧。我的箭快要用完了。
If you will keep eating like that, hunt for yourself. I am running out of arrows.
LowSpell
NokNok：法术没了。我要休息。
Spells out. Need to rest.
NokNok：又把魔法用完了。基本没剩下。
Spent all magic again. Almost nothing left.
Octavia：也许我们可以派诺克诺克去科技联盟卧底？反正他怎样也会逃跑的，但在那之前，他肯定能给那群畜生搞出一大堆麻烦，叫他们再也没工夫做施法奴隶生意了！
Maybe we can plant Nok-Nok in the Technic League? He will run away anyway, but before that those bastards will get enough trouble to cease dealing with spellcasting slaves forever!

Heh. Nok-Nok and magic. He-he-he-he... oh, don't give me that look! It even sounds hilarious, aha-ha!
Jubilost：地精法师……我的不可思议之物大全又多了一项奇谈！
A goblin mage... another oddity for my collection of Things-That-Should-Never-Be!
Harrim：动用超越自身理解的力量只会加快你的灭亡。然而，我并不会为此悲伤，因为这是你自己选择的道路。
You hasten your undoing by dealing with the powers you can't comprehend. However, I am not the one to be unhappy because you chose this path.
Kalikke/Kanerah：会魔法的地精就像一只长着龙头的小猫咪：有趣——但依然很危险！
A goblin wielding magic is like a kitten with the head of a dragon: funny — but deadly nonetheless!
NoBuilding
NokNok：我们地精很会躲猫猫，长腿怪就不会。你们为什么不做伪装，就这样休息？不怕吗？
Our kind knows how to hide. Longshanks don't. Why do you rest like this, then, without cover? Not afraid?
NokNok：你们长腿怪和半腿怪能建造城市，还有高高的墙……然后却坐在这么开阔的地方，也不加防护。真奇怪。
You longshanks and halfshanks build your cities, with walls around them... and here you sit without any protection in the clear sight. Strange.
Jaethal：有什么比地精的唠叨更糟糕呢？那就是叫他说对了。应该有人注意一下营地的防护措施。
What can be worse than goblin's nagging? Only when he is right. Somebody should have cared about the camp's protection.
Amiri：我不需要躲藏。如果有什么东西想吃我，我会叫它后悔。我说到做到！
I don't have to hide. Somebody trying to eat me will learn to regret it, I promise you that!
Linzi：说实话……诺克诺克说得对。不过，考虑到有他在这里，就算再怎么防护营地也不够安全。
Honestly... Nok-Nok is right. But, considering him being around, even protected camp isn't safe enough.
Tristian：我们依靠同伴，诺克诺克。如果有异常情况，会有人警告大家的。
We count on our companions, Nok-Nok. Someone will raise an alert and warn the others if anything goes wrong.
Kalikke/Kanerah：就算你没法躲起来，也至少应该休息一下，这样在有敌人接近时，你才能做好迎战的准备。
Even if you can't hide, you should at least get some rest, so you'll be ready to face any enemies who come by.
NoHunting
NokNok：我在路上看到鸟窝了。嫩嫩的肉……脆脆的骨头……
I saw bird nest on my way. Tender meat... bones go crunch...
NokNok：我抓住一只老鼠！我的！不分给你们！不用问我！
I caught a rat! Mine! Won't share, don't ask!
Octavia：呃！不要说给我听！
Ew! Spare me your thoughts!
Amiri：是啊，要是有新鲜的肉就好了。可惜今天没人打猎。
Yeah, fresh meat would be nice. Too bad nobody hunts today.
Valerie：我的家庭教师经常在我耳边唠叨每一件餐具的重要性，假如他听到你说的话……我倒不介意看看他脸上的表情！
If only my family tutor who nagged me about the importance of each set of cutlery could hear you... yes, wouldn't mind the pleasure of seeing his face at the sound of your babbling!
Ekun：地精吃捡来的垃圾。我们用不着喂你吃的。
Goblins eat garbage they find. No need to feed you.
Kalikke/Kanerah：你在沙漠里也活得下去，这我可以肯定！
You could survive in the desert, that's for sure!
Octavia：等我回到纽玫瑞亚以后……我也不知道我回去以后要做什么。也许我可以去找到以前的奴隶主，把他们都磨成渣。或者做一个普通人，过上自由的生活！
Once I return to Numeria... I don't know what I'll do there. Maybe I'll find my former owners and grind them to dust. Or maybe just walk the streets as a free person!
Octavia：我只是需要回去为我人生中的那一段做一个了结。
I just need to get back and close that chapter of my life.

Why is Regongar so... Regongar?! I love him terribly but... Can he be just a tiny bit less Regongarian?
Kalikke&Kanerah
Octavia：我们的队伍真不错！我们还挺适合这个地方的。要是在开化的地方，我们可能会互相仇视，不过这里无处不在的危机却让我们团结在一起。
What a party we have here! We somehow suit these lands. In a civilized place we'd probably all feud with each other all the time, but here the constant danger keeps us united.
Kalikke/Kanerah：真令人难过，往往只有危机和麻烦能让我们团结一致。就连那些真心相爱的人们有时也会发现他们有太多不同点，没法和平共处。
It's sad that danger and trouble are often the only things that keep us together. Even those who sincerely love each other can sometimes find they're too different to live together in peace.
Octavia：这么坐着感觉有点奇怪……没有人来鞭打我。不用担心你的奴隶主会突然出现，也不必为了苟活而忍受屈辱……
It's strange to sit like this... Not expecting the lash. Not fearing that your owner might show up, and you'll have to humiliate yourself just to live another day...
Kalikke/Kanerah：我理解，但保持警惕。你的奴隶主可能不在这里，不过附近肯定有饿坏了的怪物……
I understand, but stay sharp. Your masters might not be here, but there's sure to be hungry monsters nearby...
Octavia：安米芮，你非常勇敢，但是为什么你总是什么都不考虑就开始揍人呢？
Amiri, you are very brave, but why don't you think about what you're doing before you punch someone in the face?
Amiri：不不不。我要是想太多，会头疼的。但是只要能揍人，我就百病全消了。
Naah. If I think too much, I get a headache. – And when I punch someone's jaw, it disappears.
Octavia：你讲的童年故事真是让人难以置信！北方的所有部落都这么对待女人吗？
What you've said about your childhood is just mind-boggling! Do all the northern tribes treat their women like that?
Amiri：呃，不，并不是所有的。我比较走运，我出生在整个地区最野蛮的部落里！就连我们邻近的部落都把我们当地精看待！
Well, no, not all of them. I'm the lucky one – I was born in the most savage tribe in the whole country! Even our neighbors looked at us like we're goblins!
Octavia：嘿，安米芮！等我们回城里了，我们一起去找一家时髦的女装店吧。我保证把你打扮的漂漂亮亮的！
Hey, Amiri! Why don't we go to a fashionable dress shop once we get to the city. I promise, we'll make a real beauty out of you!
Amiri：什么？你想让我穿裙子？那我是不是还得开始刷牙啊？
What? You want me to pull on a dress? Maybe I should start brushing my teeth too?
Octavia：你有没有数过在旅途中杀掉了多少人？
Have you counted how many you've killed during your journeys?
Amiri：嗯……挺多的。为什么要数？我的工作是挥剑。让法拉丝玛去数死了多少人吧。
Um... A lot. Why would I count? My job is to swing my sword. Let Pharasma count the dead.
Octavia：要是我有你的这身肌肉，简直死了都值得啊。我能摸一摸吗？
Your muscles are to die for! Can I touch them?
Amiri：才不要！你和瑞贡尕，你们都别把手伸过来！
Hell no! You and Regongar both – keep your hands away from me!
Octavia：安米芮，我一直想问你一个问题。你是用什么特殊方法来做发型的？
Amiri, I've been meaning to ask you for a while now. Do you do anything special to style your hair?
Amiri：那还用说！抓住一个敌人，用头猛撞他的肚子，然后我的发型就大功告成了！
You bet! You grab an enemy, you head-butt him in the stomach — and my style is good and done!
Octavia：我觉得你的狼并不是普通的动物。他简直太通人性了！
I think your wolf isn't a regular animal. There's so much human in him!
Ekun：有的人相信人可以重生为动物。这是真的吗？我不知道。
Some believe people can be reborn as animals. Could it be true? I don't know.
Octavia：艾昆，你是不是不会笑啊？试一试吧，很简单的！
Ekun, can you smile at all? Try it, it's easy!
Ekun：我可以笑。只是找不到理由。
I can smile. I just don't see a reason.
Octavia：你知道我在想什么吗，艾昆？我们必须要找个时间，把你抓进酒馆里，然后把你喝趴下。说不定你就可以暂时不那么阴沉了。
You know what I think, Ekun? We need to pick a moment, drag you into the tavern and get you hammered. Maybe you'll stop being so grouchy for a moment.
Ekun：谢谢您。你这么关心我，我非常感动。将来总有一天我会跟你去一醉方休的。我保证。
Thank you. I appreciate your concern. One day I will go to the tavern with you. I promise.
Octavia：别再想那些伤心的事啦！我当年也被科技联盟奴役了那么长时间，如果我当时选择认命，那我肯定早就心理崩溃，变成一个无药可救的玩物了。
Enough thinking about bad things! If I'd accepted my fate for even a moment when I was enslaved by the Technic League, I would have been broken and turned into a helpless toy.
Ekun：奥克塔维雅，你承受了如此多的苦难。也许还超过了我。没错，我的家人确实都死了。不过至少他们没有背叛我。
You've endured much pain, Octavia. Perhaps even more than me. My family died, yes. But at least they didn't betray me.
Kalikke：你的话有对的地方，也有错的，奥克塔维雅。人永远不应放弃希望。但如果不去想那些坏事，牢记曾经发生和可能发生的苦难，那我们就永远没法从自己的错误中学到教训了。
You're both right and wrong, Octavia. One should never lose hope. But unless you think about bad things, and remember the woes that have happened and could happen, then we'll never be able to learn from our mistakes.
Octavia：你知道吗，艾昆，我很高兴能和你成为伙伴。我们真是相逢恨晚啊。
You know, Ekun, I'm glad you're with us. Too bad we never met earlier.
Ekun：我也是。你是我的好朋友。
Me too. You're a good friend.
Octavia：那么你有没有考虑过……建立一个新的家庭呢？说不定你可以另外找到一个地方，重新拥有一个家。
And have you ever thought about... starting a new family? What if you find another place you could call home?
Ekun：我失去了太多。不过我活了下来。如果再来一次我会受不了的。所以我最好还是孤身一人吧。
I've lost much. But I survived. I wouldn't survive again. I'm better off alone.
Octavia：噢！我好像看到一条蛇！小心点！艾昆！
Oh! I think I saw a snake! Careful! Ekun!
Ekun：那是一只没腿的蜥蜴，不是蛇。没有毒的。
That's a legless lizard, not a snake. No venom.
Octavia：那么你有没有想过回到你的故乡去呢？那里一定还有你认识的人。
And have you thought about returning to your home town? There must still be people there who you know.
Ekun：不。许多人都死了。还活着的人都觉得我也死了。我不能回去。
No. Many died. For others, I died. I couldn't go back.
Octavia：哈瑞姆，笑一笑！你那张臭脸把汤都熏臭了！
Harrim, smile! Your acid looks are going to sour the soup!
Harrim：你觉得我的笑容可以让汤变得更美味吗？如果是真的，那么只要把汤放到你旁边，汤就会变成烤肉了。
Do you think soup can get better with a smile? If that were true, then next to you it would turn into a roast.
Octavia：你为什么总是在担心死亡？人生中还有这么多有趣的事情！我真不明白，怎么能还没尝过安布萨朗最著名的蓝莓蛋糕就死呢！
Why are you so concerned about death? There are so many interesting things in life! I don't understand how one can die without trying Absalom's famous blueberry cakes!
Harrim：蓝莓蛋糕就是生命的意义？嗯……这里面有一些道理。看来如果有一块蓝莓蛋糕在手里，即便是世界末日也会变得更有趣。
Blueberry cakes as the meaning of life? Hmm... There is something in this. I suppose even the end of times would be more fun with a blueberry cake in your hand.
Octavia：你知道吗，有的时候我在想你的忧郁和认命只是一种伪装。而在这表面之下，其实你只是非常害怕。你只是惧怕生活！
You know, sometimes I think all your gloom and fatalism is just a mask. And underneath it all you're just scared. You're simply afraid to live!
Harrim：我并不惧怕生活，奥克塔维雅。而且我也不惧怕死亡。那是格罗图斯伟大的礼物。这个世界是如此不公和残酷，但它也不能将我变得更加悲伤。因为我知道，所有人迟早都会死。
I'm not afraid to live, Octavia. But I'm also not afraid to die. That is the great gift of Groetus. The unfairness and cruelty of this world don't make me sad any more, because I know – sooner or later we all will die.
Octavia：哈瑞姆，如果我们全部都会死，我们会怎么死呢？你有没有想过？
What do you think, Harrim – if we're all meant to die, how will it happen?
Harrim：我不知道。我并不是先知。我只是遵从格罗图斯的意志罢了。不论他为我准备了什么样的末日，我只希望能够带着尊严去面对。
I don't know, I'm not a prophet. I simply follow the will of Groetus. Whatever end is prepared for me, I only hope to face it with dignity.
Octavia：你真的不喜欢找乐子吗，还是你只是不懂怎么找乐子？等我们回首都以后，要不要我来给你组织一场聚会啊？我们可以大快朵颐，尽情舞蹈，然后一醉方休！你觉得怎么样？
Do you really not like to have fun – or you just don't know how to? Do you want me to hold a festival for you when we get back to the capital? We can stuff our faces, dance, and get drunk! What do you say?
Harrim：你对我太好了，奥克塔维雅，不过这样并不能帮助我。音乐和舞蹈只会让我觉得头痛。至于喝酒嘛……嗯……嗯……不行，我最好还是别碰那东西。
I say you are very kind, Octavia, but such help will do me no good. I'll only get a headache from the music and dancing. As for the drinking... Hm, hm... Well, I better keep away from that too.
Octavia：很抱歉，我不是故意要偷听，但是晚上你会发出可怕的呻吟声。你做噩梦了吗？我可以给你一点我的镇定药草。应该可以帮到你。
I'm sorry for eavesdropping, but at night you were moaning terribly in your sleep. Did you have a nightmare? I can share my calming herbs with you. They'll help.
Harrim：真的吗？谢谢你，奥克塔维雅，这可真是太好了。我只知道一种方法可以让自己不做噩梦……不过我现在已经决定要尽量少喝酒。
Really? Thank you, Octavia, this would be great. I know only one way to fight off nightmares... But I'm trying to cut down on my drinking these days.
Octavia：不死生物真的没有感觉，是真的吗？
Is it true the undead don't feel anything?
Jaethal：现在我就对你的愚蠢问题感觉到实实在在的恼怒。
Right now I feel substantial irritation from your silly questions.
Octavia：如果你想要的话，我可以把我的化妆品借给你用。你的肤色有的时候会吓我一跳。而且被吓到还不只是我！
If you wanted, I could lend you some of my cosmetics. The color of your skin frightens me sometimes. And not only me!
Jaethal：你这是建议我把甜菜根和滑石粉涂在自己脸上吗？谢谢你，不用了。如果我需要化妆品，我早就从琼宁带一套来了。我之前的家庭能够非常熟练使用眼影和粉底。
You offer me a chance to smear beetroot and ground chalk on my face? No thank you. If I needed makeup, I'd bring a set from Kyonin. My former family knows their way around eye-shadow and frankincense.
Octavia：你为什么总是闷闷不乐，还没礼貌？我们是你的朋友啊。
Why are you so obnoxious and rude – even with your allies?
Jaethal：朋友？小姑娘，我怕你听到我的回答会不开心啊。你还是就把我当成一个坏脾气的人好了。
Allies? I'm afraid you're not going to like my answer, girl. You'll just imagine I have a bad temper.
Octavia：不死不活……到底是什么感觉？
To be neither alive nor dead... What does it feel like?
Jaethal：你想要感受一下吗？我觉得你恐怕无法成为高质量的不死生物样本，只会变成一个长着漂亮脸蛋的僵尸。不过你的思维过程可能不会彻底变化。
Want to try it? I'm afraid you wouldn't make a quality undead specimen – just a zombie with a pretty face. But your thought processes would not change significantly.
Octavia：你真的希望让所有人都把你当成一条毒蛇吗？让他们都躲着你，免得被你咬一口！你怎么会喜欢这样的感觉呢？
Do you really like it when everyone looks at you like you're a venomous viper? Stay away, or it'll bite! How could you prefer that?
Jaethal：你要知道，我的状态让我可以不用去考虑别人是否喜欢我。反而是别人必须要想办法让我喜欢他们。
You see, my status allows me not to think whether others like me. On the contrary – it's they who must try to be liked by me.
Octavia：你没去过纽玫瑞亚，真是可惜。不然像你这么嚣张，早被人收拾了！
Too bad you've never been to Numeria. They'd beat your arrogance out of you quickly!
Jubilost：亲爱的，你这话可说错了。我去过纽玫瑞亚不止一次，而且过程都挺愉快的。我和那里的科研人员们畅谈政治、文化、以及其他各种东西之间的差别。当然这些话题在你听来就像是天书。
You are wrong here, darling. I've been to Numeria more than once, and had a great time, discussing with their men of science the nuances of politics, culture, and many other things – which you know as much about as a pig knows about cakes.
Octavia：朱比，你为什么留在我们的队伍里，嗯？你一直在唠叨，不停叨唠，根本不停嘴。这个你不喜欢，那个你又讨厌……那你干脆收拾东西走人啊！
Jubi, what keeps you in our party, hunh? You just keep nagging and nagging, and you won't shut up. You don't like this, and you really hate that... So pick up your things and get out of here!
Jubilost：我不是在唠叨，我这是在给你们提出一些宝贵的建议。幸运的是，{Name}理解这一点，和某位歇斯底里的泼妇不一样。
I'm not nagging, I'm giving valuable recommendations. And luckily, unlike a hysterical vixen I could name, {Name} understands this!
Octavia：该死，朱比！你能不能安静一会儿？你这样自作聪明，让我头疼。
Damn it, Jubi! Could you shut up for a minute? I've got a headache from your cleverness.
Jubilost：那是因为我睿智的观点对一颗无法进行智力活动的大脑造成了严重冲击。还有，别叫我朱比！
It's because my sagacious remarks have impacted a brain not meant for mental activity. And don't call me Jubi!
Octavia：你知道吗，朱比罗斯特，我觉得你就是喜欢骚扰别人。你是故意的，把自己的快乐建立在别人的痛苦上，这样就可以把你的脱色再拖延一天。我说得对吗？
You know, Jubilost, I think you just enjoy annoying people. You do it on purpose, to get bright new emotions out of them, and fend off your Bleaching for another day. Am I right?
Jubilost：嗯……你说的这个方法我还从来没有想到过。不过听起来确实可行！看来以后我必须要在谈话中多加入一些讽刺了！就像你说的，这是为了我的健康着想！
Hmm... I've never thought about it like that. But it sounds like quite an idea! I think I'll add a little more sarcasm to my conversations! Like you said – for my health!
Octavia：哎，对了，朱比，请告诉我……
By the way, Jubie, pray tell me...
Jubilost：不，你先告诉我。是谁给你这个权利用如此不敬的方式来称呼我的？！我绝对不会允许任何人这么叫我。所以请你以后不要再用这种粗俗的方式来跟我装亲密了。
No, YOU tell me: who gave you the right to address me in such a disrespectful manner?! I certainly don't recall ever letting anyone call me that. So please, do refrain from such vulgar familiarity in the future.
Octavia：你所说的那些伟大的骑士都是男人。其中有女人吗？一群男人，听上去就很无聊……
All those Knights you always talk about are men. Weren't there any women? A band of men sounds pretty sad and depressing...
Linzi：啊，是有一个，是个乌荼罗魔裔，叫做薇芙利尔。她是个才华横溢的法师，可以召唤出能量武器来攻击她的敌人。她的声音既美丽又致命。和你有些类似……
Oh yes, there was a Vudrani Tiefling named Vivreal. She was a talented caster who could manifest energy weapons to strike her opponents. She sounds beautiful and deadly. Kind of like you...
Octavia：琳翠，你给我们这群人写篇童话吧，怎么样？比如说‘从此以后，他们过上了幸福的生活，并在同一天一起死去……’
Linzi, why don't you write a fairy tale about us all, huh? Like 'they lived happily ever after and died together on the same day…'
Linzi：‘……死在战场上，四周还躺满了敌人的尸体！’对不起，奥克塔维雅，我真的无法想象我们队伍里有人会变老，然后坐在火炉旁，身边围着一大群子孙！
'… on the battlefield, among numerous corpses of their enemies!' I'm sorry, Octavia, but I can't imagine anyone of our team old, sitting by a fire, and surrounded by great-grandchildren!
Octavia：琳翠，你有没有多的衣服？昨天打完那一架之后，我的衣服上全是破洞。
Linzi, do you have a spare shirt? Mine is just holes after yesterday's brawl.
Linzi：有有有。不过我觉得我的衣服太小了，你应该穿不上！嘿嘿。如果你穿上一定很好笑，不过你会很不舒服！
Of course I do. But I think it'll be too short for you! Tee-hee. It would be funny to see, but it wouldn't be comfortable for you!
Octavia：琳翠，你又在些什么呢？
Linzi, what are you writing in there again?
Linzi：你懂的啊，瞎写呗……我在写一个人……等我写完了一定让你看看。可搞笑了！
You know, something... About someone... I'll let you read it once I'm finished. It's hilarious!
Octavia：那么你为什么没有留在大学院呢？你本可以生活在富裕的环境中，不用担心挨饿或受冻。你可以尽情地写你的诗。
So why didn't you stay at the Academy? You'd be living on easy street, never be hungry or cold. All you'd have to do is just write your poems.
Linzi：在皮塔克斯，只有那些屈服于伊洛维提国王的人才能过上富裕的生活！更可怕的是。他们还会对他卑躬屈膝。所以还是算了吧，我宁愿找一间路边的酒馆唱歌谋生！
In Pitax, easy street is inhabited by those who diligently lick King Irovetti's boots! Or worse. Especially if they do it literally. No thank you, I'd rather sing at a roadside tavern!
Octavia：诺克诺克，你有没有觉得……难过？在我们这个队伍里，你的种族只有你一个人。我觉得你肯定会感到孤独。
Nok-Nok, don't you feel… sad? You are the only one of your kind in our midst. I believe you get lonely sometimes.
NokNok：是啊，当英雄真难。特别周围全是长腿怪。不过我有耐心。这是我的使命，对你们保持耐心。
Yes, being a Hero is tough. Especially with longshanks around. But I stay patient. It's my duty — stay patient with you.
Octavia：我曾经在森林里见过一只粽仙。还真的有点可爱！
I once saw a brownie in a forest. It was kind of cute!
NokNok：粽仙好吃吗？
Brownies taste good?
Octavia：我又睡不着了。
I didn't get much sleep again.
Regongar：我知道。你睡觉的时候都在大叫。要是我能把你的噩梦抓出来，朝它的脸上狠狠揍一拳就好了……
I know. You were screaming in your sleep. If I could drag your nightmares outside and punch them in the face...
Octavia：之前的很长一段时间，我始终都无法相信我们终于获得自由了。我现在还觉得很难以置信！
For a long time, I couldn't believe we were finally free. I can still hardly believe it!

But I believed it at once! And if someone tried to prove otherwise, ha! He'd be a stinky spot on the floor that very instant!

Do you remember the ones we lived with in the same barracks? What do you think... happened to them?
Regongar：我没想过他们的事。而且我建议你也别去想他们。我们有了彼此，其他人我都不关心。
I don't think about them. And I recommend you don't either. We have each other, and I don't care about anyone else.

Reg, you are so moody today. And I can see you didn't get much sleep... What's bothering you?
Regongar：你不记得了，对吗？昨天晚上你乱踢乱打、发出尖叫，把我吵醒了四次。不过看来你只是在梦游……啊，科技联盟那些混蛋，想起来就气啊！
You don't remember, do you? You woke me up four times last night, kicking and screaming. But I guess you didn't wake up... Ha, what those bastards from the League did to us!
Octavia：老瑞，你知道有的时候我想要做什么吗？结束这种四处流浪的生活，在一条小河边建一幢房子，安定下来，再造条船，一边打渔一边打理花园，坐在楼梯上看日落……
Reg, do you know what I want sometimes? To quit all this traveling, settle down somewhere by a river, build a house, make a boat, fish, take care of the garden, watch the sunset, sitting on the stairs...
Regongar：这个梦想真美！有一种舒适的感觉。我不知道自己能不能过上这样的生活，不过……
That's a good dream! So cozy. I'm not sure I could live like that, but...
Octavia：不行，老瑞，这种酒我一滴都不想喝。我没跟你说过不要去找那个小贩吗？我肯定说过。你干什么？你砍了他一刀！
No, Reg, I don't want any of that wine. Didn't I ask you to leave that vendor alone? I know I did. And what did you do? Slashed him!
Regongar：怎么了？我又没杀死他！好了好了，别生气。他比我们有钱多了，损失几瓶酒有什么了不起的？
Yeah, so what? I didn't kill him! There there, don't pout. He's richer than both of us, what does he care about a few bottles?
Octavia：科技联盟一定还会派出杀手来追杀我们。我怕他们迟早会找到我们，把我们抓回去！
The Technic League must be still sending hunters after us. I'm so afraid they'll find us sooner or later and drag back!
Regongar：要是他们真的找到了我们，那倒霉的肯定是他们！科技联盟敢派人到我们这里来，有多少我杀多少！
If they find us – it's their loss! I'll kill as many Technic League snoops as they dare to send us!

Quiet, quiet! No way. They're all right as they are!

You look unhappy today. You need to have some fun. You want me to read to you in bed, the way you like?
Regongar：奥克塔维雅！可恶，别在人这么多的时候……唔，算了。回头再说吧。
OCTAVIA! Damn it, not with everyone around... Hmm, never mind. We'll talk about this later.
Octavia：崔斯提安，我很佩服你的耐心。你难道从来没有想要骂两句、或者揍谁一顿的念头吗？
I marvel at your patience, Tristian. Didn't you ever want to curse, or yell, or smash someone's face?
Tristian：当然，我也有过这种感觉。我对于普通的人类情绪并不陌生。不过要成为莎恩芮的信徒就不能将这些阴暗的冲动释放出来。
I have felt those feelings, of course. I'm not a stranger to common human passions. But it doesn't become a servant of Sarenrae to act out his sordid urges.
Octavia：崔斯提安，你这么年轻，却见多识广！你要一直这样坚持你的信仰到世界终结，这一定很艰难！
You are so young, Tristian, but you've seen so much! It must be hard, carrying your faith to all the ends of the world!
Tristian：是啊，奥克塔维雅，这的确是非常沉重的负担。有的时候我也会怀疑自己是否能够承受。不过我会闭上眼睛，向治愈之光祈祷，然后就可以感受到我的女神的存在，然后我就明白了……正所谓我不下地狱谁下地狱？
Yes, Octavia, it's a heavy burden. Sometimes I doubt I can handle it. But I close my eyes and appeal to the Healing Light, and I feel the presence of my goddess, and I know – if not me, then who?
Octavia：每次我聊到奴隶或是自由，你都会露出那个表情……就像我在其他奴隶脸上看到的表情。不过你从未被铐上枷锁，对吗？
When I talk about slavery and freedom, you get that face... Like I saw on the other slaves. But you've never been in shackles, have you?
Tristian：我们每一个人都有自己的枷锁。只是有的枷锁并不是那么容易看到罢了。
Each of us is shackled in our own way. Some shackles are just harder to see than others.
Octavia：告诉我，如果你遇到一名科技联盟的走狗，你会治疗他吗？
Tell me, if you met a dying Technic League henchman, would you heal him?
Tristian：是的。请不要皱眉，你问我这个问题，我必须如实回答。然后我就会试图劝说他改过自新、或将他送上法庭接受审判……也有可能他会攻击我，那么我就只能亲手杀死他。不过我绝不会对奄奄一息的人见死不救。
Yes. Now don't frown – you asked me a question and that's my answer. Then I'd turn him to the light or deliver him to authorities for a trial – or maybe he'd attack me and I'd have to kill him myself. But I would never walk past a dying man indifferently.
Octavia：你真是奇怪，崔斯提安。你对于玩笑和攻讦是如此容忍，总是安静的倾听，然后原谅对方……有的人会以为你是在示弱，不过我知道在内心里你比钢铁还要坚强。
You are strange, Tristian. You put up so patiently with jokes and insults, and you're always ready to hear out and forgive... Some may see this as weakness, but I know that inside you are harder than steel.
Tristian：谢谢你，奥克塔维雅。很可惜，大多数人都无法分辨软弱和强大之间的真正区别。
Thank you, Octavia. Too bad people so seldom recognize the true difference between weakness and strength.
Octavia：崔斯提安，每次我一提到自由，你就开始沉思。这是为什么呢？
Tristian, you become so thoughtful whenever I speak of freedom. Why is that?
Tristian：我经常问自己是什么自由，它真正的价值是什么。对于我来说，那就是能够跟随自己内心、摒弃邪恶的机会。
I often ask myself what freedom means, and what its true value might be. For me, it's the chance to follow my heart and turn away from evil things.
Octavia：你不应该摒弃所有与美或艺术有关的东西。你的针线活很不错！就算你现在已经不是一名雪琳的圣武士了，不过你为什么就不能单纯去做你擅长的事呢？
You shouldn't reject everything related to beauty and art. You sew very well! You may not be a paladin of Shelyn any more, but why don't you want to simply do what you're good at?
Valerie：我擅长战斗，这就是我要做的事情。而我在骑士团学到的其他东西都是毫无意义的胡闹，并不值得我为之付出时间。
I'm good at fighting, and that's what I do. Everything else I learned at the Order is useless nonsense, and not worth even a moment of my time.
Octavia：我明白你是要剃发明志，不过……你会不会再把头发留长呢？你懂的，只是为了换个发型？
I know you cut your hair to make a statement, but... Maybe you should grow it back? You know, for a change?
Valerie：等我太老了，打不动了，也许我会考虑换个发型。而现在，如果要在长头发和可以快速戴上头盔之间做一个选择，我会选后者。
I'll think about changing when I'm too old to fight. For now, when I'm deciding between long hair and getting my helmet on quickly, I'll choose the latter.
Octavia：我见过的大多数贵族都十分傲慢，即便是对待地位相当的人也是一样。而你却并不介意和一名曾经的奴隶共同进餐！
Most nobles I've seen were arrogant, even with their equals. And you aren't ashamed to be sharing a meal with former slaves!
Valerie：我在佣兵之中已经生活了很长的时间，那群人里从贵族到黥面罪犯，三教九流都有。在这样的环境中，你很快就会学到应该以一个人的行为去评判他，而不看是他的家族纹章是什么颜色或家世是否深厚。
I've lived among mercenaries for a long time, and they're a motley bunch – from nobles to branded convicts. In a situation like that, you quickly learn to judge people by what they do, not by the color of their coat of arms or how ancient their lineage is.
Octavia：你不怎么爱笑，瓦尔瑞。有什么事让你很苦恼吗？
You smile rarely, Valerie. What troubles you?
Valerie：没有。如果有可笑的事情，我就会笑。不过现在我正忙着思考我们要如何在这片荒野中生存下去。
Nothing. I'll laugh when there is something to laugh about. Right now I'm busy thinking about how we're going to survive these wild lands.
Octavia：瓦尔瑞，你能和我们一起旅行真是太好了。你就像一棵雄伟的橡树，可以让我们依靠……哦！我并不是说你是个野蛮的人……也不是说你身材不好什么的！哦天啊，我总是笨嘴笨舌的。
It's good you're with us, Valerie. You're like a mighty oak we can lean our backs against... Oh! But I didn't mean you're thickheaded – or thick any other way! Oh no, I put my foot in my mouth again.
Valerie：没关系，我明白你是出于好意。我也很高兴能和朋友们在一起。
It's all right – I understand your good intentions. I'm glad to be here for my friends.

You are amazingly beautiful... No, no, I'm not flirting! Although... I'm not saying I wouldn't like it!
Valerie：不要浪费你的时间了。也许有一天我会找一个人共度余生。不过肯定会是个男人！
Don't waste your time. Maybe someday I'll find someone to share my life with. But it will definitely be a man!
Octavia：瓦尔瑞，你的绣工活真漂亮，你的设计都让我移不开眼睛了！就连你衣服上打的补丁都显得那么特别。这种事情我就做不来，哪怕用魔法来辅助都不行！
Valerie, your embroidery is always so beautiful, I just can't take my eyes off your designs! Even the patches on your clothes are always special. I could never do anything like that, even with the help of magic!
Valerie：哦，这……没什么了不起的。我也不是想要刻意装饰。只是有的时候我闲不下来而已。
Oh, it's… nothing special. I never mean to do anything ornate. I just need to keep my hands busy sometimes.

I advised that we hide the camp. Monsters can be a problem, as are evil-minded people.

I agree. I've no concern for sleep, but I require silence and repose no less than the rest of you.
NoHunting

So, rations for lunch today? — Great! I've got dried apples!
