Kalikke/Kanerah：你知道为什么吗？因为我觉得没必要把时间浪费在一个从来不听别人说话的自大莽夫身上。
And you know why? I don't see any point in wasting my time on an arrogant bully who never listens to anyone anyway.
Regongar：琳翠，你有吃的吗？我的肚子一直咕咕叫，像是一只虎鼠一样。
Linzi, got any grub? My stomach is growling like an angry tiger-rat.
Linzi：并没有虎鼠这种东西！不过听你肚子发出的声音，我猜你可能真的找到了一只，然后把它整个囫囵吞了下去！
But tiger-rats don't exist! Although from the sound of your stomach, I'd have guessed you'd caught one and swallowed it whole!
Regongar：琳翠，我问你，你能不能写篇我的故事？在这个故事里，我冲进一群敌人之中，把他们的脑袋都砍了下来，然后把他们的尸体踢开，最后像一个疯子似的狂笑起来，怎么样？
Listen, Linzi, can you write a story about me? One where I'm bursting into a crowd of enemies, lopping off their heads, and then kicking their corpses and laughing like a madman?
Linzi：这是什么烂故事？你还是找个传记作家帮你写吧。
What kind of story is that? That's your biographer's job.

Hey, are you writing about our last brawl? What are you saying about me?
Linzi：唔，你当时非常……可怕。你会射出闪电，还一直骂骂咧咧的。瑞贡尕，你在战斗中好歹也说一些充满英雄气概的话啊！你说的那些东西我根本没法下笔！而且我又不能撒谎，写你在战斗中一语不发，对不对？
Well, you were... horrible. You shot lightning. And just cursed. Regongar, please say something heroic in battle at least once! I can't write down the stuff you say! And I can't lie that you were fighting in silence, now can I?
Regongar：你的高度正合适痛击敌人的下盘！你为什么不试试呢？面对任何敌人，只要用出这招就赢定了！
You're the perfect height for stabbing someone in the groin! Why don't you do it? That's a winning move in any battle!
Linzi：唔……听起来一点都不帅气！我读的所有书里的英雄都不会用这种下三滥的招数！
Well... it doesn't sound very heroic! None of the heroes from the books I've read ever did this!
Regongar：嘿，小虫子！给我们唱一首你们地精的歌吧！
Hey, gnat! Sing us one of your goblin songs!

Let me think. Hmmm. No. You stink.

It's so great to be free! To breath deep, without a collar around your throat! Not to fear the whip, and go wherever you like... Right, my Octavia?

Reg... I've asked you, don't call me 'yours'. Every time you say that, you make me feel like I'm back in chains!

I saw a book here... It was quite interesting – with pictures! I think it was called 'Jade Wand Unlocks Jasper Gates'. Too bad I couldn't steal it. But I remember a few pictures!

Remember that day in Starfall? I was laying there with broken ribs and my face all smashed up, and you rocked my head on your knees and wiped the blood from my face...

Atonement cannot be bought, you're right about that. It can only be realized by righteous action. And every one of us can reform and become better than we were before!

The Healing Light burns in the hearts of thousands upon thousands of believers. In trying to insult my goddess, you're only displaying your own ignorance.

The ruins of the ancients are pleasant to the eye, Regongar, for they remind us of the frailty of life. Those who left them are gone, and now we follow in their footsteps. Listen to the silence that remains — does it not fill your spirit with calm?

Octavia：打起精神来，老瑞。这些东西没那么难吃。比我们小时候吃的那些剩饭强多了……
Chin up, Reg. These meals aren't so bad. Better than the slop we ate as children...
Tristian：治愈之力，是我的女神最伟大的恩赐。
Healing is my goddess' greatest gift.
Tristian：要是我可以不那么频繁地使用这种力量就好了……
If only I didn't have to use it so often...
Tristian：失窃之地是个非常奇异的地方，这里有着如此多的不公和痛苦……
The Stolen Lands are a very strange place. There is so much injustice and pain here...
Tristian：不过同时也有荣誉、骄傲、英勇、宽恕和赎罪，所有这一切彼此缠绕、难分难解。
But then there is honor, pride, valor, forgiveness and atonement. Everything twisted up in a tight ball.
Kalikke&Kanerah
Tristian：人生是一场接连不断的试炼……莎恩芮，请赐予我力量，让我带着尊严通过所有考验！
All life is a succession of tests... Sarenrae, grant me the strength to pass them all with dignity!
Kalikke/Kanerah：至少我看出来了，你自己都保不准能否有尊严地面对考验。
At least it's good to know you doubt your ability to face trials with dignity.
Tristian：这世上有如此多的痛苦和磨难……为什么我们不能生活在和平中呢？为什么大家不愿意去宽恕呢？
There is so much pain, so much suffering in the world... Why can't we all live in peace? Why are we so bad at forgiving?
Kalikke/Kanerah：为什么我总觉得，良知最为晦暗的人对宽恕的怨言也最大声呢？
And why do I feel that those whose conscience is darkest, are the ones who complain the loudest about forgiveness?
Tristian：希望我的女神能聆听我的祈祷，没有她的慈爱，我们根本无法在这片土地上生存下来。
I hope my goddess hears me. Without her favor, we won't make it through these lands.
Kalikke/Kanerah：她什么也听不到，否则她不会允许狂信者以她的名义干出那些事，不过也可能她压根不在乎。
She hears nothing, or she wouldn't allow the things that fanatics do in her name. Or more likely – she simply doesn't care.
Tristian：我知道你在我的同教兄弟手中受了很多苦，但这并不意味着你需要提防我，凯希。
I know you've endured much at the hands of my brothers in faith. But this doesn't mean you should be wary of me, Kaessi.
Kalikke/Kanerah：你为什么会觉得我是因为你的信仰才不信任你呢？你有蛇一般的目光，祭司。
Why do you think it's because of your faith that I don't trust you? You have the eyes of a snake, priest.
Tristian：你总是指责我虚伪、满口谎言，但你隐瞒的东西不比我少。
You've accused me so many times of hypocrisy and lies. But you've been hiding no less than I.
Kalikke/Kanerah：我们姐妹俩跟你完全不同，我们是不得已才隐瞒实情，也从未背叛过同伴。
My sister and I are nothing like you. We were forced to hide our true nature, and we never betrayed our companions.
Tristian：仅仅因为那些误入歧途最深的信徒就敌视整个信仰，这真的对吗？当你住在卡蒂拉时，肯定也有亲友追随莎恩芮。
Is it right to turn against an entire faith because of its most misguided adherents? While you lived in Qadira, you must have had friends and loved ones who followed the path of Sarenrae.
Kalikke：的确有，但让我的朋友们变得亲切公正的可不是莎恩芮的教导。
This is true. But it was not Sarenrae's teachings that made my friends kind and just.
Kanerah：你不会真以为我那么傻吧？我确信我们能在拉玛什图的信徒里找到好人，在凯登·凯林的追随者里发现戒酒者。但我见多了莎恩芮的狂热信徒，足以让我对他们产生最糟糕的印象，所以你可以管这叫小心谨慎。
You don't think I'm that stupid, do you? I'm sure we could find kindness among the worshippers of Lamashtu, and abstainers among the followers of Cayden Cailean. But I've seen enough zealous servants of Sarenrae to suspect them of the worst. Call it prudent caution, if you wish.
Tristian：别误会，懂得宽恕和懂得战斗同样重要。如果对手下败将不抱仁慈，那么胜利者也无荣耀可言！
Don't get me wrong, knowing how to forgive is as important as knowing how to fight. Without mercy for the defeated, the victor wins no glory!
Amiri：你懂什么荣耀？击败所有对手的人就是最荣耀的。最仁慈的事就是利落地干掉敌人，让他们死得没有痛苦。
What do you know about glory?! The one who defeats everyone else is the most glorious. And the best mercy is killing fast, without suffering.
Tristian：你是个好人，安米芮。其实你心地善良，只是如果能控制住冲动的脾气就好了……
You're sweet, Amiri. I'm sure you have a kind heart. If only you learned to control your passions...
Amiri：如果一个野蛮人没有脾气，那就像是没有……嗯……没有胆量！对！如果我不再愤怒，那么在你念叨祷词时谁来保护你呢，嗯？
A barbarian without passions is like without... hmm... – without guts, that's it! If I lose my rage, who will fight for you while you babble your prayers, hunh?
Tristian：安米芮，你在你那荒芜的故乡所经历的磨难，使你变得非常坚韧。
The trials and tribulations of your fruitless land have hardened you, Amiri.
Amiri：没错！像你这种南方小子在我们那儿连一天都撑不下去。你要么就会被冻死，要么就会被人抓住当零食一样生吃了！
That's right! A southern boy like you, wouldn't last a day with us. – You'd either freeze or someone would eat you for a snack!
Tristian：你不该对莎恩芮如此不敬，她是一名强大的女神，击败过无数凶残的怪物，她那把辉煌的宝剑曾将许多城市从这个世间抹去！
You shouldn't treat Sarenrae with such disdain. She's a great goddess, who has defeated many horrible monsters. Her shining sword has wiped whole cities from the face of the world!
Amiri：哈，不错嘛！那为什么你一直在念叨仁慈和宽恕之类的话？跟我讲讲她如何屠杀她的敌人！
Ha, not bad! Then why do you keep nagging about mercy and forgiveness? Tell me about how she slaughtered her enemies!
Tristian：仁慈并不等于懦弱，莎恩芮一手施以治愈之光，而另一手握持——杀戮之剑！
Merciful doesn't mean spineless. In one hand, Sarenrae holds a healing light, in the other – a killing sword!
Amiri：我知道你很会使你那把小刀子，而且你也不会逃避战斗。不过每次战斗结束后，你总是满脸痛苦。既然你不享受，那为什么还要战斗呢？
I know you are good with your little sword. And you don't run away in a fight. But after a battle you always have that face, like you just swallowed a hedgehog. What's the point of fighting if you don't enjoy it?
Kanerah：你听了可能会被吓到，祭司小子，但我就是那把剑，我已经砍翻了不少人，可能比你还多。
You might be surprised to hear this, boy-priest, but I was that sword, and I've cut down many. Perhaps even more than you.
Tristian：你有没有想过放弃当战士呢？找桩新差事？金盆洗手，放下屠刀？
Have you ever thought of leaving the warrior's path? Finding a new task? Putting away your sword?
Amiri：放下屠刀？是啊，然后就会有人把它偷走！我这把武器还是挺值钱的！
Putting away? Yeah, right. It would get stolen right away! And it's pretty valuable!
Tristian：很抱歉，艾昆，我无法减轻你的痛苦……
I'm sorry that I cannot relieve your pain, Ekun...
Ekun：不必自责，这是我要自己承担的。
Don't blame yourself. It is my burden.
Tristian：艾昆，你为什么不愿意跟我们讲讲你的家人呢？人需要将心中的郁结倾诉出来……
Why don't you tell us about your family, Ekun? Everyone needs to get things off their chest sometimes...
Ekun：话语并不能将他们复活，你帮不了我，请像我一样接受这个事实。
Words will not bring them back. You can't help me. Accept this, as I have.
Tristian：如果你能找个伴，或许就会舒坦些？
Maybe you'd feel better if you found someone?
Ekun：我有你这个朋友，就已经很开心了。多杀几只巨魔，也能让我开心。这就够了。
I have you. That makes me feel better. Killing trolls – that makes me feel better too. It's enough.
Tristian：莎恩芮对众生施以仁慈，但那些摧毁了你村庄的人……我相信即便是我的女神，也无法找到宽恕他们的理由。
Sarenrae grants mercy to all. But the ones who did this to your village... I think even my goddess wouldn't find words of forgiveness for them.
Ekun：是的，不是所有事情都能被宽恕，有些事情根本就不应该得到宽恕。
Yes. Not everything can be forgiven. And there are some things that shouldn't be.
Tristian：说真的，艾昆，也许你也应该成为莎恩芮的信徒，她能治愈你内心的伤痛。
You know, Ekun, perhaps you should join those who venerate Sarenrae. She could heal the wounds on your heart.
Ekun：托拉格是个不错的神祇，在我最脆弱的时候，当我奄奄一息的时候，他与我同在。为什么我现在要背弃他呢？
Torag is a good god. He was with me when I was at my weakest. When I was dying. Why should I turn away from him now?
Tristian：我看到你悉心照顾你的狼，这是非常高尚且仁慈的行为。
I see how you care about your wolf. This is very noble and merciful.
Ekun：我们相依为命，互相照顾，和高尚无关。
We take care of each other. That's how it is in a pack. It's nothing to do with nobleness.
Tristian：你对所有人都保持距离，不愿意接近任何人，这让你内心你的创伤无法愈合。放过你自己，和大家一起到篝火边坐坐吧。
You keep your distance, and don't get close to anyone. This keeps your soul's wounds from healing. Forgive yourself and sit at the fire with the rest of us.
Ekun：心生羁绊是危险的，我无法再承受失去亲人和朋友的痛苦了。
Attachment is dangerous. I could not endure further losses.
Tristian：崇拜格罗图斯有什么意义？你自己也说过，当那一刻来临时，他会摧毁所有人，包括他自己的信徒！
What's the point in worshipping Groetus? You've said it yourself: when the moment comes, he will destroy everyone, including his congregation!
Harrim：崔斯提安，这就是意义。在格罗图斯面前众生平等，不论是否是他的信徒，不论是凡人还是神明。唯有他值得敬奉，其余一切尽是虚妄！
That's exactly why, Tristian. In the face of Groetus, everyone is equal – believers and non-believers, people and gods. Only he is worth venerating, because everything else is empty and meaningless!
Tristian：世界将归于虚无，你会为此高兴吗？如果有办法可以阻止格罗图斯前来摧毁世界，你会这么做吗？
Are you happy that the world will cease to exist? What if there was a way to stop Groetus from coming? Would you do it?
Harrim：你不明白……格罗图斯和洛瓦古格不一样。他并不是个毁灭者，只是一个昭示者，提醒着大家，万事万物终有一死。每件事物本身的终局就是毁灭，末日只能延缓，而无法避免。
You don't understand... Groetus is not a destructor like Rovagug. He is merely the herald of the fact that the world and everyone in it are mortal by their nature. Everything carries inside it the seed of its own destruction. It can be delayed, but never prevented.
Tristian：我很担心你，哈瑞姆。那道低语……我担心它会损害你的心智。
I'm afraid for you, Harrim. This whisper... I fear it might do terrible harm to your mind.
Harrim：崔斯提安啊崔斯提安……别人说我疯了，我能理解，但你是受过圣礼、接触过神祗奥秘的祭司！如果莎恩芮的启示也会损害你的心智，你会拒绝她吗？
Tristian, Tristian... I understand when others talk to me about insanity, but you are a priest – anointed to mysteries of your deity! Would you reject the revelations of Sarenrae, if they would cost you your mind?
Tristian：有时我觉得，我们在宗教信仰上的争论毫无意义……
Sometimes I feel our arguments about religion are pointless...
Harrim：那当然了，崔斯提安！一切都是浮华和徒劳，我们的谈话不过是树叶沙沙虚响……不过，你得承认，你非常享受我们之间的交谈。
Of course they are, Tristian! Of course. Everything is vanity and fuss. Our conversations are nothing more than the rustling of leaves... And yet, admit it, you do enjoy our little talks!
Tristian：我只希望你那位怪异的神祇不会命令你自尽。
I just hope your strange god will never demand that you take your own life.
Harrim：当然不会，他为什么要这样做？他不需要祭品，也不会要求他的信徒去做这种本就无法避免的事情。归向格罗图斯走的是沉思之道，而不是毁灭。
Of course not. Why would he? He doesn't need sacrifices. He doesn't demand that his followers carry out what is in any case inevitable anyhow. The path to Groetus is the path of contemplation, not destruction.
Tristian：也许借助神力可以治愈你那非自然的不死之身？你真的甘愿做一具行尸走肉吗？
Perhaps with divine assistance you could be healed of your unnatural unlife? Do you really prefer to be a walking corpse?
Jaethal：你在骗谁呢，祭司？我还是你自己？你那女神能赐给我的‘治疗’不过是一死而已。
Who are you lying to, priest? – Me or yourself? The only 'healing' your goddess can offer me is a final death.
Tristian：我保证，耶萨尔，总有一天莎恩芮的治愈之光会照耀着你。
I swear, there will be a day, Jaethal, that the healing light of Sarenrae touches you too.
Jaethal：你那糟糕的布道如此聒噪，如果我真的用心听的话，恐怕它们会让我从这个世界消失吧
I could joke that your sermons are so bad that they might drive me from the world... If I paid them more attention than I pay the buzzing of flies.
Tristian：在一次战斗结束后，我看到你蹲在一具死尸前。你对他做了什么？施展某种黑暗魔法吗？回答我！
I saw how after a battle you were leaning over one of the dead. What did you do to him? Some kind of dark magic? Answer me!
Jaethal：你觉得你有资格来质问我吗？哼，我也不介意满足一下你的好奇心。我在与他逝去的亡魂交谈。你想知道内容吗？我可以直接送你去见他，这样你就可以亲口问他了。
You think you have the right to demand something from me? Well, I don't mind satisfying your curiosity. I spoke with his passing spirit. Would you like to know what about? I could send you to him, and you could ask him yourself.
Tristian：每次我转过身，都能感觉到你那尖锐的目光，如同要把我刺穿。有哪里不对劲吗？
Every time I turn away, I feel your eyes boring a hole in my back. Is there something wrong?
Jubilost：没事，我只是看不懂你，年轻人。如果朱比罗斯特·纳斯罗泊不懂一件事情，他一定会打破砂锅问到底，并查个水落石出！
No, it's fine. I just don't understand you, young man. And when Jubilost Narthropple doesn't understand something, he tries to get to the bottom of it. And he succeeds!
Tristian：我注意到你在看一张喀勒什地图，你是想找出我的故乡在哪吗？如果你想，我可以指给你看。
I noticed you looking at a map of Kelesh. Are you trying to find where I'm from? I can show you if you'd like.
Jubilost：当然……不过我并不是在找这个，其实我对于喀勒什习俗也略有了解，而且……算了，没事。你只要知道，我一定会追查到底的！
Of course you can... But that's not what I was looking for. You see, I know a few things about the customs of Kelesh, and... Anyway, it doesn't matter. I'll get to the bottom of this, be sure of that!
Tristian：我这辈子见过不少坏脾气的人，不过你真是坏脾气的标杆！
I've met some grumpy men in life, but you are the gold standard of grumpiness!'
Jubilost：而你则是吹毛求疵的典范啊！我见识渊博，接受过高等教育，拥有丰富的人生经验！与其抱怨，不如多听我的忠告，说不定还能有所收获！
And you are the very model of hair-splitting! You know, I've seen many things – and I'm talking with the benefit of experience and education! Instead of complaining, you'd better just listen. You might learn something!
Tristian：你一直闷闷不乐，抱怨不断……但你从来没有扬言要离开队伍。
You're constantly unhappy, always complaining... But you never threaten to leave the party.
Jubilost：心存不满才能保持头脑清醒！安逸知足会让人变胖变蠢……甚至会让侏儒褪色。也许我算不上热心，却总能见识到各式各样的新鲜事！
Discontent is the normal condition of a sound mind! Comfort and contentment lead one to grow fat and stupid – and even cause gnomes to bleach. I may not be entirely warmhearted, but I'm never at a loss for new experiences!
Tristian：你总是对身边的人说那么刻薄的话，难道不会觉得羞愧吗？
Aren't you ever ashamed of all the nasty things you say to those around you?
Jubilost：算我求你，拿出你那一贯的仁慈，别再说教我了好吗？以莎恩芮的名义！
I beg you, demonstrate your famous mercy and spare me the lecture! In the name of Sarenrae!
Tristian：你的脑子就像是一座阁楼，塞满了书本和奖杯，都没有地方用来容纳最简单的同情心了！
Your head is like an attic – so stuffed with books and trophies that there's no place left for simple compassion!
Jubilost：而你的脑子就像是一座莎恩芮神殿，富丽堂皇，可惜里面只有香薰与日光！
And your head is like a temple of Sarenrae – beautiful, roomy, and filled with incense and sunlight!
Tristian：你跪拜雪琳吗？有多久了？
You bow before Shelyn, right? For how long?
Linzi：跪拜？啊对！我这么矮还跪拜？我要是跪拜，我就被淹没在草丛里了。我拜就拜，不用跪，谢谢。我从小就信奉雪琳了，村里其他人都信奉艾若斯提，不过那可太无聊了！
Bow? Yeah, right! As short as I am? – When I bow, I disappear into the grass. I'll pray standing up, thank you. I started praying to Shelyn when I was young. Everyone else in our village worshipped Erastil, but that's so boring!
Tristian：我喜欢你写的故事，内容真假无关紧要，这些故事为我们带来安宁与希望，而这正是我们很多人所匮乏的。
I like your stories – and it doesn't matter how much truth there is in them. They bring peace and give us hope – something so many of us lack.
Linzi：太好了！终于有人理解故事的真正价值了！大家都说我只是在编故事，不过是逃避现实……谢谢，你的话对我来说简直像久旱逢甘泉！
There! At least someone understands the true benefit of storytelling! Everyone keeps saying it's 'genre work' or 'escapist'… Thank you, your words are like an elixir to my soul!
Tristian：我喜欢看书，在神殿的时候，我会花好几个小时翻阅那些蒙尘的书卷和前人残破的手稿。
I like reading books. Back at the temple I spent many hours among dusty folios and ragged manuscripts.
Linzi：噢，你在那里看的书一定都很无聊，圣人传记、启蒙之人的教诲……无！聊！传说和冒险故事才有趣！
Oh, the books you have there must be so boring. Biographies of saints, the teachings of the enlightened... Boooring! Legends and sagas are completely different!
Tristian：你有没有想过成为莎恩芮信徒？你有一颗仁慈之心，灵魂也十分高洁。你一定会喜欢我们阳光灿烂的神殿！
Have you ever thought of joining Sarenrae's followers? You are merciful and your soul is pure. You would enjoy our sunlit temples.
Linzi：很抱歉，应该不行。我对神殿过敏，严重过敏！只要一走近神殿，我就会忍不住狂笑，然后把所有蜡烛都笑灭。
I'm sorry, but that's not going to work. I'm allergic to temples. Seriously! As soon as I enter one, I start laughing so hard that the candles go out.
Tristian：琳翠，你令我惊叹。你见识过那么多残暴、粗俗、甚至令人作呕的事情，却还能保持一颗童真的心，乐观又随性，这是多么美好的天赋！
You amaze me, Linzi. You've seen so many gruesome, vulgar, and disgusting things, but kept your childlike joyfulness and spontaneity. It's such a beautiful gift!
Linzi：我有点不明白了，你说这话是在夸我，还是在暗暗骂我？
I couldn't understand that. Was it a praise or a subtle insult?
Tristian：大学院里一定还有很多与你信仰相同的人……
There must have been many others of your faith at the Academy...
Linzi：你可能想不到，其实没有！虽然它叫大艺术学院，但并没有多少人赞颂美之女神。卡丽斯翠和德丝娜反倒更受欢迎！
You'd be surprised, but no! It's called the Academy of Grand Arts, but the goddess of beauty isn't widely honored there. Calistria and Desna are much more common!
Tristian：诺克诺克，你就没想过做一些改变吗？变得比你那些野蛮的同族更文明些？
Didn't you ever think about changing your ways, Nok-Nok? To be a bit less savage than your wild brethren?
NokNok：诺克诺克和其他人不一样，诺克诺克想成为英雄！其他人并没有，这就已经很不同了，为什么还要改变更多？
Nok-Nok is not like the others. Nok-Nok wants to be a Hero! Others don't. That's enough difference, why more?
Tristian：我总觉得即便是对于诺克诺克的同族来说，他也太过于瘦弱。不过当我看到他的食量以后，就放心了。
It always appears to me that Nok-Nok is too skinny even for his own kind. But when I see how much he eats I know that he is fine.
NokNok：不！诺克诺克都快饿死了！把你的口粮给我！我比你更需要食物！
No! Nok-Nok starves! Give me your ration! I need it more than you!
Tristian：很高兴有你同行，你就像一缕阳光，让悲伤的日子都变得快乐起来。
I'm glad you are with us. You are like a ray of sunshine – you make even sad days more joyful.
Octavia：噢，别再说了！我也庆幸有你，要不是你，我现在还身陷科技联盟的镣铐之中呢！
Oh, enough! I'm lucky to have you. If it weren't for you, I'd still be in the Technic League's chains!
Tristian：你看上去非常疲惫，该休息一下了。
You look tired. I think you should rest.
Octavia：谢谢你的关心，确实是，不过现在还有很多事要做！
Thank you for caring. I'm sure you're right. But there's so much to do!
Tristian：让我欣慰的是，奴隶生活并没有让你变得铁石心肠，你的意志非常坚韧。
I'm glad that being a slave didn't harden your heart. You are very strong.
Octavia：我……我就是我咯，那些奴隶主也想让我变得和他们一样邪恶……所以为了不让他们得逞，我就变成了相反的样子！
I... I'm just the way I am. I think my owners wanted me to become as wicked as they were… So I did the opposite out of spite!
Tristian：如果你愿意，我可以试着治愈你背上的伤疤。
If you want, I could try to heal the scars on your back.
Octavia：那确实很好，不过恐怕不会起作用。那些伤疤……都只是映照出来的表象，你能明白吗？真正的伤疤都烙印在了我的灵魂里，无法消除。
That would be nice! But I'm afraid it wouldn't work. Some scars are... It's like they're just a reflection on my skin, you know? But you can't remove the real scar, that's on my soul.
Tristian：奴隶生活并没有将你击垮，不过你能不能原谅那些伤害过你的人，给他们一个赎罪的机会？
Slavery didn't break you. But could you forgive those who hurt you, and give them a chance to atone?
Octavia：想都别想！那帮恶人有过上百次、上千次、无数次机会！如果他们把所有奴隶都放了，我才会考虑原谅他们。
Hell no! These wretches had a hundred chances. A thousand! Millions! I'll think about forgiving them when they set all their other slaves free.
Tristian：瑞贡尕，听我说……
Regongar, listen...
Regongar：不！我不想听！你的大道理我真是听够了！烦别人去吧！
No! I'm not going to listen! I've heard enough preaching! Go bother someone else!
Tristian：就没有什么办法可以让你放下仇恨、去原谅那些折磨你的人吗？
Isn't there anything that could set you free of your hatred, and let you forgive your tormentors?
Regongar：哦……等我把他们的肠子全部扯出来以后，自然可以原谅他们所有人！
Oh, I would definitely forgive all of them... When I wrapped their guts around my fists!
Tristian：我知道你不喜欢我，瑞贡尕。但别忘了，我们有着共同的目标！
I realize you don't like me, Regongar. But don't forget, we share in the common cause!
Regongar：是啊，有的人在为这个目标奋战，而有的人却畏缩在草丛里。怎么，你这个老好人，怕你那小手上沾血吗？
Someone is sharing in it, while someone else is cowering in the bushes. What, goody-goody, are you afraid of getting blood on your little hands?
Tristian：瑞贡尕，你大可以随意诋毁我，莎恩芮教导我要宽容，背地里的恶言都源自无助，而非愤怒。
You can say any awful thing you want about me, Regongar. Sarenrae teaches me to forgive. Insulting words spoken behind someone's back come from helplessness, not from anger.
Regongar：背地里？每次我想到什么都会当面跟你说的，哈哈哈！
Behind your back? I say everything I can think of to your face. Every chance I get, ha ha!
Tristian：瑞贡尕，奴隶生活摧残了你的肉体，更摧残了你的灵魂。为什么你不希望得到治愈呢？
Slavery maimed your soul more than your body, Regongar. Why don't you wish to be healed?
Regongar：因为我本来就好得很，你这个蠢货。我对那些奴隶主满怀正当的怒火，而我完好的双手将把他们撕成碎片，我哪里需要治愈？
Because I'm already healthy, you fool. And full of healthy anger towards those who kept me in chains. And I have two healthy hands that I'm gonna use to rip them to pieces. What do I need healing for?
Tristian：我倡导心怀仁慈，而你却始终表现出兽性般的愤怒，你一定会认为我很懦弱吧？但我并不懦弱！
You must think I'm a coward because I encourage showing mercy where you show everyone animal rage? But I am no coward!
Regongar：嗯，懦夫不敢对着我这种危险人物宣扬什么仁慈！所以你确实不懦弱，但你仍是个无聊的老好人！
Well, a coward wouldn't come bugging a dangerous fellow like me, preaching absolution! So you're not a coward. But you're still a boring goody-goody!
Tristian：你行事残忍，犯下了那么多暴行……如果你想倾诉心中的罪孽，我随时愿意听你告解。
You've done so many cruel things, so many atrocities... If you want to get anything off your chest, I'm always ready to listen to your confession.
Regongar：如果我要到你帐篷里去，那么一定是去犯罪的，而不是去告解的，哈哈哈！
If I come to your tent, it'll be to sin, not to confess, ha ha ha!
Tristian：我们对敌人是不是太残酷了？也许他们之中仍有人是可以弃暗投明的。
Are we too cruel to our enemies? Perhaps some of them could still have been turned to the light.
Regongar：你说‘我们’是什么意思？对敌人残酷的是我，你只是站一边念叨祷词而已！
What do you mean 'we'? I'm the one who's cruel to our enemies. You're the one who stands there babbling your prayers!
Tristian：你能瞒得过别人，却瞒不了我。你的愤怒其实是一种掩饰，在内心深处，你只是个惶恐不安、渴求温暖的小男孩。
You can fool the others, but not me. Your big show of anger is just a mask. Deep down you're just a scared little boy who needs warmth.
Regongar：没错啊，我确实想要温暖……要不今晚你到我被子里来，让我温暖温暖？哈哈哈！
That's true, I like warmth... Why don't you visit me tonight and warm my bed, hunh? Ha ha ha!

I don't understand Calistria's teaching. How can you see virtue in hatred, the poisoning of the soul?
Regongar：你要是不懂就别说了，卡丽斯翠并不鼓励仇恨，而是复仇！差别还是挺大的，啊哈哈！
If you don't understand it, don't talk about it. Calistria doesn't teach hatred. She teaches revenge! Big difference, ha ha!
Tristian：瓦尔瑞，你如此强大、品格高尚。看着你战斗时的英姿，会让我以为自己在观看莎恩芮和洛瓦古格作战。
You are so strong and noble, Valerie. Sometimes when I see you fighting, I think of Sarenrae in her battle against Rovagug.
Valerie：这……我听到过无数夸奖，但这绝对是我有生以来听过的最高赞美！
That's… that's the greatest compliment I've ever heard in my life, I swear by it. And I've heard a great many compliments!
Tristian：大多数贵族只是名义上身份高贵，而你却有着高贵的内心。
Most nobles are noble only in words. But you have a noble heart.
Valerie：谢谢，说实话，你所说的那种徒有其表的贵族做派，恰巧是我想逃离的。评判一个人只看实际行动，而不是嘴上说辞，那才是我向往的方式。
Thank you. Truth be told, the false nobleness you mean was precisely what I ran away from. To someplace where people are tested by their deeds, not by their words.
Tristian：雪琳骑士团的生活真的那么糟糕吗？
Was it that bad at the Order of Shelyn?
Valerie：对有些人来说可能算不错了，但对我来说——不过是虚度光阴。他们所谓的高雅艺术令人乏味，我唯一的兴趣是持剑杀敌的艺术！
For some it was probably quite good, but for me – it was just a waste of time. What they call high art makes me yawn. The only art that interests me is the art of wielding a sword!
Tristian：如果你依然是圣武士，那你本可以获得许多能力，可以治疗伤口、放逐不死生物……
If you'd remained a paladin, you would have gained many powers. You could heal wounds, banish the undead...
Valerie：无意冒犯，一位神祇因你的侍奉而赐予你的力量，就如同一件祂弃置的斗篷，随手扔给你的罢了。也许我没那么多能力，但我的本领都是我自己辛苦练习学来的，没有哪位神祇能因我的不敬而将其夺走。
No offense, but the powers a deity grants in return for your service is like a cape your master doesn't need and casts aside for you. I might not be able to claim much, but I owe all my abilities to myself only. And no one is going to take them away for disobeying.

Finally some leisure time. We can celebrate our leader's birthday!

Your words heap sadness upon my soul, but relieve it as well.
Jaethal：他们早就不想听你布道了，其他人也一样。
They stopped caring about your sermons long ago. Just like everyone else.
Debuffed
Tristian：我的意志胜过任何病痛。光明保佑，我将净化身上的污秽。待到破晓时分，我将如旭日般重获新生。
My spirit is stronger than any illness. By the light I shall cleanse myself of all filth, and with the new dawn I shall rise like the Sun.
Tristian：仁慈的莎恩芮啊，我恳求您聆听我的祷告，助我治愈身躯。
Merciful Sarenrae, I humbly beg you, hear my prayers and help me heal myself.
Jubilost：你最好采取一些更可靠的治疗方式，你看起来状态不太好。
You'd better resort to surer remedies. You're not looking so good.
Amiri：打起精神来！去举举铁，或者……扛扛行李。我每次都这么做，效果可好了！
Buck up! Go pump some iron, or let's say... carry some sacks around. Helps me every time!
Octavia：崔斯提安，你脸色好苍白……不过你知道吗，这样也很适合你！
Tristian, you look so pale… but you know, it suits you well!
Linzi：请你不要再彻夜祈祷了，好好休息吧。你需要恢复体力，就算你不需要，也为别人考虑一下啊。
Just don't pray all night this time, and get some sleep. You need your strength. — If not for you, then think of the others.
Kalikke/Kanerah：我也很希望自己不用再听着莎恩芮的祷词睡着……
And I'd so hoped that I'd never again have to fall asleep listening to the sounds of prayers to Sarenrae...
Fatigued
Tristian：我感觉像是在冰冷的地牢里被关了十年，没见到过一缕阳光。
I feel I've spent a dozen years in a cold dungeon, without a single ray of sunlight.
Tristian：好久没感觉这么疲惫了，就像一年到头都被关在没有窗户的牢房里。
It's been a long time since I felt so fatigued. Like I've spent four seasons in a windowless cell.
Jubilost：莎恩芮的牧师就像花朵，照不到太阳就蔫了！好在他们不像真花那样吸引蜜蜂。
Sarenrae's clerics are like flowers — they wilt without the sun! The good thing is, unlike flowers, they don't attract bees.
Octavia：好好休息吧，崔斯提安。如果你想，我可以替你值守。
Please have a good rest, Tristian. I'll take your place keeping watch, if you like.
Harrim：真希望能和你交换一下，彼之砒霜，吾之甘露啊。
I wish I could switch places with you. One's suffering is another's blessing.
Jaethal：这话听得我下巴痛，也不知道是笑得，还是烦得？
You're making my jaw hurt. I wonder — is it laughter or disgust?
NokNok：我不喜欢太阳，地精最喜欢黑黑的地方，偷偷摸摸，更好埋伏！
I like it better without sun. Goblins like it best in dark. More sneaking, better ambushes!
InDungeon
Tristian：这是一个不祥之地，朋友们，不要走散了。
This is an ominous place. Let us keep together, friends.
Tristian：不要走得太远，这个地方可能很危险。
Don't go too far. This place can be dangerous.
Amiri：你们这些牧师就会唉声叹气，比这更糟糕的地方我都活着回来了！
More clerical whining. I've survived alone in worse places than this!
Ekun：说得有道理，团结就是力量。
Wise words. Unity feeds our strength.
Octavia：我没意见，我很喜欢大家一起坐在篝火边的感觉。每次有谁独自走远，都让我担心。
Fine by me, I love it when we sit together by the fire. And I always worry when one of us wanders off somewhere all by themself.
Jaethal：天呢，真是个懦夫……
By the gods, what a coward...
Kalikke/Kanerah：跟你待在一块可没有什么安全感，我得去那边躺得远远的。
I wouldn't say your company gives me any sense of safety. I think I'm going to lie down wa-a-ay over there.
LowHealth
Tristian：别那样看着我，我没事！
Don't look at me like that. — I'm fine!
Tristian：一切都好，我可以照顾好自己和大家。
Everything's fine. I can take care of myself, and the others.
Jubilost：又一个逞英雄的，明明都快死了。
Another hero who keeps claiming he's fine, even as he bleeds to death.
Amiri：我现在承认，你不怯懦。没错！那点小伤口根本不算事儿！
You're not a sissy, I give you that. And you're right! Those scratches are nothing!
Valerie：你不是总劝我要好好照顾自己吗？别固执了，崔斯提安，让我们检查一下你的伤势吧。
Aren't you the one who always says I should take better care of myself? Don't be so stubborn, Tristian, and let us check your wounds.
Regongar：你现在只要有人轻轻一推就会倒了。不过你没受伤的时候估计也是一样，哈哈！
You're going to fall over, if anyone so much as taps you on the shoulder. Though I guess that's true even on your good days, ha, ha!
LowRation
Tristian：我们的食物要吃完了。我很想去打猎，可惜我没有打猎的天赋……
We're running out of food. I'd go hunting myself, but I lack the talent for it…
Tristian：我们必须补充补给了，不过我可能无法胜任这个任务，我不擅长打猎。
We must replenish our stocks. I'm not sure I can do it, though. I'm not much of a hunter.
Amiri：你就不能叫约德教你几招鹿头神的打猎秘诀吗？看你们两个总粘在一起。
Couldn't you ask Jhod to teach you a thing or two about the Stag God's hunting tricks? You tagged along with him for long enough.
Ekun：如果你想学，说一声就好。每个人都应该学会打猎。
If you wish to learn, you need only ask. Everyone should know how to hunt.
Jubilost：我就知道我们应该多带点口粮的，以我们这个队伍的打猎水平，很快就要挨饿了。
I knew we should have taken more rations with us. With hunters like this we'll soon be starving.
Linzi：想起以前我得在街上和酒馆卖唱才能吃上饭，那还是我在大学院上学时的事了。其实我并不擅长卖唱，希望这次不会沦落到那个地步……
I remember how I used to sing for food on the streets, and in the pubs — back when I was at the Academy. Truth be told, I wasn't very good at it. I do hope it doesn't come to that...
NokNok：打猎很简单，我教你！首先我们需要一个诱饵，琳缀就很合适。
Hunting is simple. I teach you! But first we need bait for snare. Linzee will do.
LowSpell
Tristian：晨花，请不要无视我！我需要您的力量来救助受苦的人们。
Dawnflower, don't turn your gaze from me! I need your power to help those who suffer.
Tristian：我的祷告之火已经燃为灰烬，晨花，我恳求您，赐予我力量，让我为您效力。
The fire of my prayers has fallen to embers. I address my plea to you, Dawnflower — grant me the strength to serve you.
Ekun：我喜欢听你祈祷，你的声音很纯净，你的灵魂也是。
I like to listen when you pray. A pure voice, and a pure soul.
Amiri：你又把祈祷浪费光了？很好，现在你得学会怎么用武器了。
You wasted all your prayers again? Good. Now you'll need to learn how to use weapons properly.
Valerie：你的祷词总是关于侍奉神明、帮助他人。这……让我想到很多事，我不禁思考，真诚和虚荣的界限在哪里。
All your prayers are always about serving, and helping others. This… brings to my mind many things. I think upon those things that separate sincerity and vanity.
Regongar：噢，女神啊，请赐予我力量……因为我自己一点力量都没有，哈哈哈！
Oh goddess, give me your strength... Because I have none of my own, ha, ha, ha!
NoBuilding
Tristian：这样毫无遮蔽地扎营是非常危险的。
It's unsafe to set up camp in the open like this.
Tristian：我们这样不谨慎，可能要付出惨痛的代价。
Being this careless could cost us dearly.
Amiri：别唉声叹气了，牧师。要是有人袭击，我来保护你！
Stop your whining, cleric. If someone jumps us, I'll save your hide!
Harrim：每个疏忽大意的举动，都会离格罗图斯更接近一步。你应该也清楚，无论怎样延缓终局，每个人都终将归于他的怀抱。
Each careless step taken is one step closer to Groetus. Surely you understand that everyone shall rest in his embrace in the end, no matter how carefully they try to delay the final moment?
Valerie：对啊，隐藏营地的技术已经发展了几百年，不应该忽视它！
You are right. The art of camouflage has been developed over many centuries. It's unwise to neglect it!
Regongar：听你这抱怨的口气，搞得好像是你负责砍杀袭击的怪物似的，哈哈哈。
You complain as though you'll be the one slicing up the monsters if they attack us, ha, ha, ha.
NoHunting
Tristian：如果今晚不去打猎的话，至少附近的兔子得以安生了，我们的口粮也还有剩余。
At least the local rabbits will rest easy, if we don't go hunting tonight. And we still have some rations.
Tristian：今晚没人去打猎吗？唔，至少对兔子和鹿来说是个好消息，它们可以见到下一个黎明了。
So no one's going to hunt tonight? Well, at least that's good for some rabbit or deer, who will live to see another dawn.
Jubilost：我也很同情兔子，但我还是希望能有那么一两只跳进我们的锅里来，鲜肉才是最健康的。
I pity the rabbits, of course, but I'd prefer one or two hop into our stew pot. A fresh meal is always healthier.
Amiri：得了吧，牧师。打猎多有趣啊，肉也很好吃。可你心里只有兔子，宁愿啃口粮！
Damn it, cleric. Hunting is fun and meat is tasty. But you're just a rabbit lover who likes chewing ration packs!
Octavia：兔子好可爱！可是也好好吃！我也拿不定哪边更重要……
Rabbits are so cute! But so tasty too! — I can't tell which one's more important.
Jaethal：哈，你把动物看得比自己还重要，你简直就像我一样不在乎自己……
Ha. You put an animal higher than yourself. You think almost as little of yourself as I do...
Kalikke/Kanerah：你还真关心那些无力自保的生命！可惜你那些同教兄弟没有给予我同样的仁慈。
You care so much for those who are unable to protect themselves! It's a shame your brothers in faith didn't show me the same mercy.
Valerie：我们这群人真是奇怪的组合。一点也不像是一支英雄的团队，更像是一群流浪汉聚在一起。
We're a strange group. More like a handful of outcasts than an army of heroes.
Valerie：不过话说回来……传说中第一名阿尔多瑞剑爵本身也是一个放逐者。我们哪点比不上他吗？
Anyway... They say even the first Aldori Swordlord was an outcast. Are we any worse than him?
Valerie：我旅行的经历越多，我越发确定一件事：真正的贵族精神绝不是继承而来的。
The more I travel, the more I'm sure: true nobleness is not inherited.
Valerie：那些头顶着侯爵头衔的家伙都是自命不凡的笨蛋，他们的修养连自己的马都比不上。
Every other smug dimwit with a marquis title has worse manners than his horse.
Valerie：盔甲一定要坚固而厚重，越重越好。
Armor must be sturdy and heavy. The heavier, the better.
Valerie：要是有一面上乘的盾牌就更好了……那样你就会感觉自己不再是一名战士，而是一座堡垒。
And if there's a good shield to add to the armor – then you feel like a whole fortress, not just a fighter.
Kalikke&Kanerah
Valerie：我的剑上又被磕出了一个缺口。我的磨刀石在哪里……
There is a nick in my sword again. Where's my whetstone...
Kalikke/Kanerah：不不，拜托别再发出那个声音了！看着我的眼睛，高贵的瓦尔瑞。你是打算用你的‘唰！唰！唰！’谋杀你的同伴吗？
Nooo, not that sound again, please! Look me in the eyes, noble Valerie. Are you prepared to murder your companion with your 'Shink! Shink! Shink!'?
Valerie：为什么每一名看到我的贵族都想要为我吟唱一首蹩脚的颂歌呢？为什么不送给我一点实用的东西，比如说一双靴子呢？
Why does every noble who so much as glances at me deem it necessary to compose embarrassing odes to me? Why not give me a new pair of boots, for instance?
Kanerah：你显然指的是更私人的事情，不过我举双手赞成！某人可以接受欢乐的穷诗人的颂歌，但一个贵族理应送出贵重的礼物，否则某人会觉得他们是吝啬鬼。
You obviously mean something more personal, but I do agree! One can accept odes from a poor joyful bard, but a noble should give precious gifts, or one doubts their generosity.
Valerie：安米芮，你为什么不穿一身像样的盔甲呢？你全身都是伤疤！
Amiri, why don't you wear serious armor? Your entire body is covered in scars!
Amiri：那又怎样？我是一个野蛮人，又不是个戎装公主。我们这种人就是这样，伤疤越多，越显荣耀！
So what? I'm a barbarian, not some princess. It's like that with us: the more scars, the better!
Valerie：你的大剑真是壮观啊。我从来没有想到过还有人可以使用这么大的一把刀剑。
Your sword is so amazing. I could never have imagined someone wielding such a huge blade.
Amiri：没错！这是一件战利品，是我从一个霜巨人手中夺过来的！我用这把剑杀掉了好多巨人！现在他们一听到我的名字，就会吓得尿裤子！
Right! I took it from a frost giant – as a trophy! I slaughtered so many of them giants with it! Now when they hear my name, they shit their pants!
Valerie：你想念你的部落吗？你有没有想过要回到你的故乡去？
Do you miss your tribe? Don't you ever want to go back to your homeland?
Amiri：为什么要回去？回去被冻成冰雕吗？去和那里的牲畜为伍？算了吧，你们现在才是我的部落。
Why the hell? To freeze my ass off on a glacier? Look at their pig snouts? No, you are my tribe now.
Valerie：安米芮，你只穿这些兽皮，不会觉得冷吗？这些衣服什么都盖不住啊。
Amiri, aren't you cold just wearing those hides? They cover almost nothing.
Amiri：哈，这里冷吗？在这里裸奔露胸都不会冷。在我的故乡，那里才叫真的冷！吐口痰还没落地就冻成冰棍了！
Ha, is it ever cold here? You could walk around naked bouncing your boobs. In my homeland – that's where it's cold! Spit freezes before it hits the ground!
Valerie：你总是身先士卒冲入敌阵，就好像这支队伍里除了你没有别的战士了。
You're always the first to get into fights – as if there are no fighters in the party except you.
Amiri：怎么，不行吗？我知道我有这么多好队友。不过这是我们那里的习俗，最强大的战士永远冲在最前面！
Why not? I know there are others. But it's the custom – the best fighter is always in the front row!
Valerie：你的剑一定很重吧。你要不要试一试用一把小一点的剑，但是多穿一些像样的盔甲？
Your sword is so heavy. Maybe you should carry a smaller weapon and wear more serious armor?
Amiri：这话说的！我为什么要穿盔甲？我的敌人连举起手来的机会都没有就被我砍倒了！但要是我用的是一根小牙签，那要做到这点就不太容易了！
Yeah right! Why do I need armor? My enemies can barely raise their hands before they fall on the ground! That wouldn't be so easy if I was swinging a toothpick!
Valerie：我在你的言行之中感觉到了高贵感。我觉得你一定出身不凡。
I feel nobleness in your words and deeds. I do not think you are from a common family.
Ekun：血统有什么意义吗？是我自己把我变成现在的样子。
Does lineage mean anything? I'm the one who made myself.
Kalikke：你错了。我来自一个非常普通的家庭，而且就连他们也排斥我。
You are wrong. I am from a very common family, and even they rejected me.
Kanerah：你错了。我来自一个非常普通的家庭。我历经了千辛万苦，才从一个游牧民，成了一名统治者身边令人敬重的同伴。
You are wrong, I'm from a very common family. I've worked hard and come a long way — from a wandering nomad to a ruler's respectable companion.
Valerie：你不太爱聊自己的事情。你是从哪里来的啊？
You don't talk about yourself too much. Where are you from?
Ekun：我出生在苏维亚，但是在安布萨朗长大。那是一座汇集了几百个种族、几百种语言、几百种信仰的城市……不过那里对我来说太过于明亮、太过于嘈杂了。
Born in Thuvia, but raised in Absalom. A city of hundreds of peoples, hundreds of languages, hundreds of faiths... But too bright and noisy for me.
Valerie：你在旅程中一定见识过不少东西。能不能分享一些有价值的建议呢？
You must have seen much in your journeys. Do you have any useful advice to share?
Ekun：我的建议就是尽量不要旅行。建立一个家，然后体面的生活。
My advice is to avoid traveling. Find your home and live with dignity.
Valerie：我一直很喜欢狼。它们都是非常高贵的捕食者！
I've always liked wolves. They are noble predators!
Ekun：高贵这种概念都是人发明的。动物都是大自然的一部分。它们只是按照自己的天性生活。
Nobleness was invented by people. Animals are a part of nature. They simply live as they do.
Valerie：艾昆，我们一定可以找到那些巨魔凶手。决不能让这些怪物逃脱公正的复仇！
Ekun, we will definitely track down those killer trolls. No monster can escape fair revenge!
Ekun：复仇是公正的行为吗？还是只是一种残忍？我不知道。甚至也不再在意了。
Is revenge fair? Or merely cruel? I do not know. Nor do I even care anymore.
Valerie：你为什么看着我做针线活？你觉得这样的消遣活动会消磨我作为战士的斗志吗？
Why are you watching me stitch? Do you think such a pastime unbecoming for a warrior?
Ekun：你缝缝补补的样子就像是在战斗一样。稳定的双手，利落的缝线。看起来很享受。
You stitch like you fight. A steady hand. A neat seam. It's good to watch.
Valerie：你的狼还挺听话的。你是如何把它训练得这么好？
Your wolf is quite obedient. How did you manage to train him so well?
Ekun：我没有训练它。它是有智慧的，而不仅仅是服从。
I didn't. It's intelligence, not obedience.
Valerie：你是个技巧高超的战士，不过有的时候你似乎会忘记团队，单枪匹马去挑战所有人。
You're a skilled warrior, but sometimes you seem to forget about the team and fight like you're alone against everybody.
Ekun：你说的没错。但是本性难改，我一辈子都是单枪匹马的。
It's all true. Old habits don't easily die. I've fought alone all my life.
Valerie：我很喜欢看着你的狼玩闹。那只动物表现出无比的高贵和尊严。
I love to watch your wolf playing. That animal is full of dignity and nobility.
Ekun：它只是只动物而已。动物只懂得对他的族群保持忠诚。其他的特质都只是人们一厢情愿的虚构。
It's just an animal. All an animal knows is loyalty to its pack. The rest is just human invention.
Valerie：哈瑞姆，我知道你想对我说什么。想都别想。我绝对不会信奉你的格罗图斯，或其他任何神祇！
Harrim, I know what you wanted to tell me. Don't even try. I'm not going to bow to your Groetus – or to anyone else!
Harrim：但是你错了。当整个世界都迎来终结之时，每个人都需要一个能在心中呼唤的名字！
But you are wrong. Everyone needs a name to call on in the hour when all things come to an end!
Valerie：你的信仰真是疯狂。你怎么能向一个要把所有人都杀掉的神灵祈祷呢？
Your religion is insane. How could you pray to someone who wants to kill us all?
Harrim：格罗图斯并不想杀死任何人。这个可悲的世界终有一天会迎来末日，不论是以什么方式。我的神祇只是耐心地等待着这一天的到来而已。他的教条就是耐心，并且在无可避免的命运面前保持谦卑……
Groetus does not want anything. This pathetic world will come to an end – one way or another. My god just patiently waits for that day. His path is patience, and humility in the face of the inevitable...
Valerie：你听到的那些声音……很抱歉这么问，但你真的确定那是你的神祇发出的吗？你是不是也应该考虑一下自己的健康原因？
Those voices you hear... I'm sorry, but are you sure it's really your god? Maybe you should think about your health?
Harrim：是的，我确实听到了格罗图斯的声音，不过我却不是总能理解他的意思，或者他呼唤我去的地方……不过这是疯狂的表现吗？捂住耳朵，假装没有听到命运的真相，这才是疯狂吧？
Yes, I hear the voice of Groetus, although I don't always understand what he says, or where he calls me... But is it insanity? Isn't it more insane to cover your ears and deny the truth about what's coming?
Valerie：矮人先生，我发现我们之间还是有很多相同之处的。在过去，我们都侍奉过不值得效忠的神祇。
I suppose we have something in common, sir dwarf. In the past we both served deities unworthy of our loyalty.
Harrim：但差别在于你再也无法找到一个新的信仰，和一个活下去的理由。而我有。
The difference is you never found a new faith or a new reason to live. I did.
Valerie：耶萨尔，你曾经在故乡担任过许多年法官。我很不明白，是什么让你背弃法律的？
Jaethal, you were a judge for many years in your homeland. I don't understand – what made you turn against the law?
Jaethal：法律都是由那些当权者书写的，其目的并不是为了限制他们自己，而是为了统治那些没有权力的人。所有遵守法律的人都不值得我去尊敬，他们都是忠于自己的镣铐的奴隶。
Laws are written by those in power – and not to constrain themselves, but to dominate the powerless. He who respects the law, is worthy of contempt, like a slave who honors his shackles.
Valerie：担任乌伽娑婀的审判官是一种什么样的体验？我本以为寿命长达数个世纪的精灵们会珍惜生命，而不是唾弃生命。
What is it like, being Urgathoa's inquisitor? I thought elves who've lived for centuries would value life, not despise it.
Jaethal：哦，相信我，瓦尔瑞，没有比长命的种族更珍惜生命的了。当然，他们珍惜的是自己的生命。
Oh, believe me, Valerie, no one values life more than the long-lived. Their own, that is.
Valerie：所以你永远不会衰老了吗，耶萨尔？就像被包裹在琥珀里的一只飞虫？不会长皱纹，不会留下伤痕，就连你的头发也不会再长长了……这可真是奇怪……
So you never change at all, Jaethal? You're like a fly stuck in amber? No wrinkles, no scars, even your hair is always the same length... That's so strange...
Jaethal：不过你会变的，瓦尔瑞。你的美丽每一天都在流逝。你难道不想永远留住它吗？我可以帮你……
But you change, Valerie. Your beauty fades by the day. Don't you want to hold on to it forever? I could help...
Valerie：朱比罗斯特，你为什么永远那么傲慢？我们的队伍已经有一个领袖，不是你。
Jubilost, why are you so arrogant? Our party has a commander, and it's not you.
Jubilost：因为我的头脑里储藏的知识比你们所有人加在一起都多。当然了，我会服从{Name}的命令……不过如果我对我们应该怎么做有什么想法的话，我是一定要表达出来的！
Because there's more knowledge in my head than in all of yours combined. Of course, I do as {Name} says... But don't expect me to keep my opinions to myself about what we should do!
Valerie：我并不否认你的优点，不过你确实太傲慢了。不论你写过多少本书，你都没有资格看低其他人。
I don't detract from your merits, but you are too arrogant. No matter how many books you've written, it doesn't give you the right to look down on others.
Jubilost：也许你还没有发现，我是一名侏儒。如果我要看低其他人，除非用魔法把自己先变大！
I'm a gnome, if you haven't noticed. I'd need a ladder to look down on anyone at all!
Valerie：我越来越受不了你时不时的讽刺了。总有一天我的耐心会被你耗光，到那时你就会后悔的。
I'm starting to grow tired of your constant sarcasm. One day my patience will run out, and then you'll regret it.
Jubilost：哦，这是什么意思？威胁我？看来你对我曾经受到过多少威胁一无所知！不过威胁对我来说没有用的。真正应该惧怕的是那些不会威胁的人，他们会直接动手。
Oh, what do we have here? Threats? If you only knew how many of those I've heard! But they're worth nothing. The people you really need to be afraid of don't make threats – they act.
Valerie：你的语气总是招惹所有人。要克制你那尖锐的言论真的就有那么难吗？
Your tone gets on everyone's nerves. Isn't it difficult to keep your acid-tongued remarks to yourself?
Jubilost：这叫坦诚。如果我不喜欢一个东西，我会毫无保留地说出来。这可比趋炎附势的赔笑和假装歌舞升平、背地里却两面三刀要好多了。
It's called honesty. When I don't like something, I'm not ashamed of saying it. It's much better than spreading fake smiles and pretending everything's fine, while secretly harboring nasty thoughts.
Valerie：你怎么会如此傲慢呢？你是个见多识广的人，社交礼节这种起码的概念对你来说有那么难理解吗？
How can you be so arrogant? Could the simple science of common decency be too complicated for such a learned man?
Jubilost：我并不傲慢！正相反，我对每个人都敞开心扉。我只是觉得假装和善没有任何意义罢了。不过，从一个贵族出生的人嘴里听到傲慢这个词真的很不容易。你们从很小的时候起就被教导要趾高气扬的生活，以及像躲避昂贵服饰上的油渍一样对真相视而不见！
I'm not arrogant at all! On the contrary, I'm open to everyone. I just don't see the point in faking amiability. But it's rich to hear about arrogance from the noble born. You were taught from a young age to stick up their nose and avoid the truth like some oily mess that might smear your doublet!
Valerie：琳翠，你这么勤奋在写什么呢？而且你为什么用这种眼神看着我？你是不是决定也要为我写一篇赞歌？
Linzi, what are you writing so diligently there – and why are you looking at me like that? Did you decide to write me an ode too?
Linzi：你说的没错！听我给你念一念：‘曾有一名雪琳的武士，她的英勇永不……’嘿，嘿，那是我的墨水瓶！别砸别砸！
You got it! Here it is, listen: 'There once lived a paladin of Shelyn, whose courage was so unfailin'...' Hey, hey, that's my ink-pot! Don't throw it at me!
Valerie：我很看重实用的书，但是付钱的故事有什么用？如果你在真实生活中遇到一头巨龙，那么屠龙骑士的传说也帮不上忙。
I respect useful books, but who needs shallow stories? Fables about a knight who slew a dragon won't help you, if you meet a dragon in real life.
Linzi：那么他们就可以写一本有关我的书！《屠龙勇士琳翠》！不不不，更像是《不惧挑战巨龙、却在风华正茂的年纪被英勇吞食的琳翠》！听起来还不错，不是吗？
Then they'll write books about me! 'Linzi the Dragon Slayer'! Or no, more like, 'Linzi, Who Was Not Afraid To Challenge a Dragon, And Got Heroically Eaten In Her Prime!' Sounds good, huh?
Valerie：你为什么要离开家？有哪个旅行的吟游诗人最终过上幸福的生活吗？还是说你希望靠在酒馆里唱歌的方式扬名立万？
Why did you leave your home? Do any traveling bards ever live happily ever after? Or did you hope to earn your glory, singing songs in taverns?
Linzi：嘿，你可以不重视自己的快乐！你从小就被包围在书本、绘画、诗词和音乐之中……但是在我的村子里，唯一的娱乐就是酒馆里的斗殴和丰收季节的舞会！要是我还留在那里，我一定会被闷死的！
Heh, you don't appreciate your happiness! You've been surrounded by books, paintings, poets and singers since you were a child... In our village the only entertainments we had were tavern fights and harvest dance parties! I'd have died of boredom if I'd stayed there!
Valerie：奥克塔维雅，你在想什么？你的表情说明你想玩什么花招。
Octavia, what are you thinking about? Your face says you're plotting some trick.
Octavia：我在想，如果我往老瑞的靴子里放进去一只鹿甲虫，那么在他发现之前我有没有机会可以跑到失窃之地的边界去！
I'm wondering whether I'll have time to make it to the border of the Stolen Lands, before Reg notices that I put a stag-beetle in his boot!
Valerie：我很欣赏你的乐观精神。即便局势十分严峻，你的脸上却始终保持笑容！
I admire your optimism. Even when the situation seems desperate, your smile never leaves your face!
Octavia：你知不知道我和老瑞在纽玫瑞亚经历过多少严峻的局面？不过我们都没死，我们都撑过来了！不管以后还有什么磨难，我们都能应付的。
Do you know how many desperate situations Reg and I survived in Numeria? And we're all right – we got out of all of them! We'll handle whatever comes.
Valerie：当我还是个孩子的时候，我非常喜欢那些漂亮的裙子、蝴蝶结、和蕾丝。现在想来真是奇怪……
It's strange to remember how, as a child, I enjoyed all the bright dresses, bows and ruffles...
Octavia：我也很喜欢啊！只可惜我没有那个机会可以打扮。
And I like them all so much! Too bad I don't often get a chance to dress up.
Kalikke：即使到了现在，我也喜欢这些。那有什么错呢？如果鸟儿决定使用利爪，它的鲜艳羽毛并不会妨碍它们。
I still love them, even now. What's wrong with that? A bird's bright feathers don't interfere with its talons, if it decides to use them.
Kanerah：即使到了现在，我也喜欢这些。那有什么错呢？如果鸟儿决定使用利爪，它的鲜艳羽毛并不会妨碍它们。
I still love them, even now. What's wrong with that? A bird's bright feathers don't interfere with its talons, if it decides to use them.
Valerie：奥克塔维雅，你觉得我要不要把自己的头发再剪短一点呢？
Octavia, do you think I should cut my hair even shorter?
Octavia：我觉得你留任何发型都会很美的，即便你剃光头也一样！要不然你试一试？
I think you'll look great with any haircut. Even if you shaved your head bald! Maybe we should try that!
Valerie：瑞贡尕，你的行为总是像一只动物。除了吃、喝、战斗之外，你的脑子还能想到其他的事情吗？
Regongar, you act like an animal. Are you capable of thinking about anything besides food, drinking and fighting?
Regongar：当然啦！我还会想到俊俏的小姑娘们，还有小伙子们……特别是在畅快地大喝一顿或者大打一场之后！
Sure! I think about pretty lasses, and lads... Especially after a good drink or a good fight!
Valerie：我发誓，瑞贡尕，若不是我们有共同的事业，我一定会向你挑战，然后让你收起脸上那自以为是的欠揍表情！
I swear, Regongar, if it weren't for our common cause, I'd challenge you to a duel and wipe that smug sneer off your face!
Regongar：为什么呢，贵族大人，您怎么能向一名出身低贱的农民挑战呢！我是绝对不可能会冒犯到您的！不，应该是您要狠狠的惩罚我！用鞭子抽打我！我现在就想把裤子脱了！
Why, Your Nobleness, you mustn't challenge the peasantry to duels! How unseemly it would be if they spanked you! No, you rather must spank them! Spank them with a whip! I'm taking off my pants right now!
Valerie：我真不明白奥克塔维雅怎么会喜欢上你的。她是个善良而亲切的女孩，你却是个粗俗而野蛮的乡巴佬，成天痴迷于杀戮。
I don't understand what Octavia sees in you. She's a good and kind girl, and you're a rude and uncouth bumpkin, desperate and obsessed with killing.
Regongar：你难道没听过这话吗？男的不坏女的不爱。因为坏男人懂得怎么让好女人舒服。我可以让你感受感受。晚上到我的帐篷来吧。
Don't you know? Good girls like bad boys. It's because bad boys know how to make girls feel good. I could show you. Come to my tent tonight.
Valerie：瑞贡尕，你完全印证了外面那些对你的族人的负面评论。
Regongar, you stand as proof of all the worst rumors about your people.
Regongar：你是希望我可以证明那些传言都是错误的吗？哈！我就是喜欢满足人们的想象。
And you'd rather I disprove them? Ha! I just give the people what they want.
Valerie：如此强大的魔法力量，却赐给了在其他所有方面都如此低劣的一个人，真是令人惊奇。
It's amazing how such a powerful magic gift could be bestowed upon one so lowly in all other respects.
Regongar：嫉妒吗？想要体会一下我的经历吗？好啊！别忘了，这份魔法力量可是从多年的奴隶生活、毒打和羞辱中得来的！
Jealous? Want to trade fates with me? Fine! Just don't forget that the magic gift comes with years of slavery, beatings and humiliation!
Valerie：崔斯提安爵士……听起来像是英雄故事里的骑士！
Sir Tristian... Sounds like the name of a knight in a ballad!
Tristian：谢谢你，瓦尔瑞，不过我可不是什么骑士。我不喜欢战斗，更不会以战斗为乐。
Thank you, Valerie, but I could hardly be a knight. I don't like fighting, and even less do I like fighting for sport.
Valerie：你如此虔诚地向你的女神祈祷……不过你却从未像其他祭司一样谴责我背弃了我的信仰。谢谢你。
You pray to your goddess with such devotion… But you never accused me of apostasy, like other priests. I appreciate that.
Tristian：这没什么好责怪的。你选择离开，总比留在那里欺骗自己和你的神祇要好。
There's nothing to be ashamed of. It's better you leave, than to stay and lie to yourself and your deity.
Valerie：我的胳膊有点疼……
My elbow hurts...
Tristian：那是因为你对自己太严格了。偶尔也休息一下吧。你用不着一有空闲就努力练习。
It's because you're so hard on yourself. Try to rest sometimes. You don't have to practice every spare minute.
Valerie：你是个很亲切的人，崔斯提安。你每天都面对着周围发生的各种暴行，这对你来说一定很艰难。
You're a kind person, Tristian. It must be hard for you to witness all the atrocities that go on around here.
Tristian：我的女神会赐予我力量。如果我觉得悲伤，我会向莎恩芮祈祷，然后她就会宽慰我。
I draw strength from my goddess. If I begin to feel sad, I turn to Sarenrae in my prayers, and she gives me solace.
Valerie：我尊敬你的信仰，不过我并不赞同。罪行就是罪行，是无法用忏悔来赎罪的。每个人都必须要为自己的罪恶付出代价。
I respect your faith, but I can't agree with it. An atrocity is an atrocity, and it cannot be fixed with repentance. Everyone must pay for their misdeeds.
Tristian：当然，这是必须的。一个真正忏悔的人必须要用尽一切力量来弥补他所造成的伤害。这是唯一的出路。
Of course they must. He who truly repents will do everything in their power to repair the harm he's done. – That is his only path.
生日祝福
Valerie：今天是我们女爵/男爵/女王/国王的华诞，我有句话要说！对于所有追随您的那些部下——还有以后加入我们队伍的人来说，今天是个特殊的日子。
It's our {baroness's/baron's/Queen's/King's} birthday, and I have a word to say! This date is special for all of those who follow you today – and to the many others who will join you in the years to come.
Valerie：您是我们的首领，是决定这片土地、还有人民的命运的大人物。这是一项崇高的荣耀，也是一个重大的责任。愿您依旧强壮和可敬，带领我们前进！
You are our leader, and the one who will decide the fate of this land – and its people. It's a great honor, and a great responsibility. Remain strong and worthy, and lead us forward!
C11_Bandit
Valerie：如此可悲的荒原。缺少法纪总会导致荒凉和空虚。
Such sad, deserted lands. Lawlessness always leads to desolation and emptiness.
Linzi：我不知道，高特已经不再那样井井有序了——不过也远没到荒凉的地步。
I wouldn't know. Galt is no more orderly — but it's nowhere near as desolate.
Octavia：相信我，法律和秩序可以被人为扭曲，用来为邪恶服务，带来更多的残酷和痛苦。
Trust me, law and order can be twisted into the service of evil, and spawn even more cruelty and suffering.
Tristian：法律和自由就像天平的两端一样彼此平衡，两者都可以导致邪恶，只有慈悲才能缓和它们。
Law and freedom balance each other like two ends of the scales. Both can lead to evil, unless tempered with compassion.
C11_SycamoreWar
Valerie：难以置信！狗头人和螨怪之间的战争！连书里都不敢这么写！
Unbelievable! A war between kobolds and mites! You can't read about that in a book!
Linzi：哈，我猜你是书读得少！有一本卡蒂拉小说……不过我得说，总体来看有点轻浮……就写道，狗头人和螨怪不仅会战斗，还会，唔，做一些其它的事情。
Ha, I guess you haven't spent much time in libraries! There is one Qadiran novel... on the whole rather frivolous, I must say... where kobolds and mites not only fight, but, hm, do a few other things as well.
Octavia：看到他们百年的和平就因为这种琐事而打破，真是难过！但我们也做不了什么：毕竟大自然没有赐给他们双方多少智慧……
It's sad to see their hundred-year peace broken over such a trifle! But there's nothing to be done: nature didn't bless either of them with much wit...
Regongar：他们开始互相撕扯对方的喉咙时，场面简直就像马戏团一样棒，哈哈哼！体格小小，却储满了啐沫和怒火！
It was as good as a circus, when they started tearing out each other's throats, ha, ha, hmm! So small, but full of spit and rage!
Lebeda
Valerie：勒贝达爵士对一个高尚的骑士该拥有怎样的美德完全一窍不通。他不断地邀请我去约会——还对我的拒绝充耳不闻！
The virtues of a noble chevalier are entirely foreign to Sir Lebeda. He kept inviting me on a date so persistently — and behaved as though he was deaf to my objections!
Amiri：你就跟他说，你会在一个僻静的地方见他……然后你不就用去了，我去，我会让他好好尝尝甜头！
Just say that you will meet him in some quiet place... and I'll show up instead of you. I will give him more than he ever bargained for!
Harrim：我会和他谈谈。谈谈虚无，还有一切存在之物的灰烬与终焉；谈谈生命的无意义。这样，他就会明白打扰别人的生活是多么加倍地无意义了。
I should talk to him. About the void, and the ashes and the end of all that exists. About the pointlessness of life. And he will see that spoiling the lives of others is doubly pointless.
Regongar：哈哈。有些人觉得接受失败、急流勇退是件难事……哪怕周围的每个人都能看出，败局已定。
Ha, ha. Some find it difficult to own defeat and stand back... Even when everyone around can see it's a lost cause.
C21_Start
Valerie：我不知道这是不是真的，不过……你有没有听当地村民说过，树林里有一条龙？它会成为很不错的狩猎战利品！
I don't know if it's true, but... have you heard what the local villagers say about a dragon in these woods? It would make a fine hunting trophy!
Ekun：不，不会是龙，这片土地上没有龙。有可能是条双足飞龙吧。
No, not dragon. There are no dragons in these lands. Could be a wyvern.
Jubilost：如果闲言碎语也能当作战利品的话，它倒确实是件不错的战利品。要是这附近真的住着龙的话，人人都会知道的——以一种最不愉快的方式！
It would surely be a fine trophy indeed, if trophies could be forged of empty gossip. If a real dragon lived anywhere nearby, everyone would learn of it — in a most unpleasant manner!
Amiri：想到这一点，我就觉得自己已经准备好作战了！我们为什么还在这里叽叽喳喳呢？！是时候去扯掉那只蜥蜴的翅膀了！
Just thinking about it, I'm ready to fight! Why are we babbling here?! It's time we go and tear off that lizard's wings!
Kalikke/Kanerah：我希望这只是闲聊。龙可不是供人打猎取乐的对象。它可能意味着这片封地的灾难！
I hope it's just idle talk. A dragon is not prey for a joyful hunt. It would mean disaster for the entire barony!
C21_TartukAndTrolls
Valerie：一般来说，巨魔对待狗头人就像对待讨厌的飞蛾一样……我在想，那个塔尔图齐奥到底对他们说了什么？
Trolls usually treat kobolds like annoying moths... I wonder what that Tartuccio could have told them?
Jubilost：结果到头来，他是个魔法师。魔法诡计可以在无知的野蛮人心中留下永远难忘的印象。
In the end, he's a magician. Magic trickery can make an unforgettable impression on the uneducated barbarian mind.
Ekun：不管那是什么，像这样的联盟对其他所有人来说都是个坏消息。
Whatever it is, an alliance like this is bad news for everyone else.
Linzi：也许他们只是喜欢紫色？或者，可能他能给他们真正渴望的东西？再或者，也许他是第一个聪明到能学会巨人语的狗头人！
Maybe they just like the color purple? Or was he able to offer them something they truly desire? Or perhaps he's the first kobold clever enough to learn the language of the giants!
C22_MivonVsBrevoy
Valerie：我遇到过很多人，也曾与他们中的许多人并肩战斗。失窃之地上最好的盟友是坚定不移的米翁人，他们永远可靠——不像布雷沃人。
I've met a lot of people. Fought side by side with many of them. The best allies in the Stolen Lands are the steadfast Mivoni. They're always reliable — unlike Brevans.
Jubilost：布雷沃的家族长于阴谋和叛变。看来，哪怕是那些平民也和他们的统治者一样，有着不老实的本能。
Brevan families specialize in scheming and treachery. It would seem even commoners of those parts possess the same dishonest instincts as their rulers.
Amiri：南方人都是一样的——既无尊严也无勇气，懦夫。
Southerners are all the same. — Cowards with no pride or courage.
Jaethal：只有一个人的话是你可以永远相信的：那就是你自己。
There's only one whose word you can always trust: yourself.
Kalikke/Kanerah：换句话说，比起胸前戳着把剑，你更喜欢被背后捅刀子。
In other words, you'd prefer a sword in the chest to a dagger in the back.
C22_MonstersOutOfPeople
Valerie：怎么可能呢？怪物从人的身体里面挣脱出来，撕碎了一个可怜的灵魂！我们杀死的每只怪物都意味着一条我们没能拯救的生命……
How could it be possible? Monsters appearing from inside a person, tearing the poor soul apart! Every monster we slay is one more life we failed to save...
Jubilost：欢迎来到现实。这就是它的本来面貌，不同于书里的英雄传说，也不同于吟游诗人的歌词。
Welcome to real life. This is how it really looks on the ground, unlike the pages of heroic legends, or the songs of bards.
Octavia：那些明白即将发生什么事的穷人的眼睛——我的噩梦中一直萦绕着这样的眼神。我们必须想办法让阻止这种疯狂！
The eyes of those poor people who recognized what was about to happen — they shall haunt me in my nightmares. We must find a way to stop this madness!
Tristian：这场可怕的悲剧只会释放我们每天都在竭力忘却的事情，我们浪费的每一分每一秒中，都有人失去生命。每一刻，都有人向路人伸出手，乞求救赎……却死于冷血无情。
This terrible tragedy only brings into relief something we try to forget every day. Every wasted hour costs someone their life. Every moment someone reaches out to a passers-by, begging for salvation... and dying of unkindness.
C31_DeadDefacedSister
Valerie：那个我们在独眼巨人墓里遇到的女人的面容总是在我眼前浮现。真的是‘无面’姐妹啊！到底是谁对她做出了那种事？
I can't stop thinking about the face of that poor woman we found in the cyclops' tomb. A 'Defaced' Sister indeed! Who could have done that to her?
Jubilost：一些狂热教团过激的宗教行为吧。有许多黑暗宗教的祭司自愿自残。
A perverse religious practice of some fanatic cult. Priests of many dark religions self-mutilate voluntarily.
Amiri：不过，反正现在都不重要了。要躺进坟墓里，你也不是非得有张脸不可。
It doesn't matter much now, anyway. You don't need a face to lay in a grave.
Harrim：世界上充满了这样的恐怖，生活是如此残酷。而比这更令人惊讶的，是你还这样紧抓着它不放。
The world is full of such terrors. Life is cruel. What is more surprising is the way you cling to it.
Kalikke/Kanerah：不知怎么的，我觉得她们真的是姐妹……来自一个非常忧郁的家庭。
For some reason I feel they really are sisters... From a very unhappy family.
C31_SeasonOfBloomFeedback
Valerie：起初我以为，开花不过是又一个自然奇观，带来邪恶并非它的本意——就像有毒的植物，或者干旱和风暴一样。但现在我们知道了，这一切的根源就是那个邪恶的意志！
At first I thought the Bloom just another wonder of nature, which causes evil unwillingly — like poisonous plants, or droughts and storms. But now we know there's a wicked will at the root of all this!
Jubilost：正如所料，大灾难的背后往往藏着某个邪恶的意志。
As should have been expected. Behind great disasters there's often someone's wicked will.
Ekun：强大的邪恶，无辜的受害者。这些永远也不能原谅，不能遗忘。
Great evil. Innocent victims. This must never be forgiven and forgotten.
Linzi：多可怕啊，怎么会有人干得出这种事？我永远也理解不了是什么驱使着这些怪物。它们的头脑和灵魂怎么会变得如此扭曲？
How horrible. How could anyone be capable of such things? I will never understand what drives such monsters. How can their minds and souls become so twisted?
C41
Valerie：正如所料，凯利德人对战术和技巧一无所知。不过，不能否认的是，他们相当……足智多谋，富有新意。
As expected, the Kellids know nothing about strategy and maneuvers. However, one can't deny they possess a certain... resourcefulness and tactical ingenuity.
Jaethal：不过是些动物本能的诡计，仅此而已。
Animal craftiness, not more than that.
Amiri：别上当了，他们就指望着对手把他们当成白痴呢。小瞧他们这个错误你只能犯一次。
Don't be fooled. They are counting on their opponent taking them for idiots. Underestimating them is a mistake you only do once.
Regongar：都靠分析决议和随机应变，在这里你可以向他们好好学学，哈哈。
It's all about resolution and improvisation. You've got something to learn from them here, ha ha.
C41_BeforeTristianReturn

I never expected this from Tristian... but now he's shown his true colors.

I'd hunt down the rat and choke him, but I don't want to get my hands dirty.

But how did he come to this?! Why did he betray us? I had no idea! Aren't you the least bit interested?
Jubilost：啊，我想到了！我总在想，而且也常说，他有哪里不对——看来我是对的！
Well I had an idea! I always thought, and often said, that there was something wrong with him — and I was right!

Ha! How nicely put! Where's my notebook...

Why not? We've already tried putting someone on a throne. Now let's try throwing someone off one, ha ha ha!

He who searches for fairness in this world only walks the path of disappointment.

Ilora Nuski's camp was more a thieves' nest than a general's headquarters. They stood no chance.

But they received such terrible orders. — Take everything, and leave nothing behind but scorched earth!
DeadInParty
Valerie：让我们举杯为英勇战斗、光荣牺牲的人致敬。
Let us raise a toast to those who fought bravely, and died a glorious death.
Valerie：斯人已逝，我们应将他们铭记心间。让我们举起酒杯。
We should honor the memory of those who are no longer with us. Let us raise our goblets.
Ekun：死人根本不在乎我们说什么祝酒词。
The dead don't care what toasts we make.

I could tell you a few things about this peculiar ritual. But perhaps this time I'd better just join the toast.
Amiri：没错，我们就应该这样纪念倒下的勇士，不应该哭哭啼啼的。如果我有一天战死，你们就痛饮一顿来纪念我，喝他个天昏地暗！
That's right. Here's how we honor the fallen fighters. Not with whining. If I drop dead one day, drink in my honor 'til the mountains shake!
Linzi：他们的功绩绝不会被世人遗忘！我保证！
Their feats will not be forgotten! I'll make sure of that!
Debuffed
Valerie：我很好，只是有点累。让我小睡一会，应该就能恢复了。
I'm perfectly fine, just a bit tired. A little sleep and I'll be back on my feet again.
Valerie：不要那样看着我，我只是稍微有点不舒服。
Don't look at me like that. I'm just a little out of sorts.
Octavia：你需要帮助，瓦尔瑞。别太骄傲了，我们都能看出你很难受！
You need help, Valerie. Stop being so proud — we can all see you're suffering!
Amiri：这样才对！你和那些软蛋南方人不一样，他们打个喷嚏都要跑去看治疗师！
That's the spirit! Not like those southern wussies who run to the healer after every sneeze!
Tristian：对自己好一点吧，瓦尔瑞。有时候，直面自己的脆弱比逞强更重要。
Show yourself some mercy, Valerie. Sometimes it's more important to be honest about your weaknesses than it is to be brave.
Jaethal：嗯，毫无疑问，你性格很要强。
Well, you have a strong character, that's for sure.

Valerie：我们该躺下休息一会了。
It's time to lay down our heads and get some rest.
Valerie：我已经精疲力尽了。
I'm completely exhausted.

It's good we can rest while our friends watch the camp.

Exhaustion is good for meditation. When the body is weak, the soul is more attuned.
Jubilost：我建议你养成睡觉前读书的习惯。还有，看在葛拉利昂诸神的份上，请你选些严肃的读物！
I recommend you to start reading before you go to sleep. And for the sake of all the gods of Golarion, choose some serious reading!

I've no idea how you manage. Wearing that heavy armor all day — and you always walk in front!

You get tired so fast! That's because you longshanks. You spend too much strength to move those big long legs of yours!
InDungeon
Valerie：如果要在这样危险的地方休息，我们应该加倍警惕。
We should be doubly watchful, if we're to rest in so dangerous a place.

It's not the safest place to set camp. Keep your weapons close and your eyes open.

We should choose better places for camp from now on.

A bad place for camp is where pine cones sting your butt when you sit. But a dangerous place surrounded by enemies — that's a good one!

Whoa! Well, if the general herself is worried, then there's sure to be trouble, ha, ha.
LowHealth

During that last fight, I barely parried a hit which might have killed me. I should work more on my defensive techniques.
Valerie：在我有幸结识过的贵族中，很多人认为真正的战斗就和骑马比武差不多。他们在真正的战场上绝对活不过一分钟。
Many of the nobles I've had the pleasure to speak with believe that real fighting is something like jousting. They wouldn't survive a minute in actual battle.

When you're feeling very serious, this tiny wrinkle appears on your forehead… but still you remain unbelievably beautiful!
Harrim：人真是不可理喻！他们为自己的骁勇善战而自豪，却仍然惧怕死亡。他们自愿投身战场，却又乞求治疗，希望我这样的人从那每个勇士都渴望的结局中拯救他们！
People are so absurd! They're proud of their military valor, but still afraid of death. They go to battle voluntarily — and then they beg for healing, hoping that someone like me will save them from the very thing that every warrior seems to crave!
Tristian：生命是无价的恩赐，瓦尔瑞。你应该照顾好自己——即使不是为了你自己，也是为了那些依靠你的人。
Life is a priceless gift, Valerie. You should take care of yourself — if not for yourself, then at least for the sake of those who rely on you.
Linzi：瓦尔瑞，请你一定要小心！你的力量和体力都无人能比，但只要失误一次，就会付出可怕的代价！
Be careful Valerie, please! Your strength and stamina are second to none, but think how much a single mistake might cost!
LowRations

We are running short of supplies. I hope there are people in this party ready to hunt — or ready to go without food until we replenish our stocks.
Valerie：我们的补给所剩无几。我们要么想办法补充食物，要么就只能勒紧腰带了。
Our supplies are running low. Either we find a way to replenish our provisions, or we'll have to tighten our belts.

Oh, I can do without food for a long time! Sometimes no one fed us slaves for several days in a row!

The food will come. By the hunt or by trade.

Magic is such a strange thing… If you don't keep an eye on your spells, the next day you may find yourself without any!

Magic is like armor and weapons: if you don't look after it, it will eventually fail you!

Trust me, it becomes a habit in no time! Preparing your spells will be routine before you know it!

Magic is a bizarre and mysterious thing. It comes to its adepts in such strange and unusual ways…
Tristian：不论魔法还是肉体的力量，甚至是信仰，都需要持之以恒地锻炼。一旦你抛下它们，它们就会退化。世间万物都要遵循这一铁则。
Magic, bodily strength, even faith demand constant dedication. The moment you turn your back on them, they falter. It's true of all that exists in this world.
Linzi：你知道吗，我喜欢睡前回想我最喜欢的歌曲里的句子。这就像是个小小的仪式一样，很能安抚情绪！
You know, before sleep I like to recall verses from my favorite songs. It's like a little ritual, and very soothing!

I never expected you to take up studying the magical arts... But you're making progress!

Sometimes the carelessness of this party is cause for great concern. No one even tried to hide the camp!

Tristian：你说得对，这样不明智。不过，或许这也是莎恩芮的旨意，我们篝火的光辉将指引不幸的灵魂找到救赎……
You're right, it is unwise. But perhaps this is the will of Sarenrae, and the light of our fire shall lead some poor soul to salvation...

Alright, I'll play scribe. We'll lodge your formal complaint properly, and pass it on to the {baroness/baron/queen/king}'s chancery. We do have a chancery in the {barony/kingdom}, don't we?

Bravery and carelessness are two sides of the same coin. Maybe our leader thinks we can handle whatever comes our way!
